\DLine{AB01-PDF0222-body-L041}{}{}{usus es, productum adde temporibus ascensionum rectarum inter utrosque gradus repertis,}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0222-body-L042}{}{}{cum haec tempora minora sint temporibus ascensionum obliquarum; sed ab iis deme, cum}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0222-body-L043}{}{}{maiora sint. Si autem horas distantiae ab ascendente delegisti, productum adde temporibus}
\par\vspace{6pt}\nointerlineskip\hbox to\textwidth{\hss\vrule width 115.43pt height.25pt depth0pt\hss}\prevdepth=-1000pt \vspace{5pt}
\noindent\begin{minipage}[t]{.48\textwidth}\vspace{0pt}\fontsize{9}{11.5}\selectfont\setlength{\GutterOffset}{0pt}\setlength{\FullSourceWidth}{\linewidth}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0222-N01}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0222-notes_left-L001}{}{}{\Indent (1) Cod.: « super Terram et sub Terra ».}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0222-N02}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0222-notes_left-L002}{}{}{\Indent (2) Cap. XIX, pag. 32.}
\end{minipage}
\hfill\vrule width.3pt\hfill\begin{minipage}[t]{.48\textwidth}\vspace{0pt}\fontsize{9}{11.5}\selectfont\setlength{\GutterOffset}{0pt}\setlength{\FullSourceWidth}{\linewidth}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0222-N03}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0222-notes_right-L001}{}{}{\Indent (3) Cod.: « gradus in quo ipsa est ». Totum lo-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0222-notes_right-L002}{}{}{cum in cod. corruptum restitui Platone duce.}
\end{minipage}
\DSource{0223}{134}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0223-body-L001}{}{}{ascensionum obliquarum, si haec minora sunt, aut ab iis deme si maiora. Habebis longinqui-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0223-body-L002}{}{}{tatem alterius gradus ab altero, per ascensiones loci gradus anterioris quem stella occupat}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0223-body-L003}{}{}{in hemisphaerio orientali.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0223-P01}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0223-body-L004}{}{}{\Indent Si autem gradus anterior datus reperitur cum gradu sequenti in hemisphaerio occidentali,}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0223-body-L005}{5}{}{sume tempora ascensionum rectarum inter utrosque posita, nec non tempora ascensionum illius}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0223-body-L006}{}{}{climatis inter duos gradus oppositos intercedentia, quae sunt tempora occasuum climatis quibus}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0223-body-L007}{}{}{duo gradus dati inter se differunt. Sextam partem differentiae inter utrosque numeros inventos}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0223-body-L008}{}{}{multiplica in horas distantiae gradus a paxillo Terrae, vel occidentis, vel medii Caeli, prout}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0223-body-L009}{}{}{mavis; productum adde temporibus ascensionum aut occasuum e paxillo delecto provenienti-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0223-body-L010}{10}{}{bus, si haec minora sunt, sed de temporibus deme si haec maiora exstant, ut supra docuimus.}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0223-body-L011}{}{}{Idest, si paxillum occidentis delegisti, productum adde occasibus inter utrosque gradus in cli-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0223-body-L012}{}{}{mate intercedentibus, si haec sunt ascensionibus rectis minora; at de iis deme, si maiora; —}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0223-body-L013}{}{}{cum autem paxillo Terrae vel medii Caeli usus sis, productum adde temporibus ascensionum}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0223-body-L014}{}{}{rectarum, si haec minora sunt, aut ab iis deme, si maiora. Habebis distantiam inter utrosque}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0223-body-L015}{15}{}{gradus expressa occasibus loci sphaerae quem gradus anterior tenebat.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0223-P02}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0223-body-L016}{}{}{\Indent Si duo gradus illi in duobus diversis hemisphaeriis sunt, ut docui cognosce distantiam}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0223-body-L017}{}{p. 202.}{gradus anterioris a medio Caelo, si est in hemisphaerio occidentali, aut a paxillo Terrae, si}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0223-body-L018}{}{}{in orientali hemisphaerio consistit; ei quod inveneris adde ascensiones rectas inter gradum}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0223-body-L019}{}{}{sequentem et medium Caelum aut paxillum Terrae; summa est longinquitas quaesita inter}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0223-body-L020}{20}{}{duos gradus illos.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0223-P03}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0223-body-L021}{}{}{\Indent Haec aliter quoque scire potes. Ut iam docui, horas quibus gradus anterior a paxillo}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0223-body-L022}{}{}{distat cognosce; postea, si gradus anterior cum sequenti inter medium Caelum et ascendens}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0223-body-L023}{}{}{sit, aut anterior illic, sequens autem inter ascendens et paxillum Terrae consistat, idest si}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0223-body-L024}{}{}{ambo in hemisphaerio orientali sint, tempora horaria diurna gradus sequentis, quae sunt tempora}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0223-body-L025}{25}{}{horaria ipsius gradus, multiplica in horas distantiae gradus anterioris a medio Caelo; et produ-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0223-body-L026}{}{}{ctum deme de temporibus ascensionum rectarum inter medium Caelum et gradum sequentem.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0223-P04}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0223-body-L027}{}{}{\Indent Si ambo gradus in hemisphaerio occidentali sunt, quod a paxillo Terrae ad medium Cae-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0223-body-L028}{}{}{lum in occasum versus protenditur, horas distantiae gradus anterioris a paxillo Terrae mul-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0223-body-L029}{}{}{tiplica [in tempora horaria nocturna gradus sequentis]; et productum deme de temporibus}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0223-body-L030}{30}{}{ascensionum rectarum inter paxillum Terrae et gradum sequentem.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0223-P05}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0223-body-L031}{}{}{\Indent Si gradus in duobus diversis hemisphaeriis sunt, cum anterior inter ascendens et paxil-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0223-body-L032}{}{}{lum Terrae reperiatur, sequens vero inter paxillum Terrae et occidentis, tempora horaria}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0223-body-L033}{}{}{nocturna gradus sequentis, quae per eius nadir cognoscuntur, multiplica in horas distantiae}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0223-body-L034}{}{}{gradus anterioris a paxillo ascendentis; et productum de temporibus ascensionum climatis inter}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0223-body-L035}{35}{}{ascendens et gradum sequentem deme.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0223-P06}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0223-body-L036}{}{}{\Indent Cum demum, exstantibus gradibus in duobus diversis hemisphaeriis, anterior inter occi-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0223-body-L037}{}{}{dentem et medium Caelum, sequens inter medium Caelum et ascendens sit, tempora horaria}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0223-body-L038}{}{}{diurna gradus sequentis multiplica in horas quibus gradus anterior a paxillo occidentis distat;}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0223-body-L039}{}{}{productum de temporibus ascensionum obliquarum inter nadir gradus occidentis et nadir gra-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0223-body-L040}{40}{}{dus sequentis deme. Remanet distantia alterius gradus dati ab altero, expressa temporibus}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0223-body-L041}{}{}{ascensionum aut occasuum primi gradus. — Similiter, inversa via, ab ascensionibus loci gradus}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0223-body-L042}{}{}{sequentis cognosci potest abhinc quantum aequatoris tempus gradus anterior a loco gradus}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0223-body-L043}{}{}{sequentis in ecliptica discesserit.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0223-P07}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0223-body-L044}{}{p. 203.}{\Indent Has quantitates praesertim cognoscere oportet in nativitatibus, ut significatores dirigantur}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0223-body-L045}{45}{}{ad sua loca; et hoc est quod Ptolemaeus in Tetrabiblo memorat, quo libro res futuras per}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0223-body-L046}{}{}{cognitionem stellarum vaticinari docet. Hoc modo hilegia, ad vitas posita, diriguntur (\textbf{\textit{b}}).}
\DSource{0224}{135}
\par\fontsize{14.5}{17}\selectfont
\par\vspace*{\dimexpr 6.02mm-\baselineskip\relax}\prevdepth=0pt
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0224-H01}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0224-body-L001}{}{}{\CenterLine{CAPUT LVI.}}
\par\vspace{2.99mm}
\par\fontsize{14.2}{16}\selectfont
\DLine{AB01-PDF0224-body-L002}{}{}{De conficiendo sive strato sive erecto instrumento, \textit{ar-rukhāmah} “ mar-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0224-body-L003}{5}{}{\mbox{}\rlap{\hspace*{7.55mm}more „ appellato, quo horae temporales diei quolibet loco cogno-}}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0224-body-L004}{}{}{\mbox{}\rlap{\hspace*{7.55mm}scuntur.}}
\par\fontsize{11.1}{13.8}\selectfont
\par\vspace{3.06mm}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0224-P01}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0224-body-L005}{}{}{\Indent Dicit [auctor]: Si horas temporales diei ab ortu ad occasum Solis e superficie umbrae}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0224-body-L006}{}{}{solaris in instrumento [ad terram] strato cognoscere vis, tabulam marmoream aut cupream,}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0224-body-L007}{10}{}{aequalis et laevigatae superficiei, cuiuslibet magnitudinis sume; optima proportio habetur cum}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0224-body-L008}{}{}{latitudo sit $\qfrac{2}{3}$ longitudinis. In linea dimidiae longitudinis, ad $\qfrac{2}{3}$ latitudinis, punctum nota, et}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0224-body-L009}{}{}{circa id quavis semidiametro circulum describe; circulum autem duabus lineis rectis se in centro}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0224-body-L010}{}{}{ad angulos rectos mutuo secantibus in quatuor partes aequales divide; et in singulis quadran-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0224-body-L011}{}{}{tibus 90 gradus signa cum intervallo unius gradus vel amplius, prout magnitudo vel angustia}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0224-body-L012}{15}{}{circuli concedit. Postea umbram initii Cancri et initii Capricorni ad unam, duas, tres, qua-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0224-body-L013}{}{}{tuor, quinque et sex horas temporarias, nec non directionem umbrae cuiusque harum horarum}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0224-body-L014}{}{}{in circulo horizontis cognosce, iis modis quos in anteriore parte libri, in capite de cognitione}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0224-body-L015}{}{}{azimuth umbrae et altitudinis ad singulos gradus eclipticae quocumque loco [cap. XI], tibi}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0224-body-L016}{}{}{ostendimus. Idest altitudines omnium horarum cognosce, et ex iis umbras atque azimuth modo}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0224-body-L017}{20}{}{memorato deprehende (1); postea regulam rectam sume, cuius superficies in partes aequales}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0224-body-L018}{}{}{divisa sit ita ut partes superent aut saltem aequent numerum umbrae initii Capricorni. Pun-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0224-body-L019}{}{}{ctum a quo divisio regulae incipit in centro circuli pone, et latus regulae in directione um-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0224-body-L020}{}{}{brae unius horae Capricorni, versus ampliorem marmoris partem; gradus azimuth in circum-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0224-body-L021}{}{p. 204}{ferentia a puncto orientis incipiens numera, et gradus regulae a centro iuxta longitudinem}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0224-body-L022}{25}{}{umbrae unius horae; quo pervenies, ibi punctum nota, quod erit signum umbrae primae ho-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0224-body-L023}{}{}{rae. Similiter signa umbrarum et azimuth ad reliquas quinque horas pone; et umbrae locum}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0224-body-L024}{}{}{in marmore nota in linea septentrionum et austri ad partem ampliorem tabulae versus, sci-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0224-body-L025}{}{}{licet in linea meridiana. Quo facto, regulam ad alterum quadrantem prope lineam meri-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0224-body-L026}{}{}{dianam verte; in eo ut nuper fecisti operare, et hoc modo umbras quinque horarum initii}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0224-body-L027}{30}{}{Capricorni signaveris ab utraque parte lineae meridianae, nempe ad ortum et occasum versus,}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0224-body-L028}{}{}{in portione ampliore tabulae.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0224-P02}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0224-body-L029}{}{}{\Indent Eodem modo directiones horarum Cancri, in angustiore parte tabulae, ab utroque lineae}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0224-body-L030}{}{}{meridianae latere signa, ita ut finis sextae horae in lineam meridianam incidat. Manifeste}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0224-body-L031}{}{}{directio erit in parte angustiore tabulae, respectu lineae ortus et occasus, cum fuerit borealis;}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0224-body-L032}{35}{}{at in parte ampliore, cum fuerit australis. Puncta horarum Cancri cum punctis respondentibus}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0224-body-L033}{}{}{horarum Capricorni coniunge lineis rectis, scilicet primam horam Cancri cum prima Capri-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0224-body-L034}{}{}{corni, secundam cum secunda etc. Item puncta horarum Cancri cum alteris Cancri, et puncta}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0224-body-L035}{}{}{horarum Capricorni cum aliis Capricorni coniunge lineis in tabula signatis, quae, ab utraque}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0224-body-L036}{}{}{parte, a puncto primae horae ad horam sextam, in linea meridiana signatam, perveniant, et}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0224-body-L037}{40}{}{definiant locum marmoris quam umbra nunquam praetergreditur.}
\par\vspace{6pt}\nointerlineskip\hbox to\textwidth{\hss\vrule width 115.43pt height.25pt depth0pt\hss}\prevdepth=-1000pt \vspace{5pt}
\noindent\begin{minipage}[t]{.48\textwidth}\vspace{0pt}\fontsize{9}{11.5}\selectfont\setlength{\GutterOffset}{0pt}\setlength{\FullSourceWidth}{\linewidth}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0224-N01}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0224-notes_left-L001}{}{}{\Indent (1) Longitudines et azimuth harum umbrarum}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0224-notes_left-L002}{}{}{ad latitudinem urbis ar-Raqqah iam supputata, exhi-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0224-notes_left-L003}{}{}{bentur in tabula fol. 240,r. — In fine capitis Plato}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0224-notes_left-L004}{}{}{habet: « Tabulam etiam ad zenith [i. e. azimuth]}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0224-notes_left-L005}{}{}{« horarum Capricorni et Cancri, et ad earum um-}
\end{minipage}
\hfill\vrule width.3pt\hfill\begin{minipage}[t]{.48\textwidth}\vspace{0pt}\fontsize{9}{11.5}\selectfont\setlength{\GutterOffset}{0pt}\setlength{\FullSourceWidth}{\linewidth}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0224-notes_right-L001}{}{}{« bras in latitudine triginta sex graduum facimus »;}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0224-notes_right-L002}{}{}{sequuntur duae tabellae ad latitudinem 36° et ad}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0224-notes_right-L003}{}{}{latitudinem 38°, quae ab aliquo Platonis lectore sup-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0224-notes_right-L004}{}{}{putata esse videntur, et cum tabula al-Battānī mi-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0224-notes_right-L005}{}{}{nime conveniunt.}
\end{minipage}
\DSource{0225}{136}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0225-P01}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0225-body-L001}{}{}{\Indent In centro circuli stilum cupreum vel ferreum, rotundum, tornatum et acuminatum fige;}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0225-body-L002}{}{}{quod eius super tabulae superficiem prominet, complectatur duodecim e partibus regulae qua}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0225-body-L003}{}{}{dimensiones umbrarum sumpsisti. Hunc stilum versus omnes circuli partes diligentissime cir-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0225-body-L004}{}{}{cino metire, ut eum scias veraciter in centro ad perpendiculum esse; foramen usque ad alte-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0225-body-L005}{5}{}{ram tabulae superficiem protende, ut extremus stilus in foramen penetrans firmiter sit infixus,}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0225-body-L006}{}{}{nec moveri aut declinare possit. Ampliorem partem tabulae ad septentriones, angustiorem ad}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0225-body-L007}{}{p. 205.}{austrum versus pone; ita, in linea meridiana, punctum boreale in loco ampliore, punctum}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0225-body-L008}{}{}{australe in angustiore erit, et loca ortus atque occasus incident in lineam quae lineam meri-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0225-body-L009}{}{}{dianam ad angulos rectos secat. Ab occasu incipiens, in ampliore parte tabulae, numeros hora-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0225-body-L010}{10}{}{rum sub punctis horarum Capricorni usque ad finem undecimae horae scribe; nam e sciothe-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0225-body-L011}{}{}{rico horae tantum diei ab 1 ad 11 deprehendi possunt, ob nimiam umbrae extensionem in}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0225-body-L012}{}{}{duabus diei extremitatibus, quae instrumento capi nequit et sciothericum maius requirit. Si}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0225-body-L013}{}{}{vis dimidias quoque horarum partes, et tertias et amplius, signa azimuth et umbram cuiusvis}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0225-body-L014}{}{}{partis horarum; id enim difficile non est.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0225-P02}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0225-body-L015}{15}{}{\Indent Sciotherico perfecto, locum qui post primam diei horam usque ad finem undecimae in}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0225-body-L016}{}{}{horizonte pateat, delige; ibi circulum describe, cuius lineam meridianam, ut in anteriore parte}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0225-body-L017}{}{}{libri docuimus [cap. XII], inveni. Lineam meridianam sciotherici super lineam meridianam}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0225-body-L018}{}{}{circuli diligentissime pone, ut ab ea nullo modo declinet; directio australis partis angustioris}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0225-body-L019}{}{}{sciotherici austrum prospiciat, atque directio borealis in parte ampliore versus septentriones}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0225-body-L020}{20}{}{sit. Superficiem superiorem tabulae parallelam ad planitiem horizontis perpendiculo constitue,}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0225-body-L021}{}{}{ita ut nusquam declinet; hoc modo, a casu extremae umbrae stili in lineas horarum poteris}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0225-body-L022}{}{}{horas temporales diei cognoscere quolibet loco, cuius latitudo sit aequalis latitudini ad quam}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0225-body-L023}{}{}{sciothericum confecisti.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0225-P03}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0225-body-L024}{}{}{\Indent Alio quoque modo sciothericum collocari potest. Altitudinem cuius azimuth declinatione}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0225-body-L025}{25}{}{caret (1), ut in anteriore parte libri [cap. XII] docui, cognosce; postea altitudinem observa}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0225-body-L026}{}{}{eo usque quo aequalis altitudini illi fiat; sciothericum tunc verte ita ut umbra stili in lineam}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0225-body-L027}{}{}{ortus et occasus incidat; quod cum fuerit, sciothericum, cuius superficies parallela ad hori-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0225-body-L028}{}{}{zontem sit, optime suo loco aptatum erit. — Aut, si mavis, calculo altitudinem unius horae,}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0225-body-L029}{}{}{vel duarum, vel trium supputa; postea umbram observa eo usque quo altitudinem illam atti-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0225-body-L030}{30}{}{gerit; sciothericum tunc verte usque quo umbra stili incidat in lineam horae cuius altitudi-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0225-body-L031}{}{}{nem cognoveras. — Fieri etiam potest ut azimuth illius altitudinis datae iam cognoscas; altitu-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0225-body-L032}{}{}{dinem igitur observa, et quando aequaverit altitudinem cuius azimuth cognoveras, sciothericum}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0225-body-L033}{}{p. 206.}{ita verte ut umbra stili in directionem illius altitudinis in circulo descripto incidat. Si umbra}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0225-body-L034}{}{}{circumferentiam non attingit, basi stili tenuissimum filum liga, ipsumque tende ad directionis}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0225-body-L035}{35}{}{locum tempore observationis; sciothericum postea verte usque quo centrum umbrae stili in}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0225-body-L036}{}{}{filum incidat; sciothericum tunc bene collocatum erit, et linea horae sextae, si Deus vult, ex}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0225-body-L037}{}{}{adverso lineae meridianae remanebit.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0225-P04}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0225-body-L038}{}{}{\Indent Si directionem urbis Mekkah, idest azimuth qiblae (2) ad orandum, ita cognoscere vis ut}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0225-body-L039}{}{}{e centro circuli lineam ducas quae in urbe tua directionem qiblae indicet, latitudinem tui loci}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0225-body-L040}{40}{}{et latitudinem Mekkah deprehende, atque utrum Mekkah borealis an australis sit respectu}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0225-body-L041}{}{}{tui loci vide. Postea longitudines amborum locorum cognosce; minorem de maiore deme ut}
\par\vspace{6pt}\nointerlineskip\hbox to\textwidth{\hss\vrule width 115.43pt height.25pt depth0pt\hss}\prevdepth=-1000pt \vspace{5pt}
\noindent\begin{minipage}[t]{.48\textwidth}\vspace{0pt}\fontsize{9}{11.5}\selectfont\setlength{\GutterOffset}{0pt}\setlength{\FullSourceWidth}{\linewidth}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0225-N01}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0225-notes_left-L001}{}{}{\Indent (1) Est altitudo in primo circulo verticali, per}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0225-notes_left-L002}{}{}{lineam E-W transeunti; eius azimuth tunc 0° est,}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0225-notes_left-L003}{}{}{nam a loco a quo omnia azimuth numerantur non}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0225-notes_left-L004}{}{}{declinat. — \Name{Delambre}, p. 57, perperam explicat}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0225-notes_left-L005}{}{}{« c'est-à-dire sans doute l'ombre méridienne ».}
\end{minipage}
\hfill\vrule width.3pt\hfill\begin{minipage}[t]{.48\textwidth}\vspace{0pt}\fontsize{9}{11.5}\selectfont\setlength{\GutterOffset}{0pt}\setlength{\FullSourceWidth}{\linewidth}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0225-N02}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0225-notes_right-L001}{}{}{\Indent (2) \textit{Qiblah} est « tractus horizontis prae oculis}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0225-notes_right-L002}{}{}{« stans », inde « locus horizontis in cuius directione}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0225-notes_right-L003}{}{}{« est templum Mekkanum »; nam in hunc locum}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0225-notes_right-L004}{}{}{versus Muslimi orantes vultus convertere debent}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0225-notes_right-L005}{}{}{(\textit{Qoran.} II, 139-145).}
\end{minipage}
\DSource{0226}{137}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0226-body-L001}{}{}{quanto differant et utrum ab ortu an occasu tui loci sit Mekkah scias; nam si longitudo Mek-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0226-body-L002}{}{}{kah est maior longitudine tui loci in tabulis descripta, Mekkah orientalis esse debet, si minor,}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0226-body-L003}{}{}{occidentalis. His cognitis, a linea orientis incipiens in partem versus latitudinis Mekkah, et}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0226-body-L004}{}{}{similiter a linea occidentis ad eandem partem in circumferentia progrediens, latus regulae pone}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0226-body-L005}{5}{}{super numerum qui differentiam duarum latitudinum exprimit, et a puncto occidentali ad orien-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0226-body-L006}{}{}{tale, quae ita reperisti, lineam rectam duc. Quo facto, differentiam longitudinum numera in}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0226-body-L007}{}{}{circumferentia a linea meridiana in boream et in austrum versus, ab ea parte a qua est}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0226-body-L008}{}{}{longitudo Mekkah respectu tui loci; duo puncta ita reperta linea recta coniunge. Locus inter-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0226-body-L009}{}{}{sectionis duarum linearum est locus directionis Mekkah respectu urbis tuae; a centro igitur}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0226-body-L010}{10}{}{circuli ad locum intersectionis lineam rectam duc et usque ad circumferentiam protrahe; haec}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0226-body-L011}{}{}{linea definiet azimuth qiblae ea in urbe.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0226-P01}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0226-body-L012}{}{}{\Indent Si azimuth qiblae calculo scire vis, sinum quadratum differentiae longitudinum adde sinui}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0226-body-L013}{}{}{quadrato differentiae latitudinum; radix summae est hypotenusa trianguli rectanguli, scilicet}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0226-body-L014}{}{}{distantia centri circuli a loco intersectionis linearum longitudinis et latitudinis in circumferentia.}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0226-body-L015}{15}{}{Sinum differentiae latitudinum in semidiametrum multiplica; productum per hypotenusam illam}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0226-body-L016}{}{}{divide; quod exibit in arcum converte, et arcus erit azimuth Mekkah. Hanc quantitatem in}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0226-body-L017}{}{p. 207.}{circumferentia numera, a loco ortus aut occasus, iuxta locum longitudinis Mekkah respectu}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0226-body-L018}{}{}{tuae urbis, in partem latitudinis Mekkah procedens; punctum circumferentiae quo perveneris,}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0226-body-L019}{}{}{cum centro circuli coniunge; haec linea igitur definiet azimuth Mekkah ea in urbe (\textbf{\textit{a}}).}
\par\vspace{0.65mm}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0226-H20}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0226-body-L020}{20}{}{\CenterLine{\textbf{Haec est sciotherici imago (}\textbf{\textit{b}}\textbf{).}}}
\par\vspace{2.30mm}
\par\noindent\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0226-body-F01}{}\CenterLine{\input{figures/AB01-PDF0226-F01}}\par
\par\vspace{-0.10mm}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0226-H21}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0226-body-L021}{}{}{\CenterLine{Ad similitudinem divisionis horum duorum quadrantum duos quadrantes reliquos, Deo volente, divides.}}
\par\vspace{5mm}\hfill{\fontsize{8}{10}\selectfont 18}\hspace{10mm}
\DSource{0227}{138}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0227-P01}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0227-body-L001}{}{}{\Indent Dicit [auctor]: Amplam tabulam marmoream, quadrilateram et oblongam ABCD sume;}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0227-body-L002}{}{}{ad $\qfrac{2}{3}$ eius latitudinis, et in longitudinis medio loco pone centrum E, circa quod circulum}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0227-body-L003}{}{}{describe; postea circulum duabus lineis, se ad angulos rectos in centro mutuo secantibus, et}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0227-body-L004}{}{}{usque ad latera tabulae productis, in quatuor quadrantes divide. Linea longior, ad longitudi-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0227-body-L005}{5}{p. 208.}{nem tabulae ducta, erit linea ortus et occasus; brevior, ad latitudinem tabulae descripta, li-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0227-body-L006}{}{}{neam meridianam indicabit. Prope extremas lineas cardines horizontis nota. In circumferentia}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0227-body-L007}{}{}{a punctis ortus et occasus lineae longioris incipe azimuthorum supputationem; azimuth australia}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0227-body-L008}{}{}{ad boream versus numera, borealia in austrum versus, postquam singulos quadrantes in 90 gra-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0227-body-L009}{}{}{dus dividisti nigro vel rubro atramento, ut divisionis vestigia deleri possint. Et circulum simi-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0227-body-L010}{10}{}{liter. Sed ambas circuli diametros, quae sunt duae lineae memoratae, marmori incide, ut earum}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0227-body-L011}{}{}{vestigia remaneant. Supra omnes directiones horarum Cancri litteram M scribe, supra dire-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0227-body-L012}{}{}{ctiones horarum Capricorni litteram L; numeros horarum locis in quae umbrae incidunt nota,}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0227-body-L013}{}{}{ab occasu incipiens; demum in longitudine et latitudine tabulae duc lineas inter puncta hora-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0227-body-L014}{}{}{rum et umbrarum. Urbem Mekkah in tractu orientali et australi pone; arcum latitudinis inter}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0227-body-L015}{15}{}{utrasque urbes contentum MK appella; eandem quantitatem in tractu occidentali sume, et per}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0227-body-L016}{}{}{utrumque punctum K (1) lineam parallelam ad lineam ortus et occasus describe. [Deinde sume}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0227-body-L017}{}{}{arcum longitudinis qui inter duas urbes intercedit, eumque a binis punctis meridiei ad orientem}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0227-body-L018}{}{}{versus signa, et NH appella; per duo puncta N duc lineam lineae meridianae parallelam;] (2)}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0227-body-L019}{}{}{locum intersectionis duarum linearum T [appella]. Linea ETP est directio Mekkah. Sit autem}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0227-body-L020}{20}{}{ES longitudo stili, quem pones erectum in centro E. Haec explicanda erant. — Tabulas etiam}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0227-body-L021}{}{}{confecimus, in quibus directiones, umbras et altitudines horarum Capricorni et Cancri ad lati-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0227-body-L022}{}{}{tudinem 36° 0′ describuntur (3).}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0227-P02}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0227-body-L023}{}{}{\Indent Sciothericum erectum, cuius erecta superficies meridiem prospicit, eodem modo quoad}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0227-body-L024}{}{}{directiones construitur; nam umbrae tantum variant, ut in capite de cognitione umbrae ere-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0227-body-L025}{25}{}{ctae (4) diximus. Sciotherico igitur ad quantitates umbrae stantis confecto, et eius superficie}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0227-body-L026}{}{}{erecta in linea ortus et occasus posita, necessarie superficies tabulae meridiem prospiciet,}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0227-body-L027}{}{}{se ab ortu ad occasum protendens; pars amplior versus terram, angustior versus caelum erit.}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0227-body-L028}{}{}{Notum est in hoc sciotherico umbram omnium longissimam in initio Cancri haberi, brevissi-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0227-body-L029}{}{}{mam in initio Capricorni. Stilus complectatur 12 e partibus regulae qua umbram metitus es;}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0227-body-L030}{30}{}{ita poteris e loco incidentiae extremitatis umbrae super lineas horarum cognoscere horas tem-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0227-body-L031}{}{}{porales transactas diei. — Plurimis modis variisque instrumentis horae deprehenduntur; sed}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0227-body-L032}{}{}{haec duo instrumenta, si Deus vult, omnium quibus usus sum certissima et commodissima sunt.}
\par\fontsize{14.5}{17}\selectfont
\par\vspace{5.33mm}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0227-H33}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0227-body-L033}{}{p. 209.}{\CenterLine{CAPUT LVII.}}
\par\vspace{-2.86mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0227-body-gap035}{35}{}{}
\par\vspace{-0.19mm}
\par\fontsize{14.2}{16}\selectfont
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0227-H34}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0227-body-L034}{}{}{\CenterLine{Libri conclusio: de constructione globi caelestis, quadrantis muralis}}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0227-H35}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0227-body-L035}{}{}{\CenterLine{et triquetri, quae ad perficiendas observationes inserviunt (5).}}
\par\fontsize{11.1}{13.8}\selectfont
\par\vspace{2.95mm}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0227-P03}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0227-body-L036}{}{}{\Indent Dicit [auctor]: Causae rerum, et fundamenta calculi geometricis rationibus probata, quae}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0227-body-L037}{40}{}{in libro nostro memoravimus et descripsimus, semper manent immutata, nec eorum status}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0227-body-L038}{}{}{unquam in dubium vocari potest; sed in iis rebus metiendis et observandis possibile est aliquid}
\par\vspace{6pt}\nointerlineskip\hbox to\textwidth{\hss\vrule width 115.43pt height.25pt depth0pt\hss}\prevdepth=-1000pt \vspace{5pt}
\noindent\begin{minipage}[t]{.48\textwidth}\vspace{0pt}\fontsize{9}{11.5}\selectfont\setlength{\GutterOffset}{0pt}\setlength{\FullSourceWidth}{\linewidth}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0227-N01}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0227-notes_left-L001}{}{}{\Indent (1) Cod. K, M.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0227-N02}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0227-notes_left-L002}{}{}{\Indent (2) Pro his verbis quae uncis inclusi, codex et}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0227-notes_left-L003}{}{}{Plato tantum habent: « super arcum qui inter eas}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0227-notes_left-L004}{}{}{« est NH signa, et super .... ».}
\end{minipage}
\hfill\vrule width.3pt\hfill\begin{minipage}[t]{.48\textwidth}\vspace{0pt}\fontsize{9}{11.5}\selectfont\setlength{\GutterOffset}{0pt}\setlength{\FullSourceWidth}{\linewidth}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0227-N03}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0227-notes_right-L001}{}{}{\Indent (3) Fol. 240,r. — Cfr. pag. 135, adn. 1.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0227-N04}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0227-notes_right-L002}{}{}{\Indent (4) Cap. X, p. 22-23.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0227-N05}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0227-notes_right-L003}{}{}{\Indent (5) Platonis versio, ut typis impressa et in co-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0227-notes_right-L004}{}{}{dice Parisiensi 7266 exstat, omnia omittit quae de-}
\end{minipage}
\DSource{0228}{139}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0228-body-L001}{}{}{inveniri, quod maius aut minus vero sit. Si haec quantitas, sensibili modo veram excedens}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0228-body-L002}{}{}{vel ab ea decrescens, dividatur per longum temporis spatium, error fiet minor; at per tempus}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0228-body-L003}{}{}{breve, error, licet parvus sit, crescet (1). Si error e divisione et collocatione instrumenti oritur,}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0228-body-L004}{}{}{eodem instrumento eodem modo posito rursus observans, iterum eundem errorem invenies;}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0228-body-L005}{5}{}{si autem e divisione tantum provenit, collocatio instrumenti mutata potest optima esse, at di-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0228-body-L006}{}{}{visionis error ut antea manere, qua re, altero instrumento adhibito, differentia appareat.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0228-P01}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0228-body-L007}{}{}{\Indent Fit quoque interdum ut progressu temporis instrumentum in amplitudine, iuncturis, cur-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0228-body-L008}{}{}{vaturis et similibus aliquid variationis accipiat; error tunc maior vel minor erit, prout maiores}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0228-body-L009}{}{}{vel minores fuerint perturbationes illae (2). Post hoc, si quis optima et indubia dimensione}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0228-body-L010}{10}{}{[caelestia] metiatur, in intervallo inter utrasque observationes nonnisi primum errorem faciet,}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0228-body-L011}{}{}{si ut antea operatus erit. Res igitur quae hac via deprehenduntur sunt verae, cum earum}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0228-body-L012}{}{}{dimensio fiat duobus optimis et bene aptatis instrumentis, aut uno optimo instrumento quod}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0228-body-L013}{}{}{a pristino statu nequaquam mutatum sit. Licet infirma hominum natura veritatem rerum ita}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0228-body-L014}{}{}{non attingat ut virtualiter potest, error tamen parvus ac insensibilis fit si diligentia et circum-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0228-body-L015}{15}{}{spectio praesertim in longis temporis spatiis adhibeantur; opem auxiliumque ferunt bona indoles,}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0228-body-L016}{}{}{sedulus animus, acuti oculi, attenta mens, maxima constantia in rebus, etsi haec omnia attingere}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0228-body-L017}{}{}{difficile est. Id contra impediunt parva patientia, cupido gloriae, amor gratiarum regum; tunc}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0228-body-L018}{}{p. 210.}{enim homo ea vult statim consequi quae brevi tempore cognoscere impossibile est, aut ea quae,}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0228-body-L019}{}{}{ob earum naturam ipsam, nulli homini addiscere unquam datum erit.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0228-P02}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0228-body-L020}{20}{}{\Indent Quoniam ad hunc nostri libri locum pervenimus, explicabimus, Deo volente, instrumentum}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0228-body-L021}{}{}{ad similitudinem sphaerae celestis confectum, et \textit{bayḍah} « ovum » appellatum, nec non duo}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0228-body-L022}{}{}{alia instrumenta ad observationes faciendas.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0228-P03}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0228-body-L023}{}{}{\Indent \textbf{Constructio instrumenti ad similitudinem sphaerae caelestis, in quo stellae}}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0228-body-L024}{}{}{\textbf{aetheris signantur; ipsum \Name{bayḍah} « ovum » appellatur.} — Dicit [auctor]: Globum}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0228-body-L025}{25}{}{cupreum bene rotundum, expolitae superficiei, tornatum et cuiusvis magnitudinis sume; in eo}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0228-body-L026}{}{}{duos polos, per diametrum oppositos, incide, inter quos superficiem globi in duo hemisphaeria}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0228-body-L027}{}{}{divide, et lineam quae hemisphaeria separat in quatuor partes; singulos quadrantes puncto}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0228-body-L028}{}{}{nota. Punctorum quodvis centrum pone, ut circulum primo aequalem describas, qui per pri-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0228-body-L029}{}{}{mos polos globi transeat et primum circulum in duas dimidias partes dividat; unum e qua-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0228-body-L030}{30}{}{drantibus primi circuli in 90 gradus divide, et ex eo quantitatem obliquitatis eclipticae, idest}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0228-body-L031}{}{}{23° 35′, sume. Deinde hos gradus e gradibus quadrantis circino accipe; et alteram extre-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0228-body-L032}{}{}{mitatem in alterutro polo ponens, alteram extremitatem verte ad secundum circulum, cuius}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0228-body-L033}{}{}{polus est quoddam punctorum; loco intersectionis punctum signa. Idem fac in altero polo,}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0228-body-L034}{}{}{sed in partem contrariam procedens, ut duorum punctorum alterum alteri de diametro oppo-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0228-body-L035}{35}{}{natur. Horum quodvis sume tanquam polum, circa quem, per medium inter haec puncta}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0228-body-L036}{}{}{locum, circulum describe; provenient duae circumferentiae, se in duobus oppositis punctis}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0228-body-L037}{}{}{mutuo secantes, quarum altera aequatorem, altera eclipticam effinget. Manifeste polus ecli-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0228-body-L038}{}{}{pticae sub aequatoris polo in septentriones versus consistit; circulus qui per polos [omnes]}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0228-body-L039}{}{}{transit erit circulus Cancri et Capricorni; punctum eclipticae supra aequatorem erit initium}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0228-body-L040}{40}{}{Cancri, sub aequatore initium Capricorni; denique duorum punctorum, in quibus ecliptica}
\par\vspace{6pt}\nointerlineskip\hbox to\textwidth{\hss\vrule width 115.43pt height.25pt depth0pt\hss}\prevdepth=-1000pt \vspace{5pt}
\noindent\begin{minipage}[t]{.48\textwidth}\vspace{0pt}\fontsize{9}{11.5}\selectfont\setlength{\GutterOffset}{0pt}\setlength{\FullSourceWidth}{\linewidth}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0228-notes_left-L001}{}{}{scriptionem quadrantis (p. 142) praecedunt; titulus}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0228-notes_left-L002}{}{}{capitis est: « Cap. LVII et ultimum huius libri, De}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0228-notes_left-L003}{}{}{« compositione duorum instrumentorum, videlicet}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0228-notes_left-L004}{}{}{« quadrati, et alhidadae sive regularum etc. pro ob-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0228-notes_left-L005}{}{}{« servationibus etc. utilium ». Sed Platonis codex}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0228-notes_left-L006}{}{}{Mediolanensis, ubi hoc caput alia eademque pessima}
\end{minipage}
\hfill\vrule width.3pt\hfill\begin{minipage}[t]{.48\textwidth}\vspace{0pt}\fontsize{9}{11.5}\selectfont\setlength{\GutterOffset}{0pt}\setlength{\FullSourceWidth}{\linewidth}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0228-notes_right-L001}{}{}{ac vix legibili manu exaratum est, considerationes}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0228-notes_right-L002}{}{}{generales et descriptionem globi caelestis praebet,}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0228-notes_right-L003}{}{}{sed multis, ut videtur, lacunis; capitis titulus est}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0228-notes_right-L004}{}{}{« In perfectione libri ».}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0228-N01}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0228-notes_right-L005}{}{}{\Indent (1) Cfr. \textit{Almag.} III, 2 (ed. Halma, t. I, p. 159).}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0228-N02}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0228-notes_right-L006}{}{}{\Indent (2) Cfr. \textit{Almag.} l. l. (ed. Halma, t. I, p. 155).}
\end{minipage}
\DSource{0229}{140}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0229-body-L001}{}{}{et aequator mutuo se secant, alterum initium Arietis, alterum initium Librae. Signa zodiaci}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0229-body-L002}{}{}{ad eorum successionis ordinem nota, singuli quadranti terna signa eiusdem magnitudinis tri-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0229-body-L003}{}{}{buens; singula signa senas areolas, singulae areolae quinos gradus complectantur, qua re}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0229-body-L004}{}{}{signum in 30 gradus divisum appareat, quorum numeri litteris alphabeti (1) in areolis signen-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0229-body-L005}{5}{}{tur. Postea comple divisionem circuli aequinoctialis in 360 gradus, in 72 areolas distributos;}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0229-body-L006}{}{}{in quaque areola summam graduum scribe usque ad finem 360, a puncto initii Arietis nume-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0229-body-L007}{}{p. 211.}{rationem incipiens, ut haec apud idem punctum desinat, quod est finis duodecim signorum.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0229-P01}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0229-body-L008}{}{}{\Indent Loca stellarum fixarum, ad constellationes pertinentium, omnia vel partim, ut mavis,}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0229-body-L009}{}{}{describe eo modo quem nunc docebo. Quantitatem latitudinis stellae circino sume ex aequatore;}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0229-body-L010}{10}{}{extremitatum alteram in gradu [eclipticae] quem stella occupat pone, alteram versus partem}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0229-body-L011}{}{}{latitudinis, et lineam levem describe, cuius vestigia in globo non imprimantur. Postea extremitates}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0229-body-L012}{}{}{alterius circini aperi ita ut quadrantem circumferentiae globi complectantur; alteram in qua-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0229-body-L013}{}{}{dratura scilicet 90° gradibus a gradu [longitudinis] stellae in ecliptica ponens, cum altera}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0229-body-L014}{}{}{necessarie in gradum [longitudinis] stellae incides; hanc extremitatem versus lineam iam ob}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0229-body-L015}{15}{}{latitudinem descriptam circina; locus intersectionis linearum erit centrum stellae, quam ibi}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0229-body-L016}{}{}{nota. Similiter omnes stellas quas cupis in longitudine et latitudine indica. Ad singula signa}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0229-body-L017}{}{}{circulum per polos eclipticae transeuntem describe, si vis, ut magis perspicua appareat divisio}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0229-body-L018}{}{}{signorum; habebis ita duodecim circulos in superficie globi, qui per polos eclipticae transibunt}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0229-body-L019}{}{}{et signa ab aliis separabunt.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0229-P02}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0229-body-L020}{20}{}{\Indent Postea duas armillas aequales, cupreas sume, quae habeant superficies ad angulos rectos,}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0229-body-L021}{}{}{incurvationem et latera optime confecta, crassitudinem pollicis, latitudinem necessariam ne}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0229-body-L022}{}{}{abrumpantur; earum amplitudo diametrum globi aequet, ut globus in iis ad unguem volvatur.}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0229-body-L023}{}{}{Postea duas alias armillas sume, quarum altera crassitudinem ut $\qfrac{1}{3}$ priorum, altera vero ut}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0229-body-L024}{}{}{$\qfrac{2}{3}$ habeat; ut, si minor intra maiorem ponatur, una armilla appareant. Itaque partem inter-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0229-body-L025}{25}{}{nam minoris circino fac ut internam armillarum priorum, externam ut oportet; maioris autem}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0229-body-L026}{}{}{partem internam ut externam minoris, et externam ut externam duarum priorum.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0229-P03}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0229-body-L027}{}{}{\Indent Quintam armillam etiam sume, cuius pars interna aequet externam duarum priorum,}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0229-body-L028}{}{}{externa autem ut oportet sit; intra eam igitur duae priores armillae ad unguem volvantur,}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0229-body-L029}{}{}{absque vacillatione aut perturbatione. Earum superficies autem aequales sint. Una duarum}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0229-body-L030}{30}{}{armillarum priorum horizontem effingat; eam, reliquasque armillas, divide in quatuor partes}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0229-body-L031}{}{p. 212.}{aequales, et singulos quadrantes in 18 areolas quinos gradus complectentes, ita ut in quadran-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0229-body-L032}{}{}{tem 90 gradus incidant. In areolis numerum graduum litteris alphabeti scribe, numerationem}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0229-body-L033}{}{}{ab uno quadrantum incipiens et in utrumque latus versus usque ad finem 90 perficiens; simili-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0229-body-L034}{}{}{ter in quadrante opposito fac, ut nonagesimos gradus, qui quater in armilla scribi deberent, in}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0229-body-L035}{35}{}{duo loca tantum armillae incidere facias apud extremitatem cuiusque quadrantis. In altero}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0229-body-L036}{}{}{locorum ubi 90° habentur, punctum boreale nota; in altero opposito, punctum australe.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0229-P04}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0229-body-L037}{}{}{\Indent In quovis quadrante armillae minoris punctum statue polum borealem esse, et eius oppo-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0229-body-L038}{}{}{situm polum australem; his duobus locis armillam in medio eius latitudinis et crassitudinis}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0229-body-L039}{}{}{perfora, similiterque perfora ambos aequatoris polos in globo. Tunc in ea minore armilla glo-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0229-body-L040}{40}{}{bum fige, illis duobus locis ponens duos clavos limatos ad externam armillae superficiem, ut}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0229-body-L041}{}{}{globus circa polos aequatoris volvatur. — Alteram armillam, qua minor continetur, in 360}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0229-body-L042}{}{}{gradus in 72 areolas distributos divide, atque, ut supra fecimus, litteris alphabeti numeros}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0229-body-L043}{}{}{signa; ex iis quae in areolas incidunt magnae sint ut oram armillae tangant, quae vero in}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0229-body-L044}{}{}{circulum horizontis cadunt sint $\qfrac{2}{3}$ huius magnitudinis. Numerationem ut supra fac, qua re}
\par\vspace{6pt}\nointerlineskip\hbox to\textwidth{\hss\vrule width 115.43pt height.25pt depth0pt\hss}\prevdepth=-1000pt \vspace{5pt}{\fontsize{9}{11.5}\selectfont
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0229-N01}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0229-notes-L001}{}{}{\Indent (1) Quae apud Arabes etiam valorem numeralem habent.}
}
\DSource{0230}{141}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0230-body-L001}{}{}{nonagesimus gradus duobus locis oppositis, bis in singulo loco habeatur. A loco unde numerare}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0230-body-L002}{}{}{incepisti versus partem inferiorem armillae procedens, duc incisionem quae in armillam ipsam}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0230-body-L003}{}{}{dimidia crassitudine penetret, et lata sit ut armilla horizontis. Haec incisio sit in parte ex-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0230-body-L004}{}{}{terna armillae; incisionem similem in loco opposito fac. Postea in latere interno armillae}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0230-body-L005}{5}{}{horizontis incisuram duc profundam ut est incisio alterius armillae atque armilla minor; eam}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0230-body-L006}{}{}{ab utraque parte lineae boreae et austri protrahe, latitudine armillae quae prima incisa est.}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0230-body-L007}{}{}{Supra globum postea armillarum alteram alteri apta; ita superficies circuli horizontis hemi-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0230-body-L008}{}{}{sphaerium superius globi terminabit, latitudo armillae versus hemisphaerium inferius erit, et}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0230-body-L009}{}{}{ubicumque a superficie armillae horizontis ad caput fornicis (1) exstabunt 90 gradus.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0230-P01}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0230-body-L010}{10}{}{\Indent Externae parti armillae horizontis, ab utroque latere lineae ortus occasusque, duas inci-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0230-body-L011}{}{}{siones aequales et oppositas fac, quantitate dimidiae crassitudinis armillae; in parte interna}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0230-body-L012}{}{}{alterius armillae adhuc restantis, aliam aequalem incisuram fac in duobus oppositis quadran-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0230-body-L013}{}{p. 213.}{tibus; armillam demum pone super armillam horizontis, postquam in externa superficie ar-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0230-body-L014}{}{}{millae medii Caeli, ab utroque latere lineae fornicis et paxilli Terrae — qui fornici oppo-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0230-body-L015}{15}{}{nitur — incisionem ut dimidiam crassitudinem superioris armillae fecisti, et in parte huius}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0230-body-L016}{}{}{armillae interna, ab utroque latere duorum reliquorum quadrantum, incisuram ut illam atque}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0230-body-L017}{}{}{ut crassitudinem armillae minoris in qua ambo poli sunt. His rebus perfectis, armilla perpen-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0230-body-L018}{}{}{dicularis ad armillam horizontis, per boream et austrum transiens, fiet armilla medii Caeli,}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0230-body-L019}{}{}{et locus medius eius latitudinis erit linea medii Caeli; item altera armilla, quae secat omnem}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0230-body-L020}{20}{}{regionem ab ortu ad occasum, terminabit partem sphaerae inter septentriones et meridiem}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0230-body-L021}{}{}{positam, et in medio loco eius latitudinis erit linea ortus et occasus.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0230-P02}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0230-body-L022}{}{}{\Indent Quadrantes armillae maioris, intra quam reliquae armillae volvuntur, in 90 gradus in}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0230-body-L023}{}{}{18 areolas distributos divide; litteris alphabeti, ut supra fecimus, numerum graduum usque}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0230-body-L024}{}{}{ad omnes 90° in areolis nota. In medio loco latitudinis foramen fac, quod a duobus lateribus}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0230-body-L025}{25}{}{lineae quadrantis unde divisio incepta est progrediatur; super id, in sectione superiore armillae,}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0230-body-L026}{}{}{ab utroque lineae latere incisuram fac longam ut quadrantem armillae. Fragmentum quadri-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0230-body-L027}{}{}{laterum cupri, magnitudine latitudinis armillae et latitudinis incisurae sume; in eius medio}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0230-body-L028}{}{}{lineam rectam incide, ipsum in duas partes aequales dividentem; ipsum postea ab utroque}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0230-body-L029}{}{}{lineae latere lima, inferiorem fragmenti partem rotundam et acuminatam faciens veluti clavum;}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0230-body-L030}{30}{}{longum ita sit ut armillam pertranseat et extremitate inferiore acuminata superficiem globi}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0230-body-L031}{}{}{tangat. In parte reliqua quadrilateri incide iuxta quantitatem profunditatis incisurae; quod au-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0230-body-L032}{}{}{tem super armillam eminet sit quantitate pollicis vel sicut oportet ut haec pars apparens alti-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0230-body-L033}{}{}{tudinem et radios indicet. Quando vis, hoc suo loco apta; postea hanc armillam colloca inter}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0230-body-L034}{}{}{duo ligna, quae virga ferrea coniungenda sunt ad similitudinem poli astrolabii; habeat duas}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0230-body-L035}{35}{}{extremitates acuminatas, ad quas fora, in medio latitudinis, armillam medii Caeli et armillam}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0230-body-L036}{}{}{lineae ortus occasusque. Armilla igitur in sustentaculis horum duorum polorum, quae equulo}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0230-body-L037}{}{}{in polo astrolabii (\textbf{\textit{a}}) infixo respondent, ad boream et austrum versus movebitur; sustentaculum}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0230-body-L038}{}{}{superius fac ad similitudinem ansae vel annuli, cui globus, quasi astrolabium, suspendi possit.}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0230-body-L039}{}{}{Ambas polorum extremitates cura ut in duobus iis foraminibus fortiter sint infixae; armilla}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0230-body-L040}{40}{}{itaque suo loco firma maneat et circa polos ad ortum et occasum volvatur. In armilla maiore}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0230-body-L041}{}{}{incisuram longam ut extremitatem poli fac, qui in foramen intrat; cum igitur bene suo loco}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0230-body-L042}{}{}{aptata sit, fragmento cupri, locum implenti, eam firma ne, Deo volente, a loco suo declinet (\textbf{\textit{b}}).}
\par\vspace{6pt}\nointerlineskip\hbox to\textwidth{\hss\vrule width 115.43pt height.25pt depth0pt\hss}\prevdepth=-1000pt \vspace{5pt}
\noindent\begin{minipage}[t]{.48\textwidth}\vspace{0pt}\fontsize{9}{11.5}\selectfont\setlength{\GutterOffset}{0pt}\setlength{\FullSourceWidth}{\linewidth}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0230-N01}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0230-notes_left-L001}{}{}{\Indent (1) \textit{Qubbat al-arḍ} « fornix Terrae » vel tan-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0230-notes_left-L002}{}{}{tum \textit{al-qubbah} « fornix » (Italice \textit{cupola}) est locus}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0230-notes_left-L003}{}{}{quo circulus aequinoctialis secatur circulo meri-}
\end{minipage}
\hfill\vrule width.3pt\hfill\begin{minipage}[t]{.48\textwidth}\vspace{0pt}\fontsize{9}{11.5}\selectfont\setlength{\GutterOffset}{0pt}\setlength{\FullSourceWidth}{\linewidth}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0230-notes_right-L001}{}{}{diano per medium hemisphaerium terrestre habi-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0230-notes_right-L002}{}{}{tabile transeunte. Cfr. caput VI, p. 17, l. 22 (ibique}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0230-notes_right-L003}{}{}{adn. \textbf{\textit{b}}).}
\end{minipage}
\DSource{0231}{142}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0231-P01}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0231-body-L001}{}{}{\Indent Si quolibet Terrae loco vis altitudinem cognoscere, polum borealem aequatoris, in minore}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0231-body-L002}{}{}{armilla signatum, supra horizontem borealem tantum extolle quanta est latitudo loci; eum}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0231-body-L003}{}{}{tunc firma in hac positione. Indicem radiorum et altitudinis suo loco apta, et globum tuis}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0231-body-L004}{}{}{manibus, quasi esset astrolabium suspende; indicem verte ad Solem in quadrante horizontis}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0231-body-L005}{5}{}{quem Sol occupat, et armillam ad boream et austrum volve usque quo index sibi ipsi obum-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0231-body-L006}{}{p. 214.}{bret, quod fieri nequit nisi index sit in directione Solis. Gradum signorum quem Sol occupat}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0231-body-L007}{}{}{deprehende; hunc gradum versus quadrantem Solis volve, et armillam tunc firma; gradus}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0231-body-L008}{}{}{quadrantis supra circulum horizontis exstantes erunt quantitas altitudinis. Cum armillam versus}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0231-body-L009}{}{}{gradum [longitudinis] Solis movemus, eam et gradum Solis movere non desinamus usque quo}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0231-body-L010}{10}{}{extremitas acuta indicis, quae globum tangit, in gradum Solis in ecliptica signatum incidat;}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0231-body-L011}{}{}{quod, ut diximus, non evenit nisi in loco sphaerae ubi Sol tunc est iuxta suam altitudinem}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0231-body-L012}{}{}{supra horizontem. Quod cum contingit, sphaera nobis apparet secundum suum verum statum;}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0231-body-L013}{}{}{arcus eclipticae quem armilla horizontis in oriente secat indicat gradum eo tempore ascenden-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0231-body-L014}{}{}{tem, quod autem eclipticae in occidente resecatur est gradus occidens. Locus eclipticae media}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0231-body-L015}{15}{}{latitudine armillae medii Caeli sectus, est gradus culminans; eius nadir paxillus Terrae.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0231-P02}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0231-body-L016}{}{}{\Indent Si horas diurnas elapsas scire vis, deprehende arcum circuli aequinoctialis quem armilla}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0231-body-L017}{}{}{percurrerit ab ascensione gradus Solis in globo ad ascensionem gradus tempore constituto}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0231-body-L018}{}{}{ascendentis; hic arcus est pars sphaerae revoluta ab ortu Solis ad horam datam, et quindecim}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0231-body-L019}{}{}{eius gradus unam horam aequinoctialem efficiunt. Hoc si per tempora horaria gradus Solis}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0231-body-L020}{20}{}{dividis, horas temporales invenis.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0231-P03}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0231-body-L021}{}{}{\Indent Quotiescumque polum elevaveris, usque quo ad 90 gradus altitudinis perveneris, appare-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0231-body-L022}{}{}{bunt tibi ambitus signorum et incrementa diei; item ascensiones signorum in omnibus regio-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0231-body-L023}{}{}{nibus nec non aliae res. In armilla horizontis, in eius triente (1) ad septentriones versus residuo,}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0231-body-L024}{}{}{scribe \textit{ortus aestivos}, et in austrum versus, a parte lineae orientis, \textit{ortus hiemales}; similiter}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0231-body-L025}{25}{}{a parte lineae occidentis, ad boream \textit{occasus aestivos} et ad austrum \textit{occasus hiemales}; hoc}
\par\vspace{-\ht\strutbox}\noindent\begin{minipage}[t]{0.462\FullSourceWidth}\vspace{0pt}\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0231-body-F01}{}\input{figures/AB01-PDF0231-F01}\end{minipage}\hfill\begin{minipage}[t]{0.518\FullSourceWidth}\vspace{0pt}\setlength{\GutterOffset}{0.482\FullSourceWidth}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0231-body-L026}{}{p. 215.}{modo necessarias directiones ascensio-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0231-body-L027}{}{}{num et occasuum cognosces. — Si}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0231-body-L028}{}{}{gradum Solis volvis, index autem in}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0231-body-L029}{}{}{sua positione manens Solem prospicit,}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0231-body-L030}{30}{}{etiam armilla medii Caeli lineam me-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0231-body-L031}{}{}{ridianam prospiciet.}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0231-body-L032}{}{}{\textbf{Haec est imago quadrantis mu-}}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0231-body-L033}{}{}{\mbox{}\rlap{\hspace*{3.87mm}\textbf{ralis de quo nunc est dicen-}}}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0231-body-L034}{}{}{\mbox{}\rlap{\hspace*{3.87mm}\textbf{dum} (2).}}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0231-P04}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0231-body-L035}{35}{}{\Indent Dicit [auctor]; Tabulam cupream}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0231-body-L036}{}{}{vel lapideam vel ligneam, quae formam}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0231-body-L037}{}{}{quadratam habeat, et cuius latus duos}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0231-body-L038}{}{}{cubitos (3) complectatur, sume; quo}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0231-body-L039}{}{}{maior eo exactior erit. Sit igitur tabu-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0231-body-L040}{40}{}{la ABCD; eius punctum A centrum}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0231-body-L041}{}{}{ponens, quantitate AB, AC (4) arcum}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0231-body-L042}{}{}{BC quadrantis describe, quem, lineis}
\end{minipage}\par
\par\vspace{6pt}\nointerlineskip\hbox to\textwidth{\hss\vrule width 115.43pt height.25pt depth0pt\hss}\prevdepth=-1000pt \vspace{5pt}
\noindent\begin{minipage}[t]{.48\textwidth}\vspace{0pt}\fontsize{9}{11.5}\selectfont\setlength{\GutterOffset}{0pt}\setlength{\FullSourceWidth}{\linewidth}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0231-N01}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0231-notes_left-L001}{}{}{\Indent (1) Error pro « quadrante » videtur.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0231-N02}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0231-notes_left-L002}{}{}{\Indent (2) Est \textgreek{τεταρτημόριον} \Name{Ptolemaei}, \textit{Almag.} I,}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0231-notes_left-L003}{}{}{11 (ed. Halma, t. I, p. 47-49).}
\end{minipage}
\hfill\vrule width.3pt\hfill\begin{minipage}[t]{.48\textwidth}\vspace{0pt}\fontsize{9}{11.5}\selectfont\setlength{\GutterOffset}{0pt}\setlength{\FullSourceWidth}{\linewidth}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0231-N03}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0231-notes_right-L001}{}{}{\Indent (3) V. adn. 3, pag. 144.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0231-N04}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0231-notes_right-L002}{}{}{\Indent (4) Pro AB, AC, cod. ABC.}
\end{minipage}
\DSource{0232}{143}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0232-body-L001}{}{}{per centrum transeuntibus, in 90 gradus divide; in intervallo unius gradus quot minuta putes}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0232-body-L002}{}{}{nota. Superficies quadrati sit expolita, plana, nusquam declinans sive vacillans, ut divisiones}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0232-body-L003}{}{}{bene fieri possint. Deinde duos stilos cupreos, eiusdem magnitudinis, rotunde tornatos, et acutos}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0232-body-L004}{}{}{sume; quorum alterum in A, alterum in C (1) fige. Item, prius quam lineam meridianam (2)}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0232-body-L005}{5}{}{invenias, filum perpendiculi ab extremo stilo A mitte usque ad extremum stilum C, ut pro-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0232-body-L006}{}{}{cul dubio scias superficiem et collocationem tabulae nusquam declinare. Superficies in qua}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0232-body-L007}{}{}{lineae et divisiones notatae sunt, ortum prospiciat; latus AB sit austri (3) directio. Umbram}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0232-body-L008}{}{}{tempore meridiano observa, et locum ex divisionibus quadrantis in quem umbra stili A singulo}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0232-body-L009}{}{}{quoque die incidit nota. Postea fragmentum cupreum H, cuius curvatura arcui BC conveniat,}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0232-body-L010}{10}{}{sume; per medium eius locum H lineam describe, atque fragmentum ipsum sub umbrae loco}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0232-body-L011}{}{}{ita pone ut certissime indicet locum umbrae, et linea H sit in centro latitudinis umbrae stili.}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0232-body-L012}{}{}{Hoc modo gradus et minuta incidentiae umbrae nota; item maximam distantiam zenithalem}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0232-body-L013}{}{}{Solis aestate et hieme. Sit G terminus aestivus, K terminus hiemalis; arcus KG erit arcus}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0232-body-L014}{}{}{inter duo solstitia, cuius locus medius est L; Sole igitur per punctum aequinoctii vernalis}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0232-body-L015}{15}{p. 216.}{aut autumni transeunte, umbra stili A in punctum L arcus BC incidet. Hoc modo, si Deus}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0232-body-L016}{}{}{vult, distantia zenithalis et altitudo Solis super horizontem deprehenditur quotidie; apud Deum}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0232-body-L017}{}{}{auxilium reperitur. Necesse etiam est latera tabulae, Deo volente, aequalia et ad angulos}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0232-body-L018}{}{}{rectos esse.}
\par\vspace{-0.54mm}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0232-H19}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0232-body-L019}{}{}{\CenterLine{\textbf{Haec est imago triquetri} (4).}}
\par\vspace{0.56mm}
\par\vspace{-\ht\strutbox}\noindent\begin{minipage}[t]{0.418\FullSourceWidth}\vspace{0pt}\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0232-body-F01}{}\input{figures/AB01-PDF0232-F01}\end{minipage}\hfill\begin{minipage}[t]{0.562\FullSourceWidth}\vspace{0pt}\setlength{\GutterOffset}{0.438\FullSourceWidth}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0232-P01}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0232-body-L020}{20}{}{\Indent Dicit [auctor]: Tres regulas ligneas}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0232-body-L021}{}{}{et planas, superficies quadrilateras habentes,}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0232-body-L022}{}{}{sume; per medium locum cuiusque regulae}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0232-body-L023}{}{}{lineam duc longam ut totam superficiem.}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0232-body-L024}{}{}{Ad repraesentandas lineas quae per media}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0232-body-L025}{25}{}{loca regularum FG, FL, HM transeunt, hanc}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0232-body-L026}{}{}{figuram pone. Punctum H sit in linea re-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0232-body-L027}{}{}{gulae FG; linea FH longitudinem quinque}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0232-body-L028}{}{}{cubitorum habeat; reliqua linea HG regulae}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0232-body-L029}{}{}{lapidi vel columnae firmissime sit infixa, ut}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0232-body-L030}{30}{}{nusquam moveatur et vacillet. — Secundam}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0232-body-L031}{}{}{regulam, omnium minimam, sume, cuius}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0232-body-L032}{}{}{linea FL lineam FH aequet; super eam,}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0232-body-L033}{}{}{in eius latitudine quae per superficiem re-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0232-body-L034}{}{}{gulae FG transit, pone et superficiei regu-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0232-body-L035}{35}{}{lae firmissime apta duas pinnulas cupreas}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0232-body-L036}{}{}{aequales et in medio loco perforatas, ut}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0232-body-L037}{}{}{sunt pinnulae astrolabii (5). Earum alteram}
\end{minipage}\par
\DLine{AB01-PDF0232-body-L038}{}{}{prope F, alteram prope L constitue. Postea has duas regulas (6) in F fora et coniunge inter}
\par\vspace{6pt}\nointerlineskip\hbox to\textwidth{\hss\vrule width 115.43pt height.25pt depth0pt\hss}\prevdepth=-1000pt \vspace{5pt}
\noindent\begin{minipage}[t]{.48\textwidth}\vspace{0pt}\fontsize{9}{11.5}\selectfont\setlength{\GutterOffset}{0pt}\setlength{\FullSourceWidth}{\linewidth}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0232-N01}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0232-notes_left-L001}{}{}{\Indent (1) Cod. B; similiter in linea sequenti.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0232-N02}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0232-notes_left-L002}{}{}{\Indent (2) Cod., in cuius figura prope B littera E, prope}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0232-notes_left-L003}{}{}{D littera F legitur, perperam, ut Plato, addit: « quae}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0232-notes_left-L004}{}{}{« est linea EF ». Linea meridiana nonnisi AB esse}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0232-notes_left-L005}{}{}{potest, ut infra auctor ipse dicit.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0232-N03}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0232-notes_left-L006}{}{}{\Indent (3) Cod. « qiblae ». AB est linea a borea ad}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0232-notes_left-L007}{}{}{austrum.}
\end{minipage}
\hfill\vrule width.3pt\hfill\begin{minipage}[t]{.48\textwidth}\vspace{0pt}\fontsize{9}{11.5}\selectfont\setlength{\GutterOffset}{0pt}\setlength{\FullSourceWidth}{\linewidth}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0232-N04}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0232-notes_right-L001}{}{}{\Indent (4) Triquetrum seu regula parallactica ab Al-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0232-notes_right-L002}{}{}{-Battānī semper « alhidada longa » appellatur. Est}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0232-notes_right-L003}{}{}{\textgreek{ὄργανον παραλλακτικόν} \Name{Ptolemaei} \textit{Almag.} V, 12}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0232-notes_right-L004}{}{}{(ed. Halma, t. I, 328-332).}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0232-N05}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0232-notes_right-L005}{}{}{\Indent (5) Pinnulae scilicet quae sunt pars alhidadae}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0232-notes_right-L006}{}{}{(dioptrae) in astrolabio.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0232-N06}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0232-notes_right-L007}{}{}{\Indent (6) Cod. « pinnulas ».}
\end{minipage}
\DSource{0233}{144}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0233-body-L001}{}{}{se polo (1), firmato quasi esset polus astrolabii, ut regulam FL ad libitum tuum in boream et}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0233-body-L002}{}{}{austrum versus sine perturbatione movere possis.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0233-P01}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0233-body-L003}{}{}{\Indent Deinde lineam HK, regulae HM, aequalem fac lineae FH et lineae FL; eam in 30 partes,}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0233-body-L004}{}{}{partes in quot minuta potes divide; similiter lineam residuam KM partire usque ad finem}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0233-body-L005}{5}{p. 217.}{sinus arcus 45°, qua re partes totius lineae HM non excedent 42\textsuperscript{\textit{p}} $\qfrac{1}{2}$ circiter; reliquum re-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0233-body-L006}{}{}{gulae abscinde. Regulas FM et HM in puncto H duobus rotundis foraminibus, ad similitudinem}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0233-body-L007}{}{}{duorum priorum, perfora, et eas inter se coniunge polo, firmato quasi esset polus astrolabii,}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0233-body-L008}{}{}{ut regula HM ad libitum versus boream et austrum sine ulla perturbatione verti possit. In}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0233-body-L009}{}{}{linea HM, hoc est in medio latitudinis loco regulae HM, in superficie superiore externa, inci-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0233-body-L010}{10}{}{sionem ut dimidiam regulae latitudinem per totam longitudinem duc; similiter in extremitate}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0233-body-L011}{}{}{regulae FL incisuram ut dimidiam latitudinem regulae HM fac; postea extremitates quadra-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0233-body-L012}{}{}{turae regulae FL paulatim in utraque parte retunde, ut facile et leviter possit supra lineam}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0233-body-L013}{}{}{HM moveri ac volvi. Ob incisionem, facies duarum regularum una fient, nam altera super}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0233-body-L014}{}{}{alteram non elevabitur, nec prominebit. Deinde columnam ABCD, in qua regula FHG est infixa,}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0233-body-L015}{15}{}{verte usque quo linea BC eius perimetri quadratae in lineam meridianam incidat; a puncto}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0233-body-L016}{}{}{autem F ad H versus perpendiculum ponatur, ut regula erecta ad angulos rectos appareat,}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0233-body-L017}{}{}{et superficies regulae sit perpendicularis ad lineam meridianam atque prospiciat orientem.}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0233-body-L018}{}{}{Ambae pinnulae in secunda regula positae, et divisiones regulae HM ortum quoque prospi-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0233-body-L019}{}{}{ciant; hae divisiones autem in longitudine dimidiae partis regulae, in cuius altera medietate}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0233-body-L020}{20}{}{est incisio, notentur.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0233-P02}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0233-body-L021}{}{}{\Indent Cum Sol per lineam meridianam transit, regulam pinnulis praeditam verte ad septentriones}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0233-body-L022}{}{}{et austrum, ut pinnula superior [inferiori] toti obumbret, et radii Solis, per foramen pin-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0233-body-L023}{}{}{nulae superioris transeuntes, foramen inferioris intrent. Eodem tempore regulam HM porrige}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0233-body-L024}{}{}{atque versus boream et austrum volve usque quo linea HM, in medio regulae incisa, cum}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0233-body-L025}{25}{}{puncto L regulae FL cohaereat per duas incisiones quas fecisti. Numerum divisionum regulae}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0233-body-L026}{}{}{HM, in quem punctum L incidit, quaere in tabula sinuum et in arcum converte; arcus dupli-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0233-body-L027}{}{}{catus indicabit distantiam zenithalem Solis, si numeratio regulae inceperit a puncto H. Simili-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0233-body-L028}{}{}{ter si lineam HK in 60 partes, quae semidiametrum efficiunt, diviseris, et lineam KM [a 60\textsuperscript{\textit{p}}]}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0233-body-L029}{}{}{ad finem 85\textsuperscript{\textit{p}} (2) partitus eris, postea dimidium numerum incidentiae puncti L converteris in}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0233-body-L030}{30}{}{arcum, arcus duplicatus erit praecedenti aequalis (\textbf{\textit{c}}).}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0233-P03}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0233-body-L031}{}{p. 218.}{\Indent Observationes quae his regulis fiunt optimae sunt; proveniunt enim e circulo cuius dia-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0233-body-L032}{}{}{metrus 10 cubitos (3) complectitur. Item si longitudinem regulae FL duplam aut minorem duplo}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0233-body-L033}{}{}{usque ad punctum N feceris, et pinnulam quae est prope F prope N posueris, distantia inter}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0233-body-L034}{}{}{duas pinnulas maior fiet, et observatio certior. — His regulis altitudinem quocumque tempore}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0233-body-L035}{35}{}{deprehendes, regulam FG firmiter columnae ABCD infigendo, ut ad omnes horizontis regiones}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0233-body-L036}{}{}{in quibus Sol consistat verti possit. Item, si necessarium est, altitudinem Lunae vel alterius}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0233-body-L037}{}{}{sideris cognosces, arcum distantiae zenithalis a 90° (4) demendo; nam, si Deus vult, altitudo}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0233-body-L038}{}{}{est complementum distantiae zenithalis (5).}
\par\vspace{6pt}\nointerlineskip\hbox to\textwidth{\hss\vrule width 115.43pt height.25pt depth0pt\hss}\prevdepth=-1000pt \vspace{5pt}
\noindent\begin{minipage}[t]{.48\textwidth}\vspace{0pt}\fontsize{9}{11.5}\selectfont\setlength{\GutterOffset}{0pt}\setlength{\FullSourceWidth}{\linewidth}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0233-N01}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0233-notes_left-L001}{}{}{\Indent (1) I. e. axe. Cfr. adn. \textbf{\textit{a}} huius capitis.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0233-N02}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0233-notes_left-L002}{}{}{\Indent (2) Plato 35\textsuperscript{\textit{p}}.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0233-N03}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0233-notes_left-L003}{}{}{\Indent (3) Cubiti varii sunt; ab astronomis adhibetur}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0233-notes_left-L004}{}{}{cubitus legalis (\textit{dhirā‘ shar‘iyyah}) vel communis}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0233-notes_left-L005}{}{}{(\textit{dhirā‘ al-‘āmmah}) vel iustae manus (\textit{dhirā‘ al-}}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0233-notes_left-L006}{}{}{\textit{-yad al-‘ādilah}) vel niger (\textit{dhirā‘ sawdā’}), 24 digitos}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0233-notes_left-L007}{}{}{complectens. Eius longitudo 0,493 metrorum; v. quae}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0233-notes_left-L008}{}{}{scripsi in \textit{Il valore metrico del grado di meridiano}}
\end{minipage}
\hfill\vrule width.3pt\hfill\begin{minipage}[t]{.48\textwidth}\vspace{0pt}\fontsize{9}{11.5}\selectfont\setlength{\GutterOffset}{0pt}\setlength{\FullSourceWidth}{\linewidth}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0233-notes_right-L001}{}{}{\textit{secondo i geografi arabi}, Torino 1893, pag. 23-39}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0233-notes_right-L002}{}{}{(= \textit{Cosmos di G. Cora}, ser. II, t. XI, p. 105-121).}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0233-N04}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0233-notes_right-L003}{}{}{\Indent (4) Plato 60°.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0233-N05}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0233-notes_right-L004}{}{}{\Indent (5) Hic desinit versio Platonis tam in editioni-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0233-notes_right-L005}{}{}{bus typis excusis quam in codicibus Parisiensibus;}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0233-notes_right-L006}{}{}{codicis Mediolanensis finem iam antea fecit titulus:}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0233-notes_right-L007}{}{}{« De compositione alhidadae per quam fiunt obser-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0233-notes_right-L008}{}{}{« vationes », quem nihil sequitur.}
\end{minipage}
\DSource{0234}{145}
\par\fontsize{14.5}{17}\selectfont
\par\vspace*{\dimexpr 9.13mm-\baselineskip\relax}\prevdepth=0pt
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0234-H01}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0234-body-L001}{}{}{\CenterLine{\textbf{A.} {\fontsize{11.1}{13.8}\selectfont (1)}}}
\par\fontsize{14.2}{16}\selectfont
\par\vspace{3.65mm}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0234-H02}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0234-body-L002}{5}{}{\CenterLine{Caput de inveniendis per tabulas initiis annorum et mensium Arabum,}}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0234-H03}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0234-body-L003}{}{}{\CenterLine{quae ad chronologiam statuendam necessaria sunt {\fontsize{11.1}{13.8}\selectfont (\textbf{\textit{a}})}.}}
\par\fontsize{11.1}{13.8}\selectfont
\par\vspace{3.96mm}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0234-P01}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0234-body-L004}{}{}{\Indent Dicit [auctor]: Signum anni aerae Arabicae, id est hegirae, in quo es, si deprehendere}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0234-body-L005}{}{}{cupis, numero annorum hegirae, si 210 excedat, quoties potes 210 deme, et residuo tan-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0234-body-L006}{10}{}{tum utere; nam, singulis 210 annis [perfectis], annorum signa ad pristinum ordinem semper}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0234-body-L007}{}{}{reveniunt (2). Annos ita repertos, addito anno in quo es si eius primus dies iam inceperit,}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0234-body-L008}{}{}{quaere in columna annorum [ab hegira] collectorum, et signum eis respondens sume. Si nu-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0234-body-L009}{}{}{merum tuum in ea tabula non reperis, accipe signum numeri proxime inferioris in tabula}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0234-body-L010}{}{}{notati; annos residuos quaere in tabula annorum singulorum, eorumque signum adde signo}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0234-body-L011}{15}{}{antea reperto. Si summa numerum septem excedit, de ea 7 deme; remanebit signum anni}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0234-body-L012}{}{}{quaesiti. Si contra tabula annorum singulorum uti necesse tibi non fuit, numero reperto sub}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0234-body-L013}{}{}{annis [ab hegira] collectis semper unum adde; aut, si mavis, in tabula annorum collectorum}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0234-body-L014}{}{}{annos praeter triginta quaere, in tabula annorum singulorum triginta, et quod ex utraque}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0234-body-L015}{}{p. 219.}{tabula inveneris in unum collige. Ambae viae ad reperiendum signum tui anni ducunt. Nu-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0234-body-L016}{20}{}{merum repertum a die dominica proiice; exibit dies quo annus incipit, idest initium mensis}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0234-body-L017}{}{}{al–muḥarram (\textbf{\textit{b}}).}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0234-P02}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0234-body-L018}{}{}{\Indent Si initium alterius mensis scire cupis, huius mensis signum in tabula mensium quaere,}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0234-body-L019}{}{}{ipsumque signo anni adde; excedente summa numerum septem, de ea 7 deme, et residuum}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0234-body-L020}{}{}{a die dominica proiice; habebis initium mensis quaesiti. Hoc calculo utendum est ad invenien-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0234-body-L021}{25}{}{dos motus stellarum in tabulis ad aeram Arabum descriptis; aliam rationem calculi ne adhi-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0234-body-L022}{}{}{beas, si quid magis vel minus quam haec praebeat.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0234-P03}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0234-body-L023}{}{}{\Indent Si vis annos bisextiles Arabum cognoscere, annos hegirae divide per 30; si residuum}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0234-body-L024}{}{}{divisionis 2, 5, 7 (3), 10, 13, 16, 18 (4), 21, 24, 26 (5), 29 est, annus bisextilis habetur; alio-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0234-body-L025}{}{}{quin non. Id, si Deus vult, in tabula ad invenienda signa singulorum annorum ostendam. Apud}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0234-body-L026}{30}{}{Deum solum auxilium invenimus.}
\par\fontsize{14.5}{17}\selectfont
\par\vspace{7.48mm}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0234-H27}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0234-body-L027}{}{}{\CenterLine{\textbf{B.}}}
\par\fontsize{14.2}{15.8}\selectfont
\par\vspace{3.57mm}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0234-H28}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0234-body-L028}{35}{}{\CenterLine{Caput de cognoscendis initiis mensium Romanorum}}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0234-H29}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0234-body-L029}{}{}{\CenterLine{ad aeram Dhū ’l-qarnayn,}}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0234-H30}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0234-body-L030}{}{}{\CenterLine{quae est per 28 dividenda, cum additione anni.}}
\par\fontsize{11.1}{13.8}\selectfont
\par\vspace{3.96mm}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0234-P04}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0234-body-L031}{}{}{\Indent Dicit [auctor]: Si diem hebdomadae in quem initium cuiuslibet mensis Romani incidit}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0234-body-L032}{40}{}{scire vis, annos aerae Dhū ’l–qarnayn, simul cum anno in quo ipse es licet unus dies tantum}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0234-body-L033}{}{}{mensis aylūl inceperit, sume, iisque semper annum adde quasi radicem praeteritam (6). De}
\par\vspace{6pt}\nointerlineskip\hbox to\textwidth{\hss\vrule width 115.43pt height.25pt depth0pt\hss}\prevdepth=-1000pt \vspace{5pt}
\noindent\begin{minipage}[t]{.48\textwidth}\vspace{0pt}\fontsize{9}{11.5}\selectfont\setlength{\GutterOffset}{0pt}\setlength{\FullSourceWidth}{\linewidth}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0234-N01}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0234-notes_left-L001}{}{}{\Indent (1) His litteris parva capita sequentia, quae}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0234-notes_left-L002}{}{}{tantum in textu Arabico Escurialensi leguntur et}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0234-notes_left-L003}{}{}{nullum numerum habent, distinxi.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0234-N02}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0234-notes_left-L004}{}{}{\Indent (2) Cfr. tabulam fol. 157,v.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0234-N03}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0234-notes_left-L005}{}{}{\Indent (3) Cod. 8.}
\end{minipage}
\hfill\vrule width.3pt\hfill\begin{minipage}[t]{.48\textwidth}\vspace{0pt}\fontsize{9}{11.5}\selectfont\setlength{\GutterOffset}{0pt}\setlength{\FullSourceWidth}{\linewidth}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0234-N04}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0234-notes_right-L001}{}{}{\Indent (4) Cod. 19.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0234-N05}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0234-notes_right-L002}{}{}{\Indent (5) Cod. 27.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0234-N06}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0234-notes_right-L003}{}{}{\Indent (6) Haec verba in codice corrupta; pro \ArabicRun{ماصلا},}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0234-notes_right-L004}{}{}{quod nullum sensum praebet, lego \ArabicRun{ماضيا} « praeteri-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0234-notes_right-L005}{}{}{tam ». Ita quoque pag. 146, adn. 5.}
\end{minipage}
\par\vspace{5mm}\hfill{\fontsize{8}{10}\selectfont 19}\hspace{10mm}
\DSource{0235}{146}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0235-body-L001}{}{}{summa quoties potes 28 deme (1), ita ut residuum sit 28 aut minus quam 28; residuum in}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0235-body-L002}{}{}{tabula annorum Romanorum singulorum quaere, et numerus ei in columna mensis quaesiti}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0235-body-L003}{}{}{respondens erit signum illius mensis Romani. Hoc signum a die dominica proiice; numerus}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0235-body-L004}{}{}{ita proveniens erit nomen primi diei mensis (2). — Primus dies aylūl (3) est initium anni. —}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0235-body-L005}{5}{}{Annus bisextilis cognoscitur e nota quam a latere mensis subāṭ posita est; volente Deo, quartus}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0235-body-L006}{}{}{quisque annus bisextilis est (\textbf{\textit{a}}).}
\par\fontsize{14.5}{17}\selectfont
\par\vspace{4.01mm}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0235-H07}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0235-body-L007}{}{p. 220.}{\CenterLine{\textbf{C.}}}
\par\fontsize{14.2}{15.87}\selectfont
\par\vspace{2.86mm}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0235-H08}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0235-body-L008}{10}{}{\CenterLine{Caput de inveniendis per has tabulas aeris Arabum et Romanorum}}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0235-H09}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0235-body-L009}{}{}{\CenterLine{altera ex altera.}}
\par\fontsize{11.1}{13.8}\selectfont
\par\vspace{4.02mm}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0235-P01}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0235-body-L010}{}{}{\Indent Dicit [auctor]: Si ab aera hegirae aeram Romanorum cognoscere vis, tuos annos Arabicos}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0235-body-L011}{}{}{quaere in prima columna annorum hegirae, et sume quod iis respondet ex annis, mensibus}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0235-body-L012}{15}{}{et diebus Romanis in columna quae « anni Dhū ’l–qarnayn » inscribitur (4); habebis annos, men-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0235-body-L013}{}{}{ses et dies aerae Dhū ’l–qarnayn transactos usque ad primum diem mensis al–muḥarram anni}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0235-body-L014}{}{}{Arabici in quo es. [Hoc anni initium] in quem hebdomadae diem incidat ita cognosce: Nume-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0235-body-L015}{}{}{rum dierum hebdomadae qui, in columna « initio al–muḥarram » inscripta, respondet numero}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0235-body-L016}{}{}{tui anni hegirae sume; ipse erit dies transactus mensis Romani iam in tabula reperti, et cum}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0235-body-L017}{20}{}{chronologia in initio libri descripta conveniet.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0235-P02}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0235-body-L018}{}{}{\Indent Si alium mensem Romanum vis, initium tui mensis Arabici vide in quem hebdomadae}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0235-body-L019}{}{}{diem incidat: scilicet numero dierum per quos initium al–muḥarram cognovisti, adde signum}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0235-body-L020}{}{}{mensis Arabici dati, quod in tabula signorum mensium Arabum invenitur; summa numerum 7}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0235-body-L021}{}{}{excedente, 7 de ea deme; residuum a die dominica proiice, et habebis primum diem mensis}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0235-body-L022}{25}{}{Arabici dati. Huius mensis dies elapsos cognosce; postea ab initio al-muḥarram usque ad}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0235-body-L023}{}{}{diem datam procedens, numera dies mensium praeteritorum, alteri mensi 30 dies, alteri 29}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0235-body-L024}{}{}{tribuens; de horum dierum summa, una cum diebus mensis tantum incepti collecta, diem}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0235-body-L025}{}{}{deme; residuo adde dies quos in tabula iam e mense Romano elapsisse inveneras. Hanc sum-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0235-body-L026}{}{}{mam ab initio illius mensis Romani proiice, singulis mensibus dies ipsorum tribuens; dies mensis}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0235-body-L027}{30}{}{ad quem pervenies erit dies in quo es e mense Romano anni Dhū ’l–qarnayn in tabula re-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0235-body-L028}{}{p. 221.}{perti; sed si aylūl iam incepisse invenis, annis Dhū ’l–qarnayn in tabula repertis annum adde.}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0235-body-L029}{}{}{Si annus est bisextilis, mensi subāṭ 29 dies tribue; annus bisextilis autem a notis positis in}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0235-body-L030}{}{}{tabula signorum mensium Romanorum cognoscitur.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0235-P03}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0235-body-L031}{}{}{\Indent Similiter ab aera Romana deprehendes diem mensis et anni Arabici in quo es. In hac}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0235-body-L032}{35}{}{tabula, e columna annorum Dhū ’l–qarnayn cognosce quot dies mensis Romani transierint,}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0235-body-L033}{}{}{nam in ultimum diem elapsum incidet initium al–muḥarram tui anni hegirae. Postea dies resi-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0235-body-L034}{}{}{duos illius mensis Romani accipe, et iis adde dies elapsos post eum mensem usque ad diem}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0235-body-L035}{}{}{datam; huic summae diem semper tanquam radicem praeteritam (5) adde. Quod ita provenit,}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0235-body-L036}{}{}{ab initio al–muḥarram proiice, singulis hegirae mensibus dies ipsorum tribuens; et dies ad}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0235-body-L037}{40}{}{quem pervenies erit dies quaesitus ex anno Arabico in tabula invento; sed si numerando men-}
