\DLine{AB01-PDF0235-body-L038}{}{}{sem al-muḥarram iam uno saltem die incepisse invenis, annis hegirae e tabula depromptis}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0235-body-L039}{}{}{annum adde. Hoc calculo, si Deus vult, utendum est.}
\par\vspace{6pt}\nointerlineskip\hbox to\textwidth{\hss\vrule width 115.43pt height.25pt depth0pt\hss}\prevdepth=-1000pt \vspace{5pt}
\noindent\begin{minipage}[t]{.48\textwidth}\vspace{0pt}\fontsize{9}{11.5}\selectfont\setlength{\GutterOffset}{0pt}\setlength{\FullSourceWidth}{\linewidth}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0235-N01}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0235-notes_left-L001}{}{}{\Indent (1) In hac aera 28 annis perfectis, signa men-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0235-notes_left-L002}{}{}{sium eodem ordine reveniunt.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0235-N02}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0235-notes_left-L003}{}{}{\Indent (2) Cfr. tabulam fol. 158,r.}
\end{minipage}
\hfill\vrule width.3pt\hfill\begin{minipage}[t]{.48\textwidth}\vspace{0pt}\fontsize{9}{11.5}\selectfont\setlength{\GutterOffset}{0pt}\setlength{\FullSourceWidth}{\linewidth}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0235-N03}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0235-notes_right-L001}{}{}{\Indent (3) I. e. Septembris; cfr. cap. XXXII, p. 67, adn. 1.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0235-N04}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0235-notes_right-L002}{}{}{\Indent (4) F. 158,v. et seqq.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0235-N05}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0235-notes_right-L003}{}{}{\Indent (5) Cfr. pag. 145, adn. 6.}
\end{minipage}
\DSource{0236}{147}
\par\fontsize{14.5}{17}\selectfont
\par\vspace*{\dimexpr 9.10mm-\baselineskip\relax}\prevdepth=0pt
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0236-H01}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0236-body-L001}{}{}{\CenterLine{\textbf{D.}}}
\par\fontsize{14.2}{16}\selectfont
\par\vspace{3.79mm}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0236-H02}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0236-body-L002}{5}{}{\CenterLine{Caput de praegressionibus, stationibus et regressibus planetarum,}}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0236-H03}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0236-body-L003}{}{}{\CenterLine{atque de eorum cursu in epicyclis.}}
\par\fontsize{11.1}{13.8}\selectfont
\par\vspace{3.96mm}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0236-P01}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0236-body-L004}{}{}{\Indent Dicit [auctor]: Saturnus a superiore parte epicycli 118 $\qfrac{1}{2}$ dies directus movetur; postea}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0236-body-L005}{}{}{2 $\qfrac{1}{2}$ + $\qfrac{1}{4}$ + $\qfrac{1}{8}$ [= 2 $\qfrac{7}{8}$] dies moratur, demum in suo itinere 135 $\qfrac{1}{3}$ + $\qfrac{1}{4}$ dies regreditur;}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0236-body-L006}{10}{}{quo facto, ut antea moratur et progreditur usque quo ad superiorem epicycli partem redierit.}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0236-body-L007}{}{}{Dies omnes quibus epicyclum totum percurrit sunt fere 378 dies et 2 horae. — Si eius ano-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0236-body-L008}{}{}{malia aequata est a 0° ad 112° 45′ (1) ipse progreditur; deinde usque ad finem 115° 29′ mo-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0236-body-L009}{}{}{ratur, postea ad finem 244° 31′ regreditur, usque ad finem 247° 15′ (2) rursus moratur, de-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0236-body-L010}{}{}{mum, si Deus vult, usque ad finem circumferentiae directus est.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0236-P02}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0236-body-L011}{15}{p. 222.}{\Indent Iupiter a puncto superiore epicycli 137 $\qfrac{1}{2}$ dies directus est, postea 3 $\qfrac{1}{4}$ + $\qfrac{1}{5}$ dies mo-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0236-body-L012}{}{}{rans, et 117 (3) dies regrediens; deinde ut antea moratur et praegreditur, qua re 399 (4) die-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0236-body-L013}{}{}{bus, detractis 2 horis, circiter epicyclum totum percurrit. — Eius anomalia aequata a 0° ad}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0236-body-L014}{}{}{124° 5′ exstante, ipse directus est; usque ad finem 127° 11′, morans; ad finem 232° 49′, re-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0236-body-L015}{}{}{trogradus; ad finem 235° 55′ (5), morans denuo; et ad finem circumferentiae rursus directus.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0236-P03}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0236-body-L016}{20}{}{\Indent Mars a puncto superiore epicycli 341 $\qfrac{1}{5}$ dies directus procedit, postea 25 $\qfrac{1}{3}$ (6) dies mo-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0236-body-L017}{}{}{ratur, et 46 $\qfrac{2}{3}$ regreditur; denique ut antea moratur et praegreditur; itaque fere 780 — $\qfrac{1}{10}$ (7)}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0236-body-L018}{}{}{diebus epicyclum totum percurrit. — Eius anomalia aequata a 0° ad 157° 33′ (8) exstante,}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0236-body-L019}{}{}{ipse directus est; usque ad finem 169° 14′, stans; usque ad finem 190° 46′, retrogradus, usque}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0236-body-L020}{}{}{ad finem 202° 27′ (9), stans, demum usque ad circumferentiae finem directus.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0236-P04}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0236-body-L021}{25}{}{\Indent Venus a puncto superiore epicycli 269 $\qfrac{1}{6\times 2}$ dies praegreditur, 4 (10) dies moratur, 37 $\qfrac{4}{5}$}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0236-body-L022}{}{}{dies retrogradus manet; postea ut antea moratur et praegreditur, qua re epicyclum totum}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0236-body-L023}{}{}{583 $\qfrac{2}{3}$ + $\qfrac{1}{4}$ (11) diebus percurrit. — Eius anomalia aequata a 0° ad 165° 53′ exstante, pla-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0236-body-L024}{}{}{neta directus est; ad finem 168° 21′, stans; ad finem 191° 39′, retrogradus; ad finem 194° 7′,}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0236-body-L025}{}{}{rursus stans; denique ad finem circumferentiae, directus.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0236-P05}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0236-body-L026}{30}{}{\Indent Mercurius a puncto superiore epicycli 46 $\qfrac{1}{3}$ + $\qfrac{1}{4}$ dies praegreditur, 20 fere horas mo-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0236-body-L027}{}{}{ratur, 21 $\qfrac{1}{10}$ dies retrogradus est, demum rursus ut antea moratur et praegreditur. Epicy-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0236-body-L028}{}{}{clum igitur 115 $\qfrac{1}{2}$ + $\qfrac{1}{4}$ + $\qfrac{1}{8}$ [= 115 $\qfrac{7}{8}$] fere diebus percurrit. — Eius anomalia aequata}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0236-body-L029}{}{}{a 0° ad 144° 40′ exstante, ipse directus est; ad finem 147° 13′, stans; ad finem 212° 47′, re-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0236-body-L030}{}{}{trogradus; postea ad finem 215° 20′ (12), stans; et ad finem circumferentiae, directus. — Deus}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0236-body-L031}{35}{}{haec melius scit (\textbf{\textit{a}}).}
\par\vspace{6pt}\nointerlineskip\hbox to\textwidth{\hss\vrule width 115.43pt height.25pt depth0pt\hss}\prevdepth=-1000pt \vspace{5pt}
\noindent\begin{minipage}[t]{.48\textwidth}\vspace{0pt}\fontsize{9}{11.5}\selectfont\setlength{\GutterOffset}{0pt}\setlength{\FullSourceWidth}{\linewidth}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0236-N01}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0236-notes_left-L001}{}{}{\Indent (1) Cod. 112° 44′. — Cfr. tabulas stationum}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0236-notes_left-L002}{}{}{fol. 223,v. et 224,r.; at de omnibus numeris huius}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0236-notes_left-L003}{}{}{capitis, quod spurium videtur, v. adn. \textbf{\textit{a}}.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0236-N02}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0236-notes_left-L004}{}{}{\Indent (2) Cod. 247° 14′.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0236-N03}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0236-notes_left-L005}{}{}{\Indent (3) Cod. 116.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0236-N04}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0236-notes_left-L006}{}{}{\Indent (4) Cod. 369.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0236-N05}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0236-notes_left-L007}{}{}{\Indent (5) Cod. 235° 0′.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0236-N06}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0236-notes_left-L008}{}{}{\Indent (6) Cod. 24 $\qfrac{1}{3}$.}
\end{minipage}
\hfill\vrule width.3pt\hfill\begin{minipage}[t]{.48\textwidth}\vspace{0pt}\fontsize{9}{11.5}\selectfont\setlength{\GutterOffset}{0pt}\setlength{\FullSourceWidth}{\linewidth}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0236-N07}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0236-notes_right-L001}{}{}{\Indent (7) Pro 780 codex verbis, non litteris, 700 ha-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0236-notes_right-L002}{}{}{bet; sed recte 780 litteris numeralibus superscri-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0236-notes_right-L003}{}{}{bitur.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0236-N08}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0236-notes_right-L004}{}{}{\Indent (8) Cod. 167° 32′.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0236-N09}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0236-notes_right-L005}{}{}{\Indent (9) Minuta in cod. 26′.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0236-N10}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0236-notes_right-L006}{}{}{\Indent (10) Cod., verbis, 3.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0236-N11}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0236-notes_right-L007}{}{}{\Indent (11) Pro 583 cod., verbis, 483.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0236-N12}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0236-notes_right-L008}{}{}{\Indent (12) Minuta in cod. desunt.}
\end{minipage}
\DSource{0237}{148}
\par\fontsize{14.5}{17}\selectfont
\par\vspace*{\dimexpr 9.74mm-\baselineskip\relax}\prevdepth=0pt
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0237-H01}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0237-body-L001}{}{p}{\CenterLine{\textbf{E.}}}
\par\fontsize{14.2}{16}\selectfont
\par\vspace{2.66mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0237-body-L002}{5}{}{Caput de cognoscendis per tabulas, quas in fine libri posuimus, tempo-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0237-body-L003}{}{}{\mbox{}\rlap{\hspace*{7.55mm}ribus revolutionum annorum, eorumque ascendentibus, atque de in-}}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0237-body-L004}{}{}{\mbox{}\rlap{\hspace*{7.55mm}cremento mediorum motuum planetarum in iis annis super pristinos}}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0237-body-L005}{}{}{\mbox{}\rlap{\hspace*{7.55mm}motus medios.}}
\par\fontsize{11.1}{13.8}\selectfont
\par\vspace{2.85mm}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0237-P01}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0237-body-L006}{10}{}{\Indent Dicit [auctor]: Si momenta et ascendentia revolutionum annorum, eorumque incremen-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0237-body-L007}{}{}{tum in motibus mediis super pristinos motus medios scire vis, accipe annos integros (1) qui}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0237-body-L008}{}{}{nativitati transierunt ab anno originis ad annum revolutionis; item locum medium et locum}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0237-body-L009}{}{}{verum (2) Solis in origine cognosce. Numerum hunc annorum in columna, qua anni Romani}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0237-body-L010}{}{}{[ab aera] collecti continentur, quaere (3); tempora ascensionalia ei, vel numero proxime in}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0237-body-L011}{15}{}{tabula inferiori respondentia sume. Reliquos annos in tabula annorum singulorum quaere; tem-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0237-body-L012}{}{}{pora ascensionalia iis respondentia sume atque temporibus antea inventis adde. De summa, si}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0237-body-L013}{}{}{360° excedit, circumferentiam [i. e. 360°] deme; residuum per 15 divide ut horas aequino-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0237-body-L014}{}{}{ctiales habeas, quas adde horis correctionis in origine. Si provenit summa 24 excedens, ab ea}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0237-body-L015}{}{}{24 deme, et diebus transactis mensis originis diem adde; si autem horarum summa est mi-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0237-body-L016}{20}{}{nor quam 24, eam serva. Anno bisextili exstante et mense subāṭ iam elapso, de diebus transa-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0237-body-L017}{}{}{ctis mensis deme diem; si contra annus non est bisextilis, nihil demi debet.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0237-P02}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0237-body-L018}{}{}{\Indent Postea Solem determina eo die illius anni; motus medius tunc erit ut pristinus motus}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0237-body-L019}{}{}{medius tempore originis; eum aequatione corrige iuxta locum apogei solaris anno revolutionis;}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0237-body-L020}{}{}{et si locus verus provenit ut primus, eum serva; si ab eo diversus, differentiam divide per}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0237-body-L021}{25}{}{motum horarium inaequalem Solis, et horas exeuntes adde horis correctionis, si secundus locus}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0237-body-L022}{}{}{est primo minor, aut ab iis deme, si maior. Horas correctionis ita repertas, in horas nychthe-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0237-body-L023}{}{}{merōn inaequalium ut nunc dicam converte: Aequationem dierum in [tabula] sphaerae rectae}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0237-body-L024}{}{}{descriptam (4) et longitudini Solis respondentem quaere; eam in horas aequinoctiales converte,}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0237-body-L025}{}{}{quas horis correctionis adde; proveniunt horae momenti revolutionis. Iis, per quindecim divisis}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0237-body-L026}{30}{}{adde ascensiones rectas gradus Solis; postea, ut soles, ascendens et medium Caelum cognosce.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0237-P03}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0237-body-L027}{}{}{\Indent Deinde quid numero annorum integrorum [tropicorum] respondeat in columnis motuum}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0237-body-L028}{}{p. 224.}{Lunae et planetarum (5) in annis revolutionis vide; incrementum Lunae, anomaliae Lunae et}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0237-body-L029}{}{}{nodi adde ipsorum motibus mediis originis tempore; incrementum trium planetarum superiorum}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0237-body-L030}{}{}{adde motibus mediis in origine, et deme de ipsorum anomalia pristina; incrementum Veneris}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0237-body-L031}{35}{}{et Mercurii adde anomaliae originis. Motus medius Veneris et Mercurii est ut motus medius}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0237-body-L032}{}{}{Solis. Si hoc feceris, ea, ut soles, corrige (6). Scias momenta revolutionum unum diem amit-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0237-body-L033}{}{}{tere centesimo sexto (7) quoque anno; quotiescumque igitur 106 anni praeterierint, de diebus}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0237-body-L034}{}{}{mensis originis elapsis semper, si Deus vult, diem demes. Hoc autem in nativitatibus non eve-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0237-body-L035}{}{}{nit, sed tantum in vitis civitatum, dynastiarum et gentium, quarum revolutiones, Deo volente,}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0237-body-L036}{40}{}{interdum opus est scire (\textbf{\textit{a}}).}
\par\vspace{6pt}\nointerlineskip\hbox to\textwidth{\hss\vrule width 115.43pt height.25pt depth0pt\hss}\prevdepth=-1000pt \vspace{5pt}
\noindent\begin{minipage}[t]{.48\textwidth}\vspace{0pt}\fontsize{9}{11.5}\selectfont\setlength{\GutterOffset}{0pt}\setlength{\FullSourceWidth}{\linewidth}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0237-N01}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0237-notes_left-L001}{}{}{\Indent (1) Intelliguntur anni tropici, quorum quisque,}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0237-notes_left-L002}{}{}{iuxta al-Battānī, 365\textsuperscript{\textit{d}} 5\textsuperscript{\textit{h}} 46\textsuperscript{\textit{m}} 24\textsuperscript{\textit{s}} complectitur.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0237-N02}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0237-notes_left-L003}{}{}{\Indent (2) Seu longitudinem mediam et longitudinem}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0237-notes_left-L004}{}{}{veram, tempore originis.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0237-N03}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0237-notes_left-L005}{}{}{\Indent (3) Vide tabulam fol. 240,r.}
\end{minipage}
\hfill\vrule width.3pt\hfill\begin{minipage}[t]{.48\textwidth}\vspace{0pt}\fontsize{9}{11.5}\selectfont\setlength{\GutterOffset}{0pt}\setlength{\FullSourceWidth}{\linewidth}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0237-N04}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0237-notes_right-L001}{}{}{\Indent (4) Fol. 179,v. et seqq.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0237-N05}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0237-notes_right-L002}{}{}{\Indent (5) Fol. 239,v.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0237-N06}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0237-notes_right-L003}{}{}{\Indent (6) I. e. loca vera determina.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0237-N07}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0237-notes_right-L004}{}{}{\Indent (7) Cod. hic et infra 166; sed recte 106 legi-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0237-notes_right-L005}{}{}{tur in cap. LIII, pag. 129.}
\end{minipage}
\DSource{0238}{149}
\par\fontsize{14.5}{17}\selectfont
\par\vspace*{\dimexpr 9.68mm-\baselineskip\relax}\prevdepth=0pt
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0238-H01}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0238-body-L001}{}{}{\CenterLine{\textbf{F.}}}
\par\fontsize{14.2}{16}\selectfont
\par\vspace{3.69mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0238-body-L002}{5}{}{Caput de dirigendis, modo veritati proximo, gradibus a quolibet loco}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0238-body-L003}{}{}{\mbox{}\rlap{\hspace*{7.55mm}ad alterum quemlibet; et scias hanc esse directionem quam Ptole-}}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0238-body-L004}{}{}{\mbox{}\rlap{\hspace*{7.55mm}maeus {\fontsize{11.1}{13.8}\selectfont (1)} in profectione hilegii explicat, ascensiones in ea \textit{ǵuwīrāst}}}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0238-body-L005}{}{}{\mbox{}\rlap{\hspace*{7.55mm}appellans; qua re dicit \textit{ǵuwīrāst} Capricorni i. e. ascensiones rectas,}}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0238-body-L006}{10}{}{\mbox{}\rlap{\hspace*{7.55mm}et \textit{ǵuwīrāst} regionis i. e. ascensiones loci [obliquas]. Si Deus Opti-}}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0238-body-L007}{}{}{\mbox{}\rlap{\hspace*{7.55mm}mus Maximus vult, [haec directio] est aequatio distantiae eorum}}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0238-body-L008}{}{}{\mbox{}\rlap{\hspace*{7.55mm}[graduum] a paxillis.}}
\par\fontsize{11.1}{13.8}\selectfont
\par\vspace{4.33mm}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0238-P01}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0238-body-L009}{}{}{\Indent Dicit [auctor]: Si directionem gradus cuiuslibet ad quemcumque locum scire vis, ascen-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0238-body-L010}{15}{}{siones rectas primi gradus a quo directionem incipere statuisti, deme de ascensionibus rectis}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0238-body-L011}{}{}{secundi gradus ad quem directionem pervenire iubes; quod remanet \textit{primum residuum} ap-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0238-body-L012}{}{}{pella. Postea ascensiones obliquas primi gradus de ascensionibus secundi in eodem climate deme;}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0238-body-L013}{}{}{quod remanet \textit{secundum residuum} voca. Residuorum differentiam inveni, et utrum ad pri-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0238-body-L014}{}{}{mum an ad secundum residuum pertineat cognosce; idque clare nota. Postea distantiam primi}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0238-body-L015}{20}{}{gradus a paxillo qui est ante eum sume; si paxillus hic est decimus [i. e. paxillus medii Caeli],}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0238-body-L016}{}{}{distantiam appella ab arcu semidiurno, eamque de residuorum differentia deme; quod remanet}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0238-body-L017}{}{}{\textit{proventum} voca. Si differentia residuorum ad primum residuum pertinet, proventum de primo}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0238-body-L018}{}{}{residuo deme; si ad secundum, eidem primo residuo proventum adde. Quod alterutra opera-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0238-body-L019}{}{p. 225.}{tione invenis est, si Deus vult, numerus graduum directionis.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0238-P02}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0238-body-L020}{25}{}{\Indent Si paxillus qui primum gradum praecedit est quartus [i. e. paxillus Terrae, \textgreek{ὑπόγειον}], di-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0238-body-L021}{}{}{stantiam primi gradus ab eo ab arcu seminocturno appella; eam de residuorum differentia}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0238-body-L022}{}{}{deme; quod remanet est \textit{proventum}. Hoc de primo residuo deme, si differentia ad primum}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0238-body-L023}{}{}{residuum pertinet; si ad secundum, eidem primo residuo proventum adde; exeunt gradus di-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0238-body-L024}{}{}{rectionis.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0238-P03}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0238-body-L025}{30}{}{\Indent Si paxillus qui primum gradum praecedit est ascendens, distantiam primi gradus ab eo}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0238-body-L026}{}{}{compara arcui seminocturno (2), et secundum hanc proportionem sume quantitatem ex diffe-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0238-body-L027}{}{}{rentia residuorum; quod erit appella « \textit{proventum} ». Si secundum residuum est primo maius,}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0238-body-L028}{}{}{proventum de secundo deme; si minus, secundo residuo adde proventum; habebis gradus di-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0238-body-L029}{}{}{rectionis.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0238-P04}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0238-body-L030}{35}{}{\Indent Demum si paxillus primum gradum praecedens est septimus [i. e. paxillus occidentis,}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0238-body-L031}{}{}{\textgreek{δύσις}], distantiam primi gradus ab eo compara dimidio (3) arcui diurno, et hac ratione quan-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0238-body-L032}{}{}{titatem sume ex differentia residuorum; habebis \textit{proventum}. Si secundum residuum est primo}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0238-body-L033}{}{}{maius, de secundo proventum deme; si minus, ei proventum adde; exibit numerus graduum}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0238-body-L034}{}{}{directionis, qui sunt gradus aequati, commixti ex ascensionibus rectis et ascensionibus obliquis (\textbf{\textit{a}}).}
\par\vspace{6pt}\nointerlineskip\hbox to\textwidth{\hss\vrule width 115.43pt height.25pt depth0pt\hss}\prevdepth=-1000pt \vspace{5pt}
\noindent\begin{minipage}[t]{.48\textwidth}\vspace{0pt}\fontsize{9}{11.5}\selectfont\setlength{\GutterOffset}{0pt}\setlength{\FullSourceWidth}{\linewidth}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0238-N01}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0238-notes_left-L001}{}{}{\Indent (1) Ut videtur, in \textit{Tetrabiblo} sive \textit{Quadripar-}}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0238-notes_left-L002}{}{}{\textit{tito} III, 10 (III, 10-14 versionis Latinae Francofurti}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0238-notes_left-L003}{}{}{anno 1622 impressae).}
\end{minipage}
\hfill\vrule width.3pt\hfill\begin{minipage}[t]{.48\textwidth}\vspace{0pt}\fontsize{9}{11.5}\selectfont\setlength{\GutterOffset}{0pt}\setlength{\FullSourceWidth}{\linewidth}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0238-N02}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0238-notes_right-L001}{}{}{\Indent (2) Cod. « duplo arcui nocturno ».}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0238-N03}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0238-notes_right-L002}{}{}{\Indent (3) Cod. « duplo ».}
\end{minipage}
\DSource{0239}{150}
\par\fontsize{14.5}{17}\selectfont
\par\vspace*{\dimexpr 9.68mm-\baselineskip\relax}\prevdepth=0pt
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0239-H01}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0239-body-L001}{}{}{\CenterLine{\textbf{G.}}}
\par\fontsize{14.2}{16}\selectfont
\par\vspace{2.70mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0239-body-L002}{5}{}{Caput de cognoscendis ascensionibus rectis decadum graduum eclipticae,}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0239-body-L003}{}{}{\mbox{}\rlap{\hspace*{7.55mm}et de sinibus harum ascensionum, ut qualibet regione ascensiones}}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0239-body-L004}{}{}{\mbox{}\rlap{\hspace*{7.55mm}signorum facile deprehendantur.}}
\par\fontsize{11.1}{13.8}\selectfont
\par\vspace{3.53mm}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0239-P01}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0239-body-L005}{}{}{\Indent Ascensiones rectas decimi cuiusque gradus, cum praecedentibus in unum collecti, usque}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0239-body-L006}{10}{}{ad finem 90° descripsimus, et apud eas notavimus sinus ascensionum (1). Cum ascensiones co-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0239-body-L007}{}{}{gnoscere velis, ut in parte anteriore libri [cap. XIII] ostendimus operare: sinum dimidii in-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0239-body-L008}{}{}{crementi diei longissimi in ea urbe cognosce, eumque multiplica in sinus harum decadum}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0239-body-L009}{}{p. 226.}{apud ipsas descriptos; productum in arcum converte; arcus erit argumentum diversitatis diei}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0239-body-L010}{}{}{quod ad decades collectas pertinet. — Id in tabula posuimus ad omnia signa cum intervallo}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0239-body-L011}{15}{}{decem graduum; nam hoc sufficit ad necessariam cognitionem ascensionum in ea urbe et in}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0239-body-L012}{}{}{regionibus eiusdem latitudinis. Hoc deprehenditur e parva tabella posita sub quadrato ordinum}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0239-body-L013}{}{}{declinationis signorum.}
\par\fontsize{14.5}{17}\selectfont
\par\vspace{8.51mm}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0239-H14}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0239-body-L014}{20}{}{\CenterLine{\textbf{H.}}}
\par\fontsize{14.2}{16}\selectfont
\par\vspace{3.99mm}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0239-H15}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0239-body-L015}{}{}{\CenterLine{Caput [de motu synodico Lunae].}}
\par\fontsize{11.1}{13.8}\selectfont
\par\vspace{3.94mm}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0239-P02}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0239-body-L016}{}{}{\Indent Si vis motum horarium inaequalem Lunae ad supputandas syzygias cognoscere, praeci-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0239-body-L017}{25}{}{sam (2) differentiam [longitudinis] inter Solem et Lunam accipe, et inveni quod ei respondet ex}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0239-body-L018}{}{}{motu horario Lunae in columnis incrementi et decrementi (3); ipsum ad singulos gradus des-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0239-body-L019}{}{}{cribitur. Minuta de motu horario inaequali Lunae deme, si anomalia aequata Lunae, cuius}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0239-body-L020}{}{}{iter horarium cognovisti, a 0° ad 95° vel a 265° ad 360° est; sed minuta adde motui illi, cum}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0239-body-L021}{}{}{anomalia aequata a 95° ad 265° sit. Exibit iter definitum, de quo motum horarium Solis deme;}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0239-body-L022}{30}{}{remanet praecessio Lunae [respectu Solis] (4) (\textbf{\textit{a}}).}
\par\vspace{6pt}\nointerlineskip\hbox to\textwidth{\hss\vrule width 115.43pt height.25pt depth0pt\hss}\prevdepth=-1000pt \vspace{5pt}
\noindent\begin{minipage}[t]{.48\textwidth}\vspace{0pt}\fontsize{9}{11.5}\selectfont\setlength{\GutterOffset}{0pt}\setlength{\FullSourceWidth}{\linewidth}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0239-N01}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0239-notes_left-L001}{}{}{\Indent (1) Fol. 178,r.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0239-N02}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0239-notes_left-L002}{}{}{\Indent (2) Cfr. pag. 110, adn. 7.}
\end{minipage}
\hfill\vrule width.3pt\hfill\begin{minipage}[t]{.48\textwidth}\vspace{0pt}\fontsize{9}{11.5}\selectfont\setlength{\GutterOffset}{0pt}\setlength{\FullSourceWidth}{\linewidth}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0239-N03}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0239-notes_right-L001}{}{}{\Indent (3) Fol. 194,v.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0239-N04}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0239-notes_right-L002}{}{}{\Indent (4) I. e. motus synodicus Lunae.}
\end{minipage}
\par\vspace{28.33mm}\noindent\makebox[\linewidth][c]{\input{figures/AB01-PDF0239-F01}}
\DSource{0240}{151}

\par\vspace{8.43mm}
\par\noindent\makebox[\linewidth][c]{\rule[.5ex]{18mm}{.4pt}}\par

\par\noindent\rule{\linewidth}{1.2pt}\par\nointerlineskip\vspace{1pt}\noindent\rule{\linewidth}{.4pt}\par
\par\vspace{42.32mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0242-body-L001}{}{}{\CenterLine{\fontsize{24}{28}\selectfont ADNOTATIONES}}
\par\vspace{6.19mm}
\par\noindent\makebox[\linewidth][c]{\rule[.5ex]{18mm}{.4pt}}\par
\par\vspace{20.19mm}
\par\fontsize{15}{17}\selectfont
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0242-H02}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0242-body-L002}{}{}{\CenterLine{A\textsc{d} \textsc{caput} III.}}
\par\fontsize{11.1}{13.8}\selectfont
\par\vspace{3.83mm}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0242-H03}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0242-body-L003}{}{}{\CenterLine{\textbf{\textit{a}}) — \textit{Pag. 9–10.}}}
\par\vspace{4.3mm}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0242-P01}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0242-body-L004}{}{}{\Indent Propositiones omnes quas auctor de \textit{chordis} habet, in \textit{Almagesto} I, 9 (ed. Halma t. I,}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0242-body-L005}{}{}{p. 26–37) demonstrantur. Si littera R semidiametrum indicemus, nostra scriptura algebrica}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0242-body-L006}{}{}{hoc modo exprimuntur:}
\par\vspace{-0.54mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0242-body-L007}{}{}{\mbox{}\rlap{\hspace*{0.00mm}\textbf{(\textgreek{α})}}\rlap{\hspace*{14.26mm}chorda $\dfrac{\text{360°}}{\text{6}}$}\rlap{\hspace*{34.92mm}seu chorda 60° $= R$}}
\par\vspace{0.83mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0242-body-L008}{}{}{\mbox{}\rlap{\hspace*{0.00mm}\textbf{(\textgreek{β})}}\rlap{\hspace*{14.26mm}chorda $\dfrac{\text{360°}}{\text{3}}$}\rlap{\hspace*{34.92mm}seu chorda 120° $= \sqrt{4\,R^2 - \text{chorda}^2\,\dfrac{\text{360°}}{\text{6}}}$}}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0242-body-L009}{}{}{\mbox{}\rlap{\hspace*{0.00mm}\textbf{(\textgreek{γ})}}\rlap{\hspace*{14.26mm}chorda $(\text{180°} - a) = \sqrt{4\,R^2 - \text{chorda}^2\,a}$}}
\par\vspace{0.26mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0242-body-L010}{}{}{\mbox{}\rlap{\hspace*{0.00mm}\textbf{(\textgreek{δ})}}\rlap{\hspace*{14.26mm}chorda $\dfrac{\text{360°}}{\text{4}}$}\rlap{\hspace*{34.92mm}seu chorda 90° $= \sqrt{2\,R^2}$}}
\par\vspace{0.70mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0242-body-L011}{}{}{\mbox{}\rlap{\hspace*{0.00mm}\textbf{(\textgreek{ε})}}\rlap{\hspace*{14.26mm}chorda $\dfrac{\text{360°}}{\text{10}}$}\rlap{\hspace*{34.92mm}seu chorda 36° $= \sqrt{R^2 + \dfrac{2\,R}{4}} - \dfrac{2\,R}{4}$}}
\par\vspace{0.41mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0242-body-L012}{}{}{\mbox{}\rlap{\hspace*{0.00mm}\textbf{(\textgreek{ζ})}}\rlap{\hspace*{14.26mm}chorda $\dfrac{\text{360°}}{\text{5}}$}\rlap{\hspace*{34.92mm}seu chorda 72° $= \sqrt{\text{chorda}^2\,\dfrac{\text{360°}}{\text{10}} + R^2}$}}
\par\vspace{0.05mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0242-body-L013}{}{}{\mbox{}\rlap{\hspace*{0.00mm}\textbf{(\textgreek{η})}}\rlap{\hspace*{14.26mm}chorda $(a - b) = \dfrac{\text{chorda } a \text{ chorda } (\text{180°} - b) - \text{chorda } b \text{ chorda } (\text{180°} - a)}{2\,R}$}}
\par\vspace{0.60mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0242-body-L014}{}{}{\mbox{}\rlap{\hspace*{0.00mm}\textbf{(\textgreek{ϑ})}}\rlap{\hspace*{14.26mm}chorda $\dfrac{a}{2} = \sqrt{\dfrac{2\,R - \text{chorda } (\text{180°} - a)}{2} \times 2\,R}$}}
\par\vspace{1.15mm}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0242-P02}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0242-body-L015}{}{}{\Indent Ad inveniendam chordam summae duorum arcuum, \Name{Ptolemaeus} (ed. Halma, t. I.}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0242-body-L016}{}{}{p. 32–33), quem noster sequitur, aliquid simile regulae (\textgreek{η}) non habet, sed per chordam sup-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0242-body-L017}{}{}{plementi transit (1); ergo}
\par\vspace{1.99mm}
\par\vspace{6pt}\nointerlineskip\hbox to\textwidth{\hss\vrule width 115.43pt height.25pt depth0pt\hss}\prevdepth=-1000pt \vspace{5pt}
\noindent\begin{minipage}[t]{.48\textwidth}\vspace{0pt}\fontsize{9}{11.5}\selectfont\setlength{\GutterOffset}{0pt}\setlength{\FullSourceWidth}{\linewidth}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0242-N01}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0242-notes_left-L001}{}{}{\Indent (1) Errat \Name{Delambre}, \textit{Astron. ancienne}, t. II,}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0242-notes_left-L002}{}{}{p. 39 (ut me \Name{Schiaparelli} monet), qui \Name{Ptole-}}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0242-notes_left-L003}{}{}{\Name{maeo} regulam tribuit:}
\end{minipage}
\hfill\vrule width.3pt\hfill\begin{minipage}[t]{.48\textwidth}\vspace{0pt}\fontsize{9}{11.5}\selectfont\setlength{\GutterOffset}{0pt}\setlength{\FullSourceWidth}{\linewidth}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0242-H01}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0242-notes_right-L001}{}{}{\CenterLine{crd $(p + q) =$}}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0242-H02}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0242-notes_right-L002}{}{}{\CenterLine{$= \dfrac{\text{crd } p \text{ crd } (\text{180°} - q) - \text{crd } q \text{ crd } (\text{180°} - p)}{2\,R}$.}}
\end{minipage}
\par\vspace{5mm}\hfill{\fontsize{8}{10}\selectfont 20}\hspace{10mm}
\DSource{0243}{154}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0243-body-L001}{}{}{\mbox{}\rlap{\hspace*{0.00mm}(\textgreek{ι})}\rlap{\hspace*{11.28mm}chorda $(\text{180°} - [a + b]) = \dfrac{\text{chorda } a \text{ chorda } b - \text{chorda } (\text{180°} - a) \text{ chorda } (\text{180°} - b)}{2\,R}$}}
\par\vspace{-0.19mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0243-body-L002}{}{}{\mbox{}\rlap{\hspace*{0.00mm}(\textgreek{κ})}\rlap{\hspace*{11.28mm}chorda $(a + b) = \sqrt{4R^2 - \text{chorda}^2\,(\text{180°} - [a + b])}$}}
\par\vspace{4.27mm}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0243-P01}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0243-body-L003}{}{}{\Indent Viam qua Ptolemaeus ad calculum chordae 1° pervenit omnes fere historiae mathesis}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0243-body-L004}{}{}{scriptores exponunt; e recentissimis consulantur M. \Name{Cantor}, \textit{Vorlesungen über Geschichte der}}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0243-body-L005}{}{}{\textit{Mathematik,} Leipzig 1880 sqq., t. I, pag. 353–355 (vel 390–392 alterius editionis anni 1894);}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0243-body-L006}{}{}{R. \Name{Wolf}, \textit{Handbuch der Astronomie, ihrer Geschichte und Litteratur,} Zürich 1890–93,}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0243-body-L007}{}{}{t. I, p. 164–165, § 61,\textit{b}; A. \Name{von Braunmühl}, \textit{Vorlesungen über Geschichte der Trigo-}}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0243-body-L008}{}{}{\textit{nometrie,} Leipzig 1900 sqq., t. I, p. 21. Error, quem F. \Name{Woepcke}, \textit{Discussion de deux}}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0243-body-L009}{}{}{\textit{méthodes arabes pour déterminer une valeur rapprochée de sin 1°} (Journal de Mathé-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0243-body-L010}{}{}{matiques pures et appliquées publié par J. Liouville, t. XIX, 1854, p. 165) per Lagrangii inter-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0243-body-L011}{}{}{polationis formulam investigavit, est 0,000\,000\,09, scilicet minorem quam $\qfrac{1}{10\,000\,000}$; sensibilis}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0243-body-L012}{}{}{igitur in tertiis tantum fit, quas Ptolemaeus in tabula chordarum omittit. — Quantitatem sinus}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0243-body-L013}{}{}{1° veritati propiorem, \Name{Abū ’l–Wefā’} (940–998 Chr.) invenit, quae in quintis tantum, seu in}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0243-body-L014}{}{}{nona figura decimali, errorem praebet (sin 30′ = 0\textsuperscript{\textit{p}} 31′ 24″ 55‴ 54\textsuperscript{IV} 55\textsuperscript{V} pro 54\textsuperscript{V} 47\textsuperscript{VI} 16\textsuperscript{VII});}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0243-body-L015}{}{}{cfr. \Name{Woepcke}, \textit{Recherches sur l’histoire des sciences mathématiques chez les Orientaux,}}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0243-body-L016}{}{}{in Journal Asiatique, V série, t. XV, 1860, p. 286–303; \Name{Cantor}, I, 641–642 (= 703 alterius}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0243-body-L017}{}{}{editionis); \Name{von Braunmühl}, I, 56–57. — Iuxta ea quae nobis tradidit \Name{Mīram Čelebī} (ob.}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0243-body-L018}{}{}{anno 931 heg. = 1524–25 Chr.) in commentario suo ad Ulugh Beg (in versione Gallica \Name{Sé-}}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0243-body-L019}{}{}{\Name{dillot}, p. 69–77, adnot.), alii iique veteres Arabes, ignoti nominis, methodum Ptolemaicam}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0243-body-L020}{}{}{absolvendo, quantitatem sinus 1° invenerunt quae veritatem excedit fractione minore quam}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0243-body-L021}{}{}{$\qfrac{1}{100\,000\,000}$; error enim est $\qfrac{37}{416}$ (vel fere $\qfrac{1}{11}$) erroris Ptolemaei, cum sin 1° = 1\textsuperscript{\textit{p}} 2′ 49″ 43‴}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0243-body-L022}{}{}{13\textsuperscript{IV} 5\textsuperscript{V} 30\textsuperscript{VI} pro 11\textsuperscript{IV} 14\textsuperscript{V} 44\textsuperscript{VI} faciant. Sed nisi de industria in calculis parvas fractiones quas-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0243-body-L023}{}{}{dam neglexissent, potuissent numerum longe meliorem invenire, cuius error fuisset $\qfrac{1}{64}$ erroris}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0243-body-L024}{}{}{Ptolemaici. Cfr. \Name{Woepcke}, \textit{Discussion de deux méthodes} etc., p. 155–157, 159–167. — Ele-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0243-body-L025}{}{}{gantissime demum, aequationem trisectionis anguli proxime solvendo, id est aequationem tertii}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0243-body-L026}{}{}{gradus in seriem evolvendo, sin 1° = 1\textsuperscript{\textit{p}} 2′ 49″ 43‴ 11\textsuperscript{IV} recte supputavit \Name{Ghiyāth ad–dīn} (1)}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0243-body-L027}{}{}{\Name{Ǵamshīd al–Kāshī}, cui probabiliter alii Arabes in hoc praeiverunt; eius methodum, nobis}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0243-body-L028}{}{}{a \Name{Mīram Čelebī}, l. c. pag. 77–83, traditam, exposuit \Name{Woepcke}, \textit{Discussion}, p. 167–176,}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0243-body-L029}{}{}{301–303. Cfr. quoque H. \Name{Hankel}, \textit{Zur Geschichte der Mathematik im Alterthum und}}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0243-body-L030}{}{}{\textit{Mittelalter,} Leipzig 1874, p. 290–293; \Name{Cantor}, I, 671-672 (= 737 alt. edit.); \Name{von Braun-}}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0243-body-L031}{}{}{\Name{mühl}, I, 73–75.}
\par\vspace{5.65mm}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0243-H32}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0243-body-L032}{}{}{\CenterLine{\textbf{\textit{b}}) — \textit{Pag. 10–11.}}}
\par\vspace{4.46mm}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0243-P02}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0243-body-L033}{}{}{\Indent Vocem technicam \textit{ǵayb}, qua Arabes sinum indicant, al–Battānī nunquam memorat, licet}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0243-body-L034}{}{}{iam ineunte IX saeculo Christi pervulgata esset, ut ex inedita versione Latina operis astro-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0243-body-L035}{}{}{nomici \Name{Muḥammad ibn Mūsà al–Khuwārizmī}, ab \Name{Adelardo Bathoniensi} saeculo XII}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0243-body-L036}{}{}{confecta, patet. Ibi enim nomen Arabicum \textit{elgeib} (praemisso scilicet articulo Arabico \textit{al} sive}
\par\vspace{4.96mm}
\par\vspace{6pt}\nointerlineskip\hbox to\textwidth{\hss\vrule width 115.43pt height.25pt depth0pt\hss}\prevdepth=-1000pt \vspace{5pt}
\noindent\begin{minipage}[t]{.48\textwidth}\vspace{0pt}\fontsize{9}{11.5}\selectfont\setlength{\GutterOffset}{0pt}\setlength{\FullSourceWidth}{\linewidth}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0243-N01}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0243-notes_left-L001}{}{}{\Indent (1) Male quidem ‘Atāb ad-dīn efferunt, \ArabicRun{عتاب}}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0243-notes_left-L002}{}{}{‘Atāb pro \ArabicRun{غياث} Ghiyāth legentes. Anno circiter 840}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0243-notes_left-L003}{}{}{heg., 1436-37 Chr., obiit, ut recte H. \Name{Suter}, \textit{Die}}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0243-notes_left-L004}{}{}{\textit{Mathematiker und Astronomen der Araber}, Leip-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0243-notes_left-L005}{}{}{zig 1900, p. 173-4, nr. 429, statuit; cuius argumentis}
\end{minipage}
\hfill\vrule width.3pt\hfill\begin{minipage}[t]{.48\textwidth}\vspace{0pt}\fontsize{9}{11.5}\selectfont\setlength{\GutterOffset}{0pt}\setlength{\FullSourceWidth}{\linewidth}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0243-notes_right-L001}{}{}{addatur quod opusculum de inveniendo sinu 1° (in}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0243-notes_right-L002}{}{}{Bibliotheca Kahirensi adhuc exstans) iam a Qāḍīzā-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0243-notes_right-L003}{}{}{deh ar-Rūmī, intra annos 840-850 heg. mortuo, lau-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0243-notes_right-L004}{}{}{dibus extollitur, ut Mīram Čelebī narrat.}
\end{minipage}
\DSource{0244}{155}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0244-body-L001}{}{}{\textit{el}) servatur, et memorantur « \textit{elgeib elmustewi} seu planum » [\textit{al–ǵayb al–mustawī}] i. e.}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0244-body-L002}{}{}{sinus rectus atque « \textit{elgeib elmacus} seu diminutum » (1) i. e. sinus versus. Item \Name{Ḥabash},}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0244-body-L003}{}{}{al–Battānī aequalis, semper voce \textit{ǵayb} utitur. Sed noster vocabulum, peregrinae originis et}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0244-body-L004}{}{}{a Graecorum usu prorsus alienum, recipere noluit. Errant igitur haud pauci iidemque optimi}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0244-body-L005}{}{}{scriptores historiae mathesis qui usum vocis \textit{ǵayb} auctori nostro tribuunt, et vocabulum \textit{sinum}}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0244-body-L006}{}{}{Platonem Tiburtinum Europae attulisse versionibus suis putant; e recentioribus adducam tan-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0244-body-L007}{}{}{tum \Name{Hankel}, p. 280; \Name{Wolf}, \textit{Handb. der Astron.}, I, 166, § 62,\textit{a}; \Name{Cantor}, I, 632 (= 693}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0244-body-L008}{}{}{alterius edit.), II, 101 et 249. Errorem tamen iam vetus \Name{Kaestner}, \textit{Geschichte der Ma-}}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0244-body-L009}{}{}{\textit{thematik,} Göttingen 1796, t. I, p. 522, correxerat; conferantur nunc M. \Name{Koppe}, \textit{Die Behand-}}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0244-body-L010}{}{}{\textit{lung der Logarithmen und der Sinus,} Programm des Andreas–Realgymnasium, Berlin 1893,}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0244-body-L011}{}{}{p. 32; \Name{von Braunmühl} I, 50. — Semel tantum in versione Platonis Tiburtini vox \textit{sinus}}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0244-body-L012}{}{}{reperitur: « si autem per has cordas [sic] versas arcus scire desideras, si chorda quam ha-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0244-body-L013}{}{}{« bueris minus 60 fuerit, eam de 60 minue, et residui arcum scito, quem de 90 minues, et}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0244-body-L014}{}{}{« quod remanserit, erit arcus \textit{sinus} versi » (fol. 7,v. editionis Norimbergensis, qui locus respon-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0244-body-L015}{}{}{det pag. 11, l. 28–30 versionis meae). Sed, ut recte \Name{von Braunmühl}, I, 50, adn. 3, censet,}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0244-body-L016}{}{}{vox est glossa Regiomontani habenda, quae in textum perperam irrepserit.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0244-P01}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0244-body-L017}{}{}{\Indent Cum in calculis trigonometricis nunquam fere chordae adhibeantur, haud mirum est al–}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0244-body-L018}{}{}{–Battānī chordarum nomine dimidias chordas duplorum arcuum designavisse; similis usus iam}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0244-body-L019}{}{}{IV Chr. saeculo apud Indos invenitur, de quibus haec in adnotationibus \Name{Burgess} et \Name{Whitney}}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0244-body-L020}{}{}{ad librum Sūrya–Siddhānta inscriptum leguntur: « In this passage, the sine is called \textit{jyārdha},}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0244-body-L021}{}{}{« ‘ half–chord ’; hereafter, however, that term does not once occur, but \textit{jyā} ‘ chord ’ (lite-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0244-body-L022}{}{}{« rally ‘ bowstring ’) is itself employed, as are also its synonims \textit{jīvā}, \textit{māurvikā}, to denote}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0244-body-L023}{}{}{« the sine. The usage of Albategnius is the same » (\textit{Translation of the Sūrya–Siddhānta,}}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0244-body-L024}{}{}{\textit{a text-book of Hindu astronomy, by} E. \Name{Burgess}, adn. ad cap. II, vers. 28, in Journal}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0244-body-L025}{}{}{of the American Oriental Society, t. VI, New Haven 1860, p. 201). — Nec praetermittendum}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0244-body-L026}{}{}{est \textit{chordae} integrae nomen apud Arabes originem ab usu Indorum trahere, ut e loco nuper}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0244-body-L027}{}{}{adducto patet; apud Graecos chorda appellabatur \textgreek{ὑποτείνουσα} « subtensa », \textgreek{ἡ ἐν τῷ κύκλῳ εὐ}-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0244-body-L028}{}{}{\textgreek{θεῖα} « recta in circulo », \textgreek{εὐθεῖα ἐν κύκλῳ μὴ διὰ τοῦ κέντρου οὖσα} « recta in circulo per cen-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0244-body-L029}{}{}{« trum non transiens », \textgreek{εὐθεῖα ἐλάττων τοῦ διαμέτρου} « recta minor diametro ».}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0244-P02}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0244-body-L030}{}{}{\Indent Vocis technicae \textit{ǵayb} originem satis explanavit \Name{Munk}, quem secuti sunt F. \Name{Woepcke},}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0244-body-L031}{}{}{\textit{Essai sur la propagation des chiffres indiens,} in Journal Asiatique, ser. VI, t. I, 1863,}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0244-body-L032}{}{}{p. 478, adn., et omnes fere recentiores. E voce Indica \textit{jīvā} = chorda et sinu, quam parum}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0244-body-L033}{}{}{supra memoravi, Arabes, littera \textit{v} carentes, \textit{ǵīb} \ArabicRun{جيب} fecerunt; Sanskritica \textit{j} enim ut Arabica}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0244-body-L034}{}{}{\textit{ǵ} pronuntiatur. Sed cum \textit{ǵīb} Arabice nullum habeat sensum, signa autem \ArabicRun{جيب} absque scri-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0244-body-L035}{}{}{pturae mutationibus possint \textit{ǵayb} quoque legi (nam in communi scriptura parvae notae quae}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0244-body-L036}{}{}{veram vocalium pronuntiationem indicant semper fere omittuntur), Arabes hanc nominis for-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0244-body-L037}{}{}{mam maluerunt, quae eorum lingua non solum « caesuram rotundam vestis ex qua collum et}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0244-body-L038}{}{}{caput hominis exeunt », sed etiam « sinum vel plicaturam in veste factam, ad parvas res secum}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0244-body-L039}{}{}{ferendas », indicat; hominis sinum ipsum interdum quoque significat, sed raro. \Name{Adelardum}}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0244-body-L040}{}{}{\Name{Bathoniensem} iam vidimus in versionibus suis nomen Arabicum \textit{elgeib} servasse; sed eius}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0244-body-L041}{}{}{aequalis \Name{Gerardus Cremonensis} (1114–1187 Chr.), in astronomia a Ǵābir ibn Aflaḥ con-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0244-body-L042}{}{}{scripta vertenda, voce \textit{sinu} usus est, quae hac via nostris linguis est allata, ut recte \Name{Koppe},}
\par\vspace{3.28mm}
\par\vspace{6pt}\nointerlineskip\hbox to\textwidth{\hss\vrule width 115.43pt height.25pt depth0pt\hss}\prevdepth=-1000pt \vspace{5pt}
\noindent\begin{minipage}[t]{.48\textwidth}\vspace{0pt}\fontsize{9}{11.5}\selectfont\setlength{\GutterOffset}{0pt}\setlength{\FullSourceWidth}{\linewidth}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0244-N01}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0244-notes_left-L001}{}{}{\Indent (1) Adelardi error; \textit{ma‘kūs} = versus, inver-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0244-notes_left-L002}{}{}{sus. Locum adduxit \Name{Chasles}, \textit{Recherches sur l’as-}}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0244-notes_left-L003}{}{}{\textit{tronomie ancienne}, in Comptes-rendus de l’Acadé-}
\end{minipage}
\hfill\vrule width.3pt\hfill\begin{minipage}[t]{.48\textwidth}\vspace{0pt}\fontsize{9}{11.5}\selectfont\setlength{\GutterOffset}{0pt}\setlength{\FullSourceWidth}{\linewidth}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0244-notes_right-L001}{}{}{mie des Sciences, t. XXIII, séance du 2 nov. 1846,}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0244-notes_right-L002}{}{}{pag. 850.}
\end{minipage}
\DSource{0245}{156}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0245-body-L001}{}{}{l. c., animadvertit (1). J. \Name{Ruska}, \textit{Zur Geschichte des} « \textit{Sinus} » (in Zeitschrift für Mathema-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0245-body-L002}{}{}{tik und Physik, t. XL, Leipzig 1895, historisch–literarische Abtheilung, p. 128) duos scriptores}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0245-body-L003}{}{}{Syros saeculi XIII Chr. (2) adducit, apud quos vox technica \textit{ǵayb} semper Syriaca \textit{‘ūbā} \textsyriac{ܥܘܒ݁ܐ}}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0245-body-L004}{}{}{vertitur, sinum hominis significante; sed errat cum scribit: « Die Uebersetzung durch \textit{sinus}}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0245-body-L005}{}{}{« ist demnach als richtig durch die syrische Belege gesichert. Die verständigere Uebersetzung}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0245-body-L006}{}{}{« aber, welche einen Mann voraussetzt, dem der mathematische Inhalt und dessen klarer Aus-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0245-body-L007}{}{}{« druck wichtiger ist als pedantische Wörtlichkeit, lautet natürlich \textit{chorda} . . . . . Beide Ueber-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0245-body-L008}{}{}{« setzungen finden sich vor: die zweite, wie \Name{Koppe} nachgewiesen hat, bei \Name{Plato von Ti-}}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0245-body-L009}{}{}{« \Name{voli} ». Plato Tiburtinus enim \textit{chordam} scribit non ob rationalioris versionis studium (quod}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0245-body-L010}{}{}{quantum ab eo ceterisque illius aetatis interpretibus alienum fuerit, satis eorum versiones}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0245-body-L011}{}{}{ubique ostendunt), sed quia hac ipsa voce \Name{al-Battānī} utitur.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0245-P01}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0245-body-L012}{}{}{\Indent Vocabula, Graecis ignota, chordam, sinum, sinum rectum (Sanskritice \textit{kramajyā}), sinum}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0245-body-L013}{}{}{versum (Sanskr. \textit{utkramajyā}), sagittam (Sanskr. \textit{śara} vel \textit{ishu}) ab Indis mathematicos Arabes}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0245-body-L014}{}{}{didicisse vix dubitari potest. Addantur quae Arabum scriptores de libro Indico \textit{as–Sindhind}}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0245-body-L015}{}{}{inscripto (i. e. Sanskr. \textit{Brahma–sphuṭa–siddhānta}) et anno 156 heg., 773 Chr., khalifae}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0245-body-L016}{}{}{Baghdādensi allato, narrant. His omnibus perpensis, historia functionum trigonometricarum se-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0245-body-L017}{}{}{quenti modo repraesentari potest. Indi, praesertim usu proiectionum orthogonalium moti (\Name{von}}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0245-body-L018}{}{}{\Name{Braunmühl}, I, 31–32), pro chordis Graecorum sinus rectos et versos adhibuerunt; eos pro}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0245-body-L019}{}{}{vicesimis quartis partibus quadrantis, scilicet pro singulis 3° 45′, calculaverunt, radio 3438}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0245-body-L020}{}{}{minutorum sumpto. Prima sinuum notione ab Indis recepta, et eodem fere tempore Ptolemaei}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0245-body-L021}{}{}{libris cognitis, recte Arabes intellexerunt commodius et exactius esse novos sinus construere,}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0245-body-L022}{}{}{chordas Ptolemaei (pro singulis dimidiis gradibus et radio sexaginta partium calculatas) dimi-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0245-body-L023}{}{}{diando. Quod iam ante al–Battānī fecerunt.}
\par\vspace{2.32mm}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0245-H24}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0245-body-L024}{}{}{\CenterLine{\textbf{\textit{c}}) — \textit{Pag. 11.}}}
\par\vspace{1.85mm}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0245-P02}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0245-body-L025}{}{}{\Indent \textit{Interpolatio} ad inveniendos sinus arcuum qui inter duos se subsequentes tabulae arcus}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0245-body-L026}{}{}{comprehenduntur, eadem est ac quae apud \Name{Ptolemaeum} legitur ad supputandas chordas;}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0245-body-L027}{}{}{variatio sinuum et chordarum censetur proportionalis esse ad variationem arcuum intra inter-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0245-body-L028}{}{}{vallum tabulare dimidii gradus. Quae \Name{Cantor}, I, 355 (= 392 alterius edit.) de Ptolemaeo}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0245-body-L029}{}{}{scribit, « ein Beweis für diese angenommene Proportionalität in engem Bereiche ist dagegen}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0245-body-L030}{}{}{« nicht vorhanden », recte possunt quoque de \Name{al–Battānī}, \Name{Ḥabash} (fol. 18,v.) ceterisque}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0245-body-L031}{}{}{Arabibus dici. Interpolationes exactiores in construendis tabulis tantum adhibebant. — Tabu-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0245-body-L032}{}{}{lae sinuum ad singula minuta quadrantis ab \Name{Ibn Yūnus} supputatae exstant in codice Ara-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0245-body-L033}{}{}{bico 5752 Bibliothecae Regiae Berolinensis; alias quoque \Name{Ulugh Beg} calculavit.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0245-P03}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0245-body-L034}{}{}{\Indent \textit{Sinus versos,} quorum usum \Name{Indi} iam ante Arabes cognoverunt, auctor definit:}
\par\vspace{1.08mm}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0245-H35}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0245-body-L035}{}{}{\CenterLine{sin vers $a = R - \cos a$.}}
\par\vspace{1.14mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0245-body-L036}{}{}{Supervacaneum duxit eorum tabulam construere, quam contra apud \Name{Ḥabash} invenimus. In}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0245-body-L037}{}{}{fine paginae duae formulae leguntur; altera est}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0245-body-L038}{}{}{\mbox{}\rlap{\hspace*{0.00mm}(\textgreek{α})}\rlap{\hspace*{98.80mm}\makebox[0pt][c]{$\cos a = \sqrt{R^2 - \sin^2 a}$,}}}
\par\vspace{0.62mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0245-body-L039}{}{}{quae e Pythagorae theoremate consequitur, et, posito $R = 1$, respondet tritae regulae nostrae}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0245-body-L040}{}{}{$\cos a = \sqrt{1 - \sin^2 a}$.}
\par\vspace{-1.40mm}
\par\vspace{6pt}\nointerlineskip\hbox to\textwidth{\hss\vrule width 115.43pt height.25pt depth0pt\hss}\prevdepth=-1000pt \vspace{5pt}
\noindent\begin{minipage}[t]{.48\textwidth}\vspace{0pt}\fontsize{9}{11.5}\selectfont\setlength{\GutterOffset}{0pt}\setlength{\FullSourceWidth}{\linewidth}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0245-N01}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0245-notes_left-L001}{}{}{\Indent (1) Sed ex eo quod non Plato Tiburtinus, sed}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0245-notes_left-L002}{}{}{Gerardus Cremonensis usum huius vocis apud nos}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0245-notes_left-L003}{}{}{vulgavit, nescio quo modo \Name{Koppe} in dubium vocare}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0245-notes_left-L004}{}{}{possit originem Arabici \textit{ǵayb} a Munk explanatam.}
\end{minipage}
\hfill\vrule width.3pt\hfill\begin{minipage}[t]{.48\textwidth}\vspace{0pt}\fontsize{9}{11.5}\selectfont\setlength{\GutterOffset}{0pt}\setlength{\FullSourceWidth}{\linewidth}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0245-N02}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0245-notes_right-L001}{}{}{\Indent (2) Pertinent igitur ad seriorem non solum Sy-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0245-notes_right-L002}{}{}{riasmi sed etiam Arabismi aetatem, non, ut \Name{von}}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0245-notes_right-L003}{}{}{\Name{Braunmühl}, I, 50, adn. 1, scribit, « in den ersten}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0245-notes_right-L004}{}{}{Zeiten des Mittelalters ».}
\end{minipage}
\DSource{0246}{157}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0246-P01}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0246-body-L001}{}{}{\Indent Altera vero}
\par\vspace{-1.53mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0246-body-L002}{}{}{\mbox{}\rlap{\hspace*{0.00mm}(\textgreek{β})}\rlap{\hspace*{94.00mm}\makebox[0pt][c]{$\sin \dfrac{\text{90°} - a}{2} = \sqrt{30^{p}\,(R - \sin a)}$}}}
\par\vspace{0.14mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0246-body-L003}{}{}{ex \Name{al–Battānī} et \Name{Ptolemaei} regula, in adn. \textbf{\textit{a}} (pag. 153) memorata}
\par\vspace{1.17mm}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0246-H04}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0246-body-L004}{}{}{\CenterLine{chorda $\dfrac{a}{2} = \sqrt{\dfrac{2R - \text{chorda}\,(\text{180°} - a)}{2} \times 2R}$,}}
\par\vspace{-2.39mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0246-body-L005}{}{}{oritur, quae, sinibus pro chordis positis et $R = 60^{p}$ sumpto, fit}
\par\vspace{0.05mm}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0246-H06}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0246-body-L006}{}{}{\CenterLine{sin $\dfrac{a}{2} = \sqrt{30^{p}\,(R - \cos a)}$.}}
\par\vspace{-0.67mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0246-body-L007}{}{}{Cur \Name{al–Battānī} pro \textit{a} eius complementum susceperit nescio.}
\par\fontsize{15}{17}\selectfont
\par\vspace{7.71mm}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0246-H08}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0246-body-L008}{}{}{\CenterLine{A\textsc{d} \textsc{caput} IV.}}
\par\fontsize{11.1}{13.8}\selectfont
\par\vspace{2.76mm}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0246-H09}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0246-body-L009}{}{}{\CenterLine{\textbf{\textit{a}}) — \textit{Pag. 12.}}}
\par\vspace{3.46mm}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0246-P02}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0246-body-L010}{}{}{\Indent De Arabibus qui ante annum 1000 Chr. \textit{obliquitatem eclipticae} dimensi sunt, haec \Name{Ibn}}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0246-body-L011}{}{}{\Name{Yūnus} in XI capite operis sui (1) narrat: « Inter \Name{Ptolemaeum} et auctores \textit{Tabulae}}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0246-body-L012}{}{}{« \textit{Probatae} (\textit{al–mumtaḥan}) observationes obliquitatis haud cognosco praeter observationem}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0246-body-L013}{}{}{« paulo post annum 160 hegirae [inc. 18 Oct. 776] factam (2), cuius auctor obliquitatem 23° 31′}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0246-body-L014}{}{}{« memorat. Quod vero quantitatem attinet ab auctoribus \textit{Tabulae probatae} (3) in observatio-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0246-body-L015}{}{}{« nibus aetati nostrae vicinis repertam, \Name{Yaḥyà ibn Abī Manṣūr}, \Name{Sanad ibn ‘Alī}, \Name{al-‘Ab-}}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0246-body-L016}{}{}{« \Name{bās ibn Sa‘īd al-Ǵawharī} et coetus virorum doctorum ac insignium (\ArabicRun{وطايفة من اهل العلم والفضل})}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0246-body-L017}{}{}{« in [regione] ash–Shammāsiyyah [vocata] urbis Baghdād, tempore [khalifae] al–Ma’mūn, de}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0246-body-L018}{}{}{« ea inquisiverunt, inveneruntque 23° 33′, ut, secundum eos, narrant Muḥammad ibn Mūsà}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0246-body-L019}{}{}{« \Name{al–Khuwārizmī} in opere suo astronomico (\ArabicRun{في زيجه}), et Muḥammad ibn Kathīr \Name{al–Farghānī}}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0246-body-L020}{}{}{« in libro suo de astrolabio conficiendo; et res nota est (4). Qui Damasci, post Yaḥyà ibn Abī}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0246-body-L021}{}{}{« Manṣūr mortuum, observaverunt instrumento quod al–Ma’mūn sumere iusserat cum ad Ro-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0246-body-L022}{}{}{« manorum terram versus profectus erat (\ArabicRun{بالالة التي امر باتخاذها المامون حين توجــه الى بــلاد الروم}), et}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0246-body-L023}{}{}{« sunt \Name{Khālid ibn ‘Abd al–Malik al–Marwarrūdhī}, \Name{Abū ’ṭ–ṭayyib Sanad ibn ‘Alī},}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0246-body-L024}{}{}{« \Name{‘Alī} ibn Mūsà \Name{al–Asṭurlābī} aliique, narrant se invenisse maximam declinationem eclipti-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0246-body-L025}{}{}{« cae 23° 33′ 52″; et fuit dimensio, per hoc instrumentum facta, anno 201 ab Yazdaǵird »}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0246-body-L026}{}{}{[inc. 27 Apr. 832] (5).}
\par\vspace{2.80mm}
\par\vspace{6pt}\nointerlineskip\hbox to\textwidth{\hss\vrule width 115.43pt height.25pt depth0pt\hss}\prevdepth=-1000pt \vspace{5pt}
\noindent\begin{minipage}[t]{.48\textwidth}\vspace{0pt}\fontsize{9}{11.5}\selectfont\setlength{\GutterOffset}{0pt}\setlength{\FullSourceWidth}{\linewidth}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0246-N01}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0246-notes_left-L001}{}{}{\Indent (1) Pag. 222 codicis Leidensis (cuius diligentis-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0246-notes_left-L002}{}{}{simum apographum, eadem paginarum divisione ser-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0246-notes_left-L003}{}{}{vata, est codex Parisiensis nr. 2495). Locum contuli}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0246-notes_left-L004}{}{}{cum codice Parisiensi 2496 (capita selecta ex Ibn}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0246-notes_left-L005}{}{}{Yūnus opere praebente, et neglegentius exarato),}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0246-notes_left-L006}{}{}{fol. 25,r.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0246-N02}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0246-notes_left-L007}{}{}{\Indent (2) Quod « paulo post » verti, Arabice est \ArabicRun{نيف}}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0246-notes_left-L008}{}{}{i. e. « plures duobus sed pauciores decem ».}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0246-N03}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0246-notes_left-L009}{}{}{\Indent (3) Est opus astronomicum quod, Yaḥyà ibn}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0246-notes_left-L010}{}{}{Abī Manṣūr duce, iuxta suas observationes astro-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0246-notes_left-L011}{}{}{nomi khalifae al-Ma’mūn (regn. 813-833 Chr.) condi-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0246-notes_left-L012}{}{}{derunt.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0246-N04}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0246-notes_left-L013}{}{}{\Indent (4) Idque confirmat ipse \Name{Ibn Yūnus} in intro-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0246-notes_left-L014}{}{}{ductione operis sui (apud \Name{Caussin}, pag. 56), dicens}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0246-notes_left-L015}{}{}{anno 214 heg. (inc. 10 Mart. 829), 198 Iezdegerdis}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0246-notes_left-L016}{}{}{(inc. 28 Apr. 829), Yaḥyà b. Abī Manṣūr, al-‘Abbās}
\end{minipage}
\hfill\vrule width.3pt\hfill\begin{minipage}[t]{.48\textwidth}\vspace{0pt}\fontsize{9}{11.5}\selectfont\setlength{\GutterOffset}{0pt}\setlength{\FullSourceWidth}{\linewidth}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0246-notes_right-L001}{}{}{ibn Sa‘īd al-Ǵawharī, Sanad ibn ‘Alī aliosque in urbe}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0246-notes_right-L002}{}{}{Baghdād 23° 33′ invenisse. — \Name{Az-Zarqālī}, in ver-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0246-notes_right-L003}{}{}{sione Latina inedita \textit{Canonum} seu \textit{Tabularum To-}}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0246-notes_right-L004}{}{}{\textit{letanarum}, et Iudaeus \Name{Abrāhām ben ‘Ezrā} (in}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0246-notes_right-L005}{}{}{secunda saeculi XII Chr. medietate) dicunt Yaḥyà}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0246-notes_right-L006}{}{}{ibn Abī Manṣūr 23° 33′ 30″ invenisse; cfr. \Name{Delam-}}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0246-notes_right-L007}{}{}{\Name{bre}, p. 177 (cuius codex Tabularum Toletanarum no-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0246-notes_right-L008}{}{}{men corruptum praebebat « Almeon fils d’Albuma-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0246-notes_right-L009}{}{}{zar »), et M. \Name{Steinschneider}, \textit{Études sur Zar-}}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0246-notes_right-L010}{}{}{\textit{kali} in Bullettino di bibliografia e di storia delle}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0246-notes_right-L011}{}{}{scienze matematiche e fisiche, t. XX, 1887-88, p. 87}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0246-notes_right-L012}{}{}{(ubi codex Tabularum indicatur, nomen « Jahiben fi-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0246-notes_right-L013}{}{}{lius Albumasaris » exhibens).}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0246-N05}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0246-notes_right-L014}{}{}{\Indent (5) Eadem narrat in introductione (\Name{Caussin},}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0246-notes_right-L015}{}{}{56); annus ibi est 217 heg. (inc. 7 Febr. 832), 201}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0246-notes_right-L016}{}{}{Iezdegerdis.}
\end{minipage}
\DSource{0247}{158}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0247-P01}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0247-body-L001}{}{}{\Indent « Dicit Abū ’l–ḥasan \Name{Thābit ibn Qorrah}: Operationes antiquas ante Ptolemaeum in-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0247-body-L002}{}{}{« veni, quae ostendunt (\ArabicRun{تدل}) maximam declinationem 23° 35′ esse (1). Narrat Muḥammad ibn}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0247-body-L003}{}{}{« Ǵābir ibn Sinān \Name{al–Battānī} se, suis observationibus, 23° 35′ invenisse. Nobilis praeclarus-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0247-body-L004}{}{}{« que Abū ’l–Qāsim ‘Alī ibn al–Ḥusayn ibn Muḥammad ibn ‘Īsà, notus nomine \Name{Ibn al–A‘lam},}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0247-body-L005}{}{}{« vir doctrina praestantique intellectu praeditus, dicit se maximam declinationem metitum esse,}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0247-body-L006}{}{}{« et, in mensura summa adhibita diligentia, 23° 34′ 2″ reperisse. Abū ’l–ḥasan \Name{aṣ–Ṣūfī ‘Abd}}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0247-body-L007}{}{}{« \Name{ar–Raḥmān} ibn ‘Omar narrat se maximam declinationem metitum esse et, dimensione}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0247-body-L008}{}{}{« accuratissime peracta, 23° 33′ 45″ (2) invenisse; vir ille locum insignem occupabat in scientia}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0247-body-L009}{}{}{« demonstrationum geometricarum. A Deo autem auxilium provenit ».}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0247-P02}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0247-body-L010}{}{}{\Indent \Name{Ibn Yūnus}, cap. IV (apud \Name{Caussin} pag. 120), dicit in \textit{Tabula Probata} quantitatem}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0247-body-L011}{}{}{23° 35′ delectam esse; idemque \Name{al–Farghānī} in cap. V (pag. 20 editionis Golii, fol. 121,r.}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0247-body-L012}{}{}{codicis Parisiensis 2504) refert. Sed \Name{Ibn Rosteh}, qui totum hoc caput aequalis sui al–Far-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0247-body-L013}{}{}{ghānī transcribit, 23° 33′ pro 23° 35′ habet (3); et 23° 33′ quoque legitur in al–Farghānī ver-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0247-body-L014}{}{}{sione latina a \Name{Iohanne Hispalensi} facta et anno 1537 Norimbergae impressa (fol. 5,r.).}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0247-body-L015}{}{}{Quod probabilius videtur si conferantur quae ipse Ibn Yūnus narrat de dimensione ab aucto-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0247-body-L016}{}{}{ribus \textit{Tabulae Probatae} facta, et quae \Name{Rabiçag de Toledo} in suo \textit{Libro de las armel-}}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0247-body-L017}{}{}{\textit{las} (4), Arabicis fontibus omnino hausto, scribit: « Et sabet que en este nuestro tiempo se-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0247-body-L018}{}{}{« gund el nostro rectificar. bien se paresció por los rectificamentos antigos et los nuevos. que}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0247-body-L019}{}{}{« lo que ha entre ellos [intervallum scilicet inter polum eclipticae et polum aequatoris] non}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0247-body-L020}{}{}{« es uno todauia. Et esto es porque Abrachis [= Hipparchus] falló la declinacion que era}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0247-body-L021}{}{}{« .XXIII. grados et .LI. menudos. et falláronla los rectificadores [auctores scilicet Tabulae}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0247-body-L022}{}{}{« Probatae] en el tiempo de Memun Abdalla. fiio de Haron Arraxit .XXIII. grados et XXXIII.}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0247-body-L023}{}{}{« menudos. Et fallóla Mohamat. fiio de Aben el Bateni [= Muḥammad ibn Sinān al–Battānī]}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0247-body-L024}{}{}{« .XXIII. grados et .XXXV. menudos ». — Nec aliter \Name{Thābit ibn Qorrah} in libro \textit{de}}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0247-body-L025}{}{}{\textit{motu octavae sphaerae seu accessionis et recessionis,} qui in duabus diversis Latinis versio-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0247-body-L026}{}{}{nibus in Bibliotheca Vaticana exstat; altera (cod. Lat. 4275, f. 84,v., col. I) dicit: « Et eius}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0247-body-L027}{}{}{« [i. e. totius obliquitatis] summa est 24 graduum secundum quod translatum est ab Indis,}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0247-body-L028}{}{}{« et similiter reperit Ptholomeus 23 g. et 51 minut., et consideratores [i. e. \ArabicRun{اصحاب الممتحن} au-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0247-body-L029}{}{}{« ctores Tabulae Probatae] invenerunt in tempore Maymonis 23 g. et 33 m. »; altera (cod.}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0247-body-L030}{}{}{Lat. 4083, f. 56,v., col. II): « pervenit talis declinatio ad 24 gradus secundum quod ab Indis}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0247-body-L031}{}{}{« traditum fuit. Ptolomeus quos ..... (?) invenit eam 23 graduum et 51 mt. Alii autem inspec-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0247-body-L032}{}{}{« tores post Ptolomeum in tempore Mamonis eam 23 graduum 33 minutorum invenerunt ».}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0247-body-L033}{}{}{— Nec demum praetermittendum est \Name{Thābit ibn Qorrah}, ut in adn. 1 vidimus, 23° 33′}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0247-body-L034}{}{}{delegisse; et similiter \Name{Qosṭā ibn Lūqā}, anno circiter 910 vel 912 Chr. mortuum, in libro}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0247-body-L035}{}{}{suo de constructione et usu globi caelestis (5). — Vix 23° 33′ delegissent nisi hanc quanti-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0247-body-L036}{}{}{tatem in \textit{Tabula Probata} invenissent.}
\par\vspace{7.68mm}
\par\vspace{6pt}\nointerlineskip\hbox to\textwidth{\hss\vrule width 115.43pt height.25pt depth0pt\hss}\prevdepth=-1000pt \vspace{5pt}
\noindent\begin{minipage}[t]{.48\textwidth}\vspace{0pt}\fontsize{9}{11.5}\selectfont\setlength{\GutterOffset}{0pt}\setlength{\FullSourceWidth}{\linewidth}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0247-N01}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0247-notes_left-L001}{}{}{\Indent (1) Sed in operibus suis \Name{Thābit} 23° 33′ delegit,}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0247-notes_left-L002}{}{}{ut ex \Name{Ḥabash}, fol. 90,v., et \Name{Delambre}, p. 73, patet.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0247-N02}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0247-notes_left-L003}{}{}{\Indent (2) Cod. Parisiensis 2496 perperam 23. 35. 45.}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0247-notes_left-L004}{}{}{38 scribit.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0247-N03}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0247-notes_left-L005}{}{}{\Indent (3) \Name{Ibn Rosteh}, \textit{Kitâb al-A‘lâk an-Nafisa}}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0247-notes_left-L006}{}{}{\textit{VII, edidit M. J. de Goeje,} Lugduni Batavorum 1889,}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0247-notes_left-L007}{}{}{p. 16 [= Bibliotheca Geographorum Arabicorum,}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0247-notes_left-L008}{}{}{t. VII].}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0247-N04}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0247-notes_left-L009}{}{}{\Indent (4) Lib. l, cap. IV = \textit{Libros del saber de astro-}}
\end{minipage}
\hfill\vrule width.3pt\hfill\begin{minipage}[t]{.48\textwidth}\vspace{0pt}\fontsize{9}{11.5}\selectfont\setlength{\GutterOffset}{0pt}\setlength{\FullSourceWidth}{\linewidth}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0247-notes_right-L001}{}{}{\textit{nomia del Rey D. Alfonso X de Castilla, copilados,}}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0247-notes_right-L002}{}{}{\textit{anotados y comentados por D. Manuel Rico y Si-}}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0247-notes_right-L003}{}{}{\textit{nobas,} Madrid 1863-67, t. II, p. 6. Pertinet ad secun-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0247-notes_right-L004}{}{}{dam medietatem XIII saec. Chr.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0247-N05}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0247-notes_right-L005}{}{}{\Indent (5) Cap. XXXIV versionis Hispanicae (\textit{Libros}}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0247-notes_right-L006}{}{}{\textit{del saber de astronomia,} t. l, p. 187), XXX vers.}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0247-notes_right-L007}{}{}{Hebraicae ineditae (apud \Name{Baldi}, \textit{Vite di matema-}}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0247-notes_right-L008}{}{}{\textit{tici arabi con note di} M. \Name{Steinschneider}, Roma}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0247-notes_right-L009}{}{}{1874, p. 70, adn.).}
\end{minipage}
\DSource{0248}{159}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0248-P01}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0248-body-L001}{}{}{\Indent Ex iis qui post auctores \textit{Tabulae Probatae} floruerunt usque ad ultimam al–Battānī aeta-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0248-body-L002}{}{}{tem scimus obliquitatem 23° 35′ observationibus invenisse familiam \Name{Banū Mūsà} (1), \Name{an–}}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0248-body-L003}{}{}{\Name{–Nayrīzī} (2), familiamque \Name{Banū Amāǵūr} (3). Item, e serioribus, \Name{Abū ’l–Wefā’}, Baghdād}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0248-body-L004}{}{}{in urbe, anno 987 Chr. (teste Delambre, p. 156), et \Name{Ibn Yūnus} anno 391 heg. (inc. 30 Nov.}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0248-body-L005}{}{}{1000 Chr.; cap. XI, apud Caussin, p. 238; exactius 23° 34′ 52″ invenerat). — Alii multi hanc}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0248-body-L006}{}{}{obliquitatem 23° 35′ in tabulis suis receperunt, ut \Name{Ḥabash} (aequalis al–Battānī), \Name{al–Kha-}}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0248-body-L007}{}{}{\Name{raqī} in suo \textit{Muntahà al–idrāk} (4), Hebraeo–Hispanus \Name{Abrāhām bar Ḥiyyā} anno 1135}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0248-body-L008}{}{}{Chr. scribens (5), et eius aequalis ‘Abd ar–Raḥmān \Name{al–Khāzinī} (6); item \Name{Naṣīr ad–dīn}}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0248-body-L009}{}{}{\Name{aṭ-Ṭūsī} in libris ante \textit{Tabulas īlkhānicas} confectis (7), Graecus \Name{Chrysococces} in suis}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0248-body-L010}{}{}{\textit{Tabulis Persicis} (8), demum, saeculo XIV Chr. ineunte, \Name{al–Ǵaghmīnī} (ut e p. 264 et 265}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0248-body-L011}{}{}{patet). \Name{Barhebraeus} seu \Name{Abū ’l–faraǵ}, in Astronomia sua Syriaca anno 1279 Chr.}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0248-body-L012}{}{}{conscripta, numeris rotundis utitur, qua re passim obliquitati 24° tribuit (9); sed ubi numeros}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0248-body-L013}{}{}{exactiores cupit eam 23° 35′ sumit, ut facile deduci potest e calculis ascensionum rectarum}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0248-body-L014}{}{}{et obliquarum (pag. 165–167 textus Syriaci).}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0248-P02}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0248-body-L015}{}{}{\Indent \Name{Abū ‘Alī al–Ḥasan} (i. e. \Name{Aboul Hhassan}), cap. XXIII, p. 175 et 176, ait obliquita-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0248-body-L016}{}{}{tem intra limites 23° 33′ et 23° 53′ variare; sed cap. XXIV, p. 180–187, et in tabulis 23° 35′}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0248-body-L017}{}{}{recepit.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0248-P03}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0248-body-L018}{}{}{\Indent Animadvertendum est errorem duorum minutorum in obliquitate statuenda, parvi mo-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0248-body-L019}{}{}{menti fieri in calculis qui per eam ducantur; in ascensionibus rectis enim discrepantiam tan-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0248-body-L020}{}{}{tum in secundis arcus gignere potest (10); in ascensionibus obliquis maxima differentia tria}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0248-body-L021}{}{}{minuta arcus nunquam attingit (praesertim cum latitudines quibus Arabes uti debebant semper}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0248-body-L022}{}{}{essent quam 45° minores); demum in calculo maximi arcus semidiurni Solis in terris Arabibus}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0248-body-L023}{}{}{subiectis differentia nunquam potest decem secundas horae superare. Tales differentiae iis tem-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0248-body-L024}{}{}{poribus neglegendae erant.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0248-P04}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0248-body-L025}{}{}{\Indent Ut iudicari possit quanta diligentia Arabes maximam declinationem eclipticae observave-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0248-body-L026}{}{}{rint, praecipuarum observationum tabellam confeci, formula Besseliana in calculis meis adhi-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0248-body-L027}{}{}{bita, quae, pro \textit{t} annis ante 1750 Chr., est}
\par\vspace{0.72mm}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0248-H28}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0248-body-L028}{}{}{\CenterLine{$\varepsilon = \text{23° 28′ 18″. 0} + \text{0″.48368}{\cdot}t - \text{0″.000\,002\,72295}{\cdot}t^{2}$.}}
\par\vspace{4.89mm}
\par\vspace{6pt}\nointerlineskip\hbox to\textwidth{\hss\vrule width 115.43pt height.25pt depth0pt\hss}\prevdepth=-1000pt \vspace{5pt}
\noindent\begin{minipage}[t]{.48\textwidth}\vspace{0pt}\fontsize{9}{11.5}\selectfont\setlength{\GutterOffset}{0pt}\setlength{\FullSourceWidth}{\linewidth}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0248-N01}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0248-notes_left-L001}{}{}{\Indent (1) \Name{Ibn Yūnus}, cap. V, apud \Name{Caussin},}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0248-notes_left-L002}{}{}{p. 152. — Ex his Banū Mūsà unus obiit mense rabī‘}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0248-notes_left-L003}{}{}{priore 259 heg. = Ian. 873 Chr.; de iis v. \Name{Suter},}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0248-notes_left-L004}{}{}{\textit{Die Mathematiker und Astronomen der Araber}}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0248-notes_left-L005}{}{}{\textit{und ihre Werke,} Leipzig 1900, pag. 20-21, nr. 43}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0248-notes_left-L006}{}{}{(= Abhandlungen zur Geschichte der mathemati-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0248-notes_left-L007}{}{}{schen Wissenschaften, fasc. X; Supplem. zum 45. Jahr-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0248-notes_left-L008}{}{}{gang der Zeitschrift für Mathematik und Physik).}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0248-N02}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0248-notes_left-L009}{}{}{\Indent (2) \Name{Ibn Yūnus}, cap. IV, apud \Name{Caussin},}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0248-notes_left-L010}{}{}{p. 120. — Obiit anno circiter 310 heg. (922-23 Chr.);}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0248-notes_left-L011}{}{}{v. \Name{Suter}, p. 45, nr. 88.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0248-N03}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0248-notes_left-L012}{}{}{\Indent (3) \Name{Ibn Yūnus}, cap. V, apud \Name{Caussin},}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0248-notes_left-L013}{}{}{p. 152. — Observaverunt annis 272-321 heg., (885-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0248-notes_left-L014}{}{}{–933 Chr.); v. \Name{Suter}, p. 49-50, n. 99.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0248-N04}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0248-notes_left-L015}{}{}{\Indent (4) Cod. Laurentianus (Orient.-Palat. 110), f. 40,r.}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0248-notes_left-L016}{}{}{(I maqālah, XIV bāb, I faṣl).}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0248-N05}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0248-notes_left-L017}{}{}{\Indent (5) \textit{Sphaera mundi auctore Rabbi} \Name{Abraha-}}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0248-notes_left-L018}{}{}{\Name{mo Hispano filio R. Haijae}. \textit{Osw. Schrecken-}}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0248-notes_left-L019}{}{}{\textit{fuchsius vertit in linguam Latinam, Seb. Mun-}}
\end{minipage}
\hfill\vrule width.3pt\hfill\begin{minipage}[t]{.48\textwidth}\vspace{0pt}\fontsize{9}{11.5}\selectfont\setlength{\GutterOffset}{0pt}\setlength{\FullSourceWidth}{\linewidth}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0248-notes_right-L001}{}{}{\textit{sterus illustravit annotationibus.} Basileae 1546,}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0248-notes_right-L002}{}{}{cap. X.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0248-N06}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0248-notes_right-L003}{}{}{\Indent (6) Cod. Vaticanus Arab. 761, f. 10,v. et passim.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0248-N07}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0248-notes_right-L004}{}{}{\Indent (7) Ut in \textit{Tadhkirah}, cod. Laurentianus (Palat.-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0248-notes_right-L005}{}{}{-Orient. 271), f. 38,v. (II bāb, IV faṣl).}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0248-N08}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0248-notes_right-L006}{}{}{\Indent (8) In \Name{Ismaelis Bullialdi} \textit{Astronomia Phi-}}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0248-notes_right-L007}{}{}{\textit{lolaica}, Parisiis 1545, II pars, p. 224. — \Name{Bullialdus}}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0248-notes_right-L008}{}{}{eum censet in prima medietate XIII saec. Chr. flo-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0248-notes_right-L009}{}{}{ruisse (l. l., p. 213); sed \Name{Krumbacher}, \textit{Geschichte}}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0248-notes_right-L010}{}{}{\textit{der Byzantinischen Litteratur}, 2. Aufl., München}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0248-notes_right-L011}{}{}{1897, dicit eum circa annum 1346 Chr. vixisse.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0248-N09}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0248-notes_right-L012}{}{}{\Indent (9) Est quantitas nota Graecis ante Eratosthe-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0248-notes_right-L013}{}{}{nem; ea semper Indi utebantur. — Cfr. quoque in-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0248-notes_right-L014}{}{}{fra, p. 161, adn. 3.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0248-N10}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0248-notes_right-L015}{}{}{\Indent (10) [Maxima differentia est in octantibus, scili-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0248-notes_right-L016}{}{}{cet in ascensionibus rectis 45°, 135°, 225°, 315°, ubi}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0248-notes_right-L017}{}{}{error unius minuti in obliquitate statuenda errorem}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0248-notes_right-L018}{}{}{0′.217 (vel 13″ praecise fere) in ascensione recta gi-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0248-notes_right-L019}{}{}{gnit. — \textsc{Schiaparelli}]}
\end{minipage}
\DSource{0249}{160}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0249-body-L001}{}{}{Stellula * indico annos observationum certos; ubi annus ignoratur, eum posui qui est auctoris}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0249-body-L002}{}{}{tabularum epocha vel qui circiter praesumi potest. Errores usque ad secundas notavi; sed}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0249-body-L003}{}{}{nemo ignorat hos numeros absolute non semper recipi posse, cum interdum annus observatio-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0249-body-L004}{}{}{nis haud certe cognoscatur, aut observator Arabicus de industria secundas neglexerit (1).}
\par\vspace{5.49mm}
\par\noindent\hspace*{2.38mm}\rule{184.06mm}{.6pt}\par\nointerlineskip\vspace{1pt}\noindent\hspace*{2.38mm}\rule{184.06mm}{.6pt}\par\nointerlineskip
