\DLine{AB01-PDF0249-notes_left-L001}{}{}{\Indent (1) In tabellis quae, post \Name{Lalande} (ut puto),}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0249-notes_left-L002}{}{}{apud scriptores nostrates circumferuntur (diligentis-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0249-notes_left-L003}{}{}{simis \Name{Houzeau} et \Name{Wolf} non exceptis) errores}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0249-notes_left-L004}{}{}{insunt, quorum originem nescio; Eratostheni enim}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0249-notes_left-L005}{}{}{23° 45′ 7″ tribuunt, al-Battānī 23° 35′ 41″, Ibn Yūnus}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0249-notes_left-L006}{}{}{23° 34′ 26″, Ulugh Beg 23° 31′ 48″, Abū ‘Alī al-Ḥasan}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0249-notes_left-L007}{}{}{(Aboul Hhassan) 23° 33′ 45″ !.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0249-N02}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0249-notes_left-L008}{}{}{\Indent (2) \Name{Ptolemaeus}, anno 140 Chr., eandem quan-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0249-notes_left-L009}{}{}{titatem servat; error igitur + 10′ 10″ fit. Error}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0249-notes_left-L010}{}{}{\Name{Theonis}, qui anno fere 360 Chr. scripsit, est}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0249-notes_left-L011}{}{}{+ 12′ 1″.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0249-N03}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0249-notes_left-L012}{}{}{\Indent (3) Si 23° 33′ 30″ admittantur, ut quidam dicunt}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0249-notes_left-L013}{}{}{(cfr. p. 6, adn. 3), error — 2′ 11″ fit.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0249-N04}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0249-notes_left-L014}{}{}{\Indent (4) Iuxta locum \Name{Ibn al-Qifṭī} quem \Name{Casiri},}
\end{minipage}
\hfill\vrule width.3pt\hfill\begin{minipage}[t]{.48\textwidth}\vspace{0pt}\fontsize{9}{11.5}\selectfont\setlength{\GutterOffset}{0pt}\setlength{\FullSourceWidth}{\linewidth}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0249-notes_right-L001}{}{}{\textit{Bibliotheca Arabico-hispana Escurialensis,} Matriti}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0249-notes_right-L002}{}{}{1760-70, t. I, p. 441, et \Name{Sédillot} in praefatione ad}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0249-notes_right-L003}{}{}{textum \Name{Oloug Beg}, p. LII-LIII, adducunt. Eandem}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0249-notes_right-L004}{}{}{lectionem praebet codex Ibn al-Qifṭī qui Romae in}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0249-notes_right-L005}{}{}{Bibliotheca Academiae Lyncaeorum asservatur.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0249-N05}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0249-notes_right-L006}{}{}{\Indent (5) Ita passim in \textit{Libro de las taulas alfonsies}}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0249-notes_right-L007}{}{}{(\textit{Libros del saber de astronomia,} t. IV) et in \textit{Libro}}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0249-notes_right-L008}{}{}{\textit{del quadrante para rectificar} (ibid. t. III, p. 296-297).}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0249-N06}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0249-notes_right-L009}{}{}{\Indent (6) Iuxta adnotationem \Name{Sédillot} ad textum}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0249-notes_right-L010}{}{}{\Name{Oloug Beg}, p. 63; locus ibi adductus reperitur}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0249-notes_right-L011}{}{}{in f. 105,r. codicis Florentini earumdem \textit{Tabularum}}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0249-notes_right-L012}{}{}{\textit{īlkhānicarum}. Iuxta hanc quantitatem supputantur}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0249-notes_right-L013}{}{}{declinationes primae (f. 120,r.-121,r.) et secundae}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0249-notes_right-L014}{}{}{(f. 121,v.-122,v.).}
\end{minipage}
\DSource{0250}{161}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0250-body-L001}{}{}{obliquitatis eclipticae deducere potuisse. Quo modo id argumentatus esset, cum ab Eratosthene}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0250-body-L002}{}{}{ad Theonem, nempe intervallo 600 annorum fere, Graeci omnes se 23° 51′ 20″ invenisse sem-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0250-body-L003}{}{}{per dicant?}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0250-P01}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0250-body-L004}{}{}{\Indent Arabibus serioribus, qui multas easdemque bonas observationes inter se comparare pote-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0250-body-L005}{}{}{rant, hypothesis continuae deminutionis haud ignota fuit, licet parvae discrepantiae erroribus}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0250-body-L006}{}{}{observatorum et instrumentorum imputari potuissent. Haec reapse legimus in libro de astro-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0250-body-L007}{}{}{nomia, Sanǵarico inscripto (\textit{az–zīǵ as–sanǵarī}), ab Abū Manṣūr ‘Abd ar–raḥmān \Name{al–Khāzinī}}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0250-body-L008}{}{}{anno fere hegirae 520 (1126 Chr.) confecto (1): « In [statuenda] eclipticae obliquitate multae}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0250-body-L009}{}{}{« sunt discrepantiae, de quarum causa variae sunt opiniones. Omnium praestantissima est id}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0250-body-L010}{}{}{« nonnisi ob refractionem radiorum fieri (\ArabicRun{وغالب الظنّ ان سببه انعطاف الشعاع دون سايره}), qua re, cum}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0250-body-L011}{}{}{« latitudo duorum observatorum una eademque sit, eadem obliquitas ambobus appareat. Ma-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0250-body-L012}{}{}{« xima apud Indos declinatio 24° 0′ est, apud Ptolemaeum et Hipparchum 23° 51′, apud au-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0250-body-L013}{}{}{« ctores [Tabulae] Probatae et al–Battānī 23° 35′, apud recentiores quosdam (\ArabicRun{بعض المحدثين}) 23° 33′.}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0250-body-L014}{}{}{« Ob has discrepantias et ob diversitatem in motu stellarum fixarum, quibusdam visum est (\ArabicRun{ظهر}}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0250-body-L015}{}{}{« \ArabicRun{لبعضهم}) motum statuere in spatio inter duas sphaeras universi et fixarum, qui singulis 1285 (\ArabicRun{١٢٨٥})}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0250-body-L016}{}{}{« annis solaribus circumferentiam totam perficeret, et cuius poli, ut poli quoque fixarum, in}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0250-body-L017}{}{}{« axe universi non essent; qua re signorum zodiacalium poli ad aequatores appropinquarent aut}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0250-body-L018}{}{}{« ab iis recederent, et motus fixarum acceleraretur aut retardaretur, iuxta modum quem nunc}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0250-body-L019}{}{}{« memorare longius est. Sed quoniam obliquitas apud plerosque [auctores] 23° 35′ est, nos hac}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0250-body-L020}{}{}{« quantitate, utpote iure recepta, in considerationibus nostris utemur (\ArabicRun{استعملناه في الاعتبار مُسلا}) ». —}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0250-body-L021}{}{}{\Name{Naṣīr ad–dīn aṭ–Ṭūsī} autem, in suo Libello de iis quae praecipue memoranda sunt in}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0250-body-L022}{}{}{astronomia (2), haec narrat: « Declinatio tota, antiquis et recentioribus observationibus com-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0250-body-L023}{}{}{« perta, non una eademque est; sed quantitas a veteribus inventa maior est ea quam recen-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0250-body-L024}{}{}{« tiores metiti sunt. Quod aliquis recentior invenit ducitur interdum minus esse quam quod anti-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0250-body-L025}{}{}{« quior reperit, cum maxima quantitas comperta 24° non attingat (3), minima autem a 23° $\qfrac{1}{2}$ +}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0250-body-L026}{}{}{« $\qfrac{1}{2\times 10}$ [= 23° 33′] non decrescat(4). Plerique (\ArabicRun{الجمهور}) eam 23° $\qfrac{1}{4}$ + $\qfrac{1}{3}$ [= 23° 35′] statuunt.}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0250-body-L027}{}{}{« Ob has discrepantias putaverunt quidam eclipticam in latitudine moveri et circulo aequinoc-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0250-body-L028}{}{}{« tiali appropinquare; quod si verum esset, alia sphaera statui deberet talem motum zodiaco}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0250-body-L029}{}{}{« deferens. Si ecliptica moveretur, posset circumferentiam perficere; aut non perficere, sed}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0250-body-L030}{}{}{« ad limitem quemdam pervenire unde postea rediret. Limes quisque autem exsistere posset}
\par\vspace{4.18mm}
\par\vspace{6pt}\nointerlineskip\hbox to\textwidth{\hss\vrule width 115.43pt height.25pt depth0pt\hss}\prevdepth=-1000pt \vspace{5pt}
\noindent\begin{minipage}[t]{.48\textwidth}\vspace{0pt}\fontsize{9}{11.5}\selectfont\setlength{\GutterOffset}{0pt}\setlength{\FullSourceWidth}{\linewidth}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0250-N01}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0250-notes_left-L001}{}{}{\Indent (1) Cod. Vatic. Arab. nr. 761, in tertio capite}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0250-notes_left-L002}{}{}{(\textit{bāb}) praefationis, f. 10,v.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0250-N02}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0250-notes_left-L003}{}{}{\Indent (2) \textit{At-tadhkirah fī ‘ilm al-hay’ah,} in IV ca-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0250-notes_left-L004}{}{}{pite (\textit{faṣl}) secundae sectionis (\textit{bāb}), f. 38,r.-39,r. co-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0250-notes_left-L005}{}{}{dicis Bibliothecae Laurentianae, commentaria \Name{Qāḍī-}}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0250-notes_left-L006}{}{}{\Name{–zādeh} quoque continentis.— Locus, Gallice in com-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0250-notes_left-L007}{}{}{pendium redactus, est apud \Name{Carra de Vaux}, \textit{Les}}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0250-notes_left-L008}{}{}{\textit{sphères célestes selon Nasîr-eddîn Attûsî,} in calce}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0250-notes_left-L009}{}{}{operis P. \Name{Tannery}, \textit{Recherches sur l’histoire de}}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0250-notes_left-L010}{}{}{\textit{l’astronomie ancienne,} Paris 1893, p. 346.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0250-N03}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0250-notes_left-L011}{}{}{\Indent (3) Indici astronomi semper 24° habent. —}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0250-notes_left-L012}{}{}{\Name{Qāḍī-zādeh} ad hunc locum monet: « Putant qui-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0250-notes_left-L013}{}{}{« dam id [i. e. 24°] sapientes Indorum invenisse, ean-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0250-notes_left-L014}{}{}{« demque quantitatem notissimam Euclidis tempore}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0250-notes_left-L015}{}{}{« fuisse, quare in libro suo [Euclidem], post latus}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0250-notes_left-L016}{}{}{« hexagoni in circulo inscripti supputatum, latus}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0250-notes_left-L017}{}{}{« pentedecagoni in circulo inscripti deduxisse, cum}
\end{minipage}
\hfill\vrule width.3pt\hfill\begin{minipage}[t]{.48\textwidth}\vspace{0pt}\fontsize{9}{11.5}\selectfont\setlength{\GutterOffset}{0pt}\setlength{\FullSourceWidth}{\linewidth}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0250-notes_right-L001}{}{}{« essent 24 quintadecima pars circumferentiae ». —}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0250-notes_right-L002}{}{}{Nec aliter Quṭb ad-dīn \Name{Maḥmūd ash–Shīrāzī}, \textit{at-}}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0250-notes_right-L003}{}{}{\textit{-tuḥfah ash-shāhiyyah,} f. 5,v.: « Putant quidam In-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0250-notes_right-L004}{}{}{« dos omnes in hac quantitate [i. e. 24°] consentire,}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0250-notes_right-L005}{}{}{« et 24° fuisse obliquitatem tempore Euclidis; qua}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0250-notes_right-L006}{}{}{« re eum in libro suo deduxisse latus pentedecagoni,}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0250-notes_right-L007}{}{}{« cum essent 24 quintadecima circumferentiae pars ».}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0250-notes_right-L008}{}{}{— Mirum satis in XV et XVI propositionibus quarti}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0250-notes_right-L009}{}{}{libri \Name{Euclidis}, de inscribendis in circulo hexa-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0250-notes_right-L010}{}{}{gonis et pentedecagonis regularibus, quosdam obli-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0250-notes_right-L011}{}{}{quitatem eclipticae adumbratam vidisse!}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0250-N04}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0250-notes_right-L012}{}{}{\Indent (4) \Name{Qāḍī-zādeh}: « Ita tempore quo hic liber}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0250-notes_right-L013}{}{}{« scriptus est; sed postea auctor ipse, novis obser-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0250-notes_right-L014}{}{}{« vationibus quas Marāghah [in urbe] instituit, 23°}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0250-notes_right-L015}{}{}{« 30′ invenit, quae est minima quantitas adhuc re-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0250-notes_right-L016}{}{}{« perta ». Et re vera \Name{Naṣīr ad-dīn}, in tabulis}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0250-notes_right-L017}{}{}{suis \textit{īlkhānicis}, obliquitatem 23° 30′ recepit.}
\end{minipage}
\par\vspace{5mm}\hfill{\fontsize{8}{10}\selectfont 21}\hspace{10mm}
\DSource{0251}{162}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0251-body-L001}{}{}{« aut post binos eclipticae in aequatorem casus, aut tempore secundi casus, aut inter duos}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0251-body-L002}{}{}{« casus; hoc vero [triplici modo, scilicet] post dimidium circulum peractum, aut tempore dimi-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0251-body-L003}{}{}{« dii circuli exacte, aut ante semicirculum perfectum. Posset demum quoque ecliptica ad spa-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0251-body-L004}{}{}{« tium inter duos casus non pervenire, cum ea redire cogiteretur aut tempore primi sui in}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0251-body-L005}{}{}{« aequatorem casus aut ante eum. Sunt ergo hypotheses octo; iuxta priores quinque binae}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0251-body-L006}{}{}{« zodiaci medietates, borealis nempe et australis, inter se permutarentur simul cum proprie-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0251-body-L007}{}{}{« tatibus suis; et iuxta tres ex iis [quinque] singulae zodiaci medietates in singulas aequa-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0251-body-L008}{}{}{« toris medietates [interdum] inciderent. Iuxta priores septem hypotheses dimidia eclipticae}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0251-body-L009}{}{}{« pars cum dimidia aequatoris sibi proxima conveniret; quotiescumque hoc fieret, dies noctes-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0251-body-L010}{}{}{« que omnibus Terrae locis inter se aequarentur, et variae anni partes deficerent. Iuxta au-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0251-body-L011}{}{}{« tem hypothesim ultimam [seu octavam] (1) nonnisi incrementum aut decrementum conse-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0251-body-L012}{}{}{« queretur in altitudinibus [Solis] et in longitudinibus dierum noctiumque ».}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0251-P01}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0251-body-L013}{}{}{\Indent \Name{Quṭb ad–dīn Maḥmūd ash–Shīrāzī}, qui mense ramaḍān 710 (Febr. 1311 Chr.) obiit,}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0251-body-L014}{}{}{in sua \textit{at–tuḥfah ash–shāhiyyah} (f. 5,v.–6,r.) dicit nullam certam decrementi proportionem}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0251-body-L015}{}{}{inveniri inter diversas obliquitatis quantitates diversis temporibus dimensas.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0251-P02}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0251-body-L016}{}{}{\Indent Qui historiam astronomiae scripserunt dicunt in secunda saeculi XVI Chr. medietate quos-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0251-body-L017}{}{}{dam primo continuam obliquitatis deminutionem agnovisse, ut \Name{Ignatium Danti} in suo}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0251-body-L018}{}{}{\textit{Trattato dell’Astrolabio,} Firenze 1569, licet alii multi illius aetatis eam in dubium vocavis-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0251-body-L019}{}{}{sent; \Name{Tychonem Brahe} (1546–1601) eandem e mutatis latitudinibus stellarum argumen-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0251-body-L020}{}{}{tatum esse; \Name{Eulerum} (1707–1783) demum legibus mechanicae caelestis demonstravisse.}
\par\vspace{5.32mm}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0251-H21}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0251-body-L021}{}{}{\CenterLine{\textbf{\textit{b}}) — \textit{Pag. 12, l. 27–32.}}}
\par\vspace{4.35mm}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0251-P03}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0251-body-L022}{}{}{\Indent Regula ad inveniendam \textit{Solis} vel cuiuscumque gradus eclipticae \textbf{\textit{declinationem}} e constru-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0251-body-L023}{}{}{ctione \Name{Ptolemaei}, \textit{Almag.} I, 12 (ed. Halma, t. I, p. 56–58) consequitur. Sit \textgreek{ε} obliquitas}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0251-body-L024}{}{}{eclipticae, \textgreek{δ} declinatio dati gradus, L longitudo Solis vel dati gradus; Ptolemaeus per notum}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0251-body-L025}{}{}{theorema Menelai demonstrat}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0251-H26}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0251-body-L026}{}{}{\CenterLine{$\dfrac{\text{chorda 180°}}{\text{chorda } 2\text{\textgreek{ε}}} = \dfrac{\text{chorda 180°}}{\text{chorda } 2\text{\textgreek{δ}}} \times \dfrac{\text{chorda } 2L}{\text{chorda 180°}}$.}}
\par\vspace{0.98mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0251-body-L027}{}{}{Cum chorda 180° = diametro sit (2R), si pro chordis sinus adhibeantur exit}
\par\vspace{0.45mm}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0251-H28}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0251-body-L028}{}{}{\CenterLine{$\dfrac{2R}{2 \sin \text{\textgreek{ε}}} = \dfrac{2R}{2 \sin \text{\textgreek{δ}}} \times \dfrac{2 \sin L}{2R}$,}}
\par\vspace{0.92mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0251-body-L029}{}{}{unde formula \Name{al–Battānī}}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0251-H30}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0251-body-L030}{}{}{\CenterLine{$\sin \text{\textgreek{δ}} = \dfrac{\sin L \sin \text{\textgreek{ε}}}{R}$ (2).}}
\par\vspace{6.01mm}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0251-H31}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0251-body-L031}{}{}{\CenterLine{\textbf{\textit{c}}) — \textit{Pag. 13, l. 15–18.}}}
\par\vspace{4.27mm}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0251-P04}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0251-body-L032}{}{}{\Indent Similia apud \Name{Ptolemaeum} non leguntur, sed exstant in \textit{Canone} \Name{Theonis}, ed. Halma,}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0251-body-L033}{}{}{t. I, p. 54: « Nam cum fuerit Sol inter 1° et 90° dicemus eum in Boream versus descendere}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0251-body-L034}{}{}{« [sic, \textgreek{βοῤῥᾶν κατιέναι}]; cum inter 91° et 180° ad Austrum descendere; inter 181° et 270° in}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0251-body-L035}{}{}{« Austrum versus ascendere [sic, \textgreek{νότον ἀνιέναι}]; inter 271° et 360° ad Boream ascendere ».}
\par\vspace{3.27mm}
\par\vspace{6pt}\nointerlineskip\hbox to\textwidth{\hss\vrule width 115.43pt height.25pt depth0pt\hss}\prevdepth=-1000pt \vspace{5pt}
\noindent\begin{minipage}[t]{.48\textwidth}\vspace{0pt}\fontsize{9}{11.5}\selectfont\setlength{\GutterOffset}{0pt}\setlength{\FullSourceWidth}{\linewidth}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0251-N01}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0251-notes_left-L001}{}{}{\Indent (1) Qua cum aequatore ecliptica nunquam con-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0251-notes_left-L002}{}{}{venire censetur.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0251-N02}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0251-notes_left-L003}{}{}{\Indent (2) Radius apud Ptolemaeum et al–Battānī est}
\end{minipage}
\hfill\vrule width.3pt\hfill\begin{minipage}[t]{.48\textwidth}\vspace{0pt}\fontsize{9}{11.5}\selectfont\setlength{\GutterOffset}{0pt}\setlength{\FullSourceWidth}{\linewidth}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0251-notes_right-L001}{}{}{sexaginta partium; in formulis nostris trigonome-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0251-notes_right-L002}{}{}{tricis sumitur contra $R = 1$, qua re iuxta nostrum}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0251-notes_right-L003}{}{}{usum regula auctoris fit $\sin \text{\textgreek{δ}} = \sin L \sin \text{\textgreek{ε}}$.}
\end{minipage}
\DSource{0252}{163}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0252-body-L001}{}{}{Item in tabulis declinationum Solis, sub columnis graduum eclipticae, \Name{Theon}, I, 144}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0252-body-L002}{}{}{scribit:}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0252-body-L003}{}{}{\mbox{}\rlap{\hspace*{31.04mm}A 0° ad 90°:}\rlap{\hspace*{59.62mm}\textgreek{Βοῤῥαῖος καταβαθμός}, descensus borealis.}}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0252-body-L004}{}{}{\mbox{}\rlap{\hspace*{31.04mm}A 90° ad 180°:}\rlap{\hspace*{59.62mm}\textgreek{Νότιος καταβαθμός}, descensus australis.}}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0252-body-L005}{}{}{\mbox{}\rlap{\hspace*{31.04mm}A 180° ad 270°:}\rlap{\hspace*{59.62mm}\textgreek{Νότιος ἀναβαθμός}, ascensus australis.}}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0252-body-L006}{}{}{\mbox{}\rlap{\hspace*{31.04mm}A 270° ad 360°:}\rlap{\hspace*{59.62mm}\textgreek{Βοῤῥαῖος ἀναβαθμός}, ascensus borealis.}}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0252-P01}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0252-body-L007}{}{}{\Indent Ut apud al–Battānī legimus in \textit{Libro de las taulas alfonsies,} cap. XX (\textit{Libros del saber}}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0252-body-L008}{}{}{\textit{de astronomia,} t. IV, p. 136): « et si el logar fuer de un grado fata tres signos. el sol es suuiente}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0252-body-L009}{}{}{« en septentrion. et si fuer de tres signos fata .VI. es decendiente en septentrion. et si fuer}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0252-body-L010}{}{}{« de .VI. signos fata .IX. es decendiente en mediodía. et si fuer de .IX. signos fata .XII. es}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0252-body-L011}{}{}{« suuiente en mediodía ».}
\par\fontsize{15}{17}\selectfont
\par\vspace{5.84mm}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0252-H12}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0252-body-L012}{}{}{\CenterLine{A\textsc{d} \textsc{caput} V.}}
\par\fontsize{11.1}{13.8}\selectfont
\par\vspace{2.65mm}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0252-H13}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0252-body-L013}{}{}{\CenterLine{\textbf{\textit{a}}) — \textit{Pag. 13–14.}}}
\par\vspace{3.33mm}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0252-P02}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0252-body-L014}{}{}{\Indent Calculus \textit{ascensionis rectae}: Sit \textgreek{δ} declinatio dati gradus eclipticae, \textit{\textgreek{α}} ascensio recta eius-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0252-body-L015}{}{}{dem, \textgreek{ε} obliquitas eclipticae; \Name{Ptolemaeus}, \textit{Almag.} I, 13 (ed. Halma, t. I, p. 60–61) per}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0252-body-L016}{}{}{theorema Menelai demonstrat}
\par\vspace{-0.41mm}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0252-H17}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0252-body-L017}{}{}{\CenterLine{$\dfrac{\text{chorda } 2\alpha}{\text{chorda 180°}} = \dfrac{\text{chorda } 2\text{\textgreek{δ}}}{\text{chorda } (\text{180°} - 2\text{\textgreek{δ}})} \times \dfrac{\text{chorda } (\text{180°} - 2\text{\textgreek{ε}})}{\text{chorda } 2\text{\textgreek{ε}}}$.}}
\par\vspace{-0.28mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0252-body-L018}{}{}{Sinibus pro chordis adhibitis, deducitur}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0252-H19}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0252-body-L019}{}{}{\CenterLine{$\dfrac{2 \sin \alpha}{2R} = \dfrac{2 \sin \text{\textgreek{δ}}}{2 \cos \text{\textgreek{δ}}} \times \dfrac{2 \cos \text{\textgreek{ε}}}{2 \sin \text{\textgreek{ε}}}$,}}
\par\vspace{-0.48mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0252-body-L020}{}{}{unde formula \Name{al–Battānī}}
\par\vspace{-1.17mm}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0252-H21}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0252-body-L021}{}{}{\CenterLine{$\sin \alpha = R\,\dfrac{\sin \text{\textgreek{δ}} \cos \text{\textgreek{ε}}}{\cos \text{\textgreek{δ}} \sin \text{\textgreek{ε}}}$.}}
\par\vspace{0.64mm}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0252-P03}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0252-body-L022}{}{}{\Indent \Name{Ḥabash}, qui eadem aetate qua al–Battānī vixit, cum tabulas tangentium et cotangen-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0252-body-L023}{}{}{tium (radio 60 partium sumpto) construxisset, in suo opere (fol. 88,r.) brevius potuit}
\par\vspace{1.51mm}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0252-H24}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0252-body-L024}{}{}{\CenterLine{$\sin \alpha = R\,\mathrm{tg}\,\text{\textgreek{δ}}\,\mathrm{cotg}\,\text{\textgreek{ε}}$}}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0252-body-L025}{}{}{docere.}
\par\vspace{2.90mm}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0252-H26}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0252-body-L026}{}{}{\CenterLine{\textbf{\textit{b}}) — \textit{Pag. 14, l. 10–12.}}}
\par\vspace{3.96mm}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0252-P04}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0252-body-L027}{}{}{\Indent [Calculo ascensionum rectarum cuiuscumque puncti eclipticae cognito, auctor nunc vult}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0252-body-L028}{}{}{ostendere quo modo tempus culminationis eorum punctorum deprehendatur, scilicet gradus}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0252-body-L029}{}{}{aequatoris caelestis qui, culminationis tempore, ab horizonte datae urbis ascendit.}
\par\vspace{-\ht\strutbox}\noindent\begin{minipage}[t]{0.248\FullSourceWidth}\vspace{0pt}\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0252-body-F01}{}\input{figures/AB01-PDF0252-F01}\end{minipage}\hfill\begin{minipage}[t]{0.732\FullSourceWidth}\vspace{0pt}\setlength{\GutterOffset}{0.268\FullSourceWidth}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0252-P05}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0252-body-L030}{}{}{\Indent Sphaera caelestis proiiciatur in planum horizontis FENO}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0252-body-L031}{}{}{datae urbis; sit P polus, PN meridianus, EMO circulus aequi-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0252-body-L032}{}{}{noctialis, {\MoonFont ♈} initium Arietis, F{\MoonFont ♈}C ecliptica, F ascendens, C ini-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0252-body-L033}{}{}{tium Capricorni, PC colurus solstitialis. Necessario {\MoonFont ♈}R, scilicet}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0252-body-L034}{}{}{arcus aequatoris caelestis inter punctum {\MoonFont ♈} aequinoctii vernalis}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0252-body-L035}{}{}{et R coluri solstitialis interceptus, est 90 graduum; item 90°}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0252-body-L036}{}{}{debet semper esse arcus EM, horizonte et meridiano intercep-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0252-body-L037}{}{}{tus. Manifeste erit igitur quocumque tempore E{\MoonFont ♈} = MR. —}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0252-body-L038}{}{}{Sit X punctum eclipticae cuius culminatio quando eveniat scire}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0252-body-L039}{}{}{volumus; erit XC eius longitudo ab initio Capricorni suppu-}
\end{minipage}\par
\DSource{0253}{164}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0253-body-L001}{}{}{tata, MR eius ascensio recta a coluro solstitiali hiemali. Quoniam vidimus MR aequari E{\MoonFont ♈}}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0253-body-L002}{}{}{(quae est ascensio obliqua respondens arcui {\MoonFont ♈}F eclipticae), numerus graduum arcu MR con-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0253-body-L003}{}{}{tentus aequabitur gradui aequatoris tunc ab horizonte ascendenti; idque omnibus Terrae locis.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0253-P01}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0253-body-L004}{}{}{\Indent Calculus arcus MR, cum XC datus sit, facillime reducitur ad supputationem arcus M{\MoonFont ♈}}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0253-body-L005}{}{}{cum X{\MoonFont ♈} sit datus, quod est problema ab al–Battānī parum antea resolutum. Ergo « unus}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0253-body-L006}{}{}{idemque numerus » auctoris significat quocumque tempore quolibet Terrae loco haberi rela-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0253-body-L007}{}{}{tionem}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0253-H08}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0253-body-L008}{}{}{\CenterLine{asc. recta gradus eclipticae X culminantis, a coluro Capricorni supputata =}}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0253-H09}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0253-body-L009}{}{}{\CenterLine{asc. obliqua gradus eclipticae F ascendentis,}}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0253-body-L010}{}{}{id est}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0253-H11}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0253-body-L011}{}{}{\CenterLine{90° + asc. recta gradus culminantis, ab initio Arietis supputata =}}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0253-H12}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0253-body-L012}{}{}{\CenterLine{asc. obliqua gradus ascendentis.}}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0253-P02}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0253-body-L013}{}{}{\Indent Tabulae ergo Albatenianae ascensionum rectarum, fol. 179,v.–181,r., ascensiones a coluro}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0253-body-L014}{}{}{Capricorni supputant, non, ut communis Graecorum et noster est usus, ab initio Arietis. Hoc}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0253-body-L015}{}{}{modo, ut exemplum praebeam, e tabulis statim cognoscitur finem decimi gradus Piscium (i. e.}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0253-body-L016}{}{}{340° ab initio Arietis) culminare eodem tempore quo ascendit punctum circuli aequinoctialis}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0253-body-L017}{}{}{ab aequinoctio vernali 71° 34′ distans. — \textsc{Schiaparelli}.]}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0253-P03}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0253-body-L018}{}{}{\Indent \Name{Ḥabash} ascensiones rectas semper ab initio Arietis supputat, ut ceteri Arabici astronomi}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0253-body-L019}{}{}{hucusque editi facere solent; Abū ‘Alī al–Ḥasan tamen (\Name{Aboul Hhassan}, cap. XXXII,}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0253-body-L020}{}{}{p. 218–227 et in tabulis) usum Albatenianum sequitur. \Name{Ibn Yūnus} (teste \Name{Delambre}, p. 122)}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0253-body-L021}{}{}{duplicem tabulam ascensionum rectarum condidit, id est tam ab initio Arietis quam a coluro}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0253-body-L022}{}{}{solstitiali Capricorni eas supputans; idemque fecit Ulugh Beg (\Name{Oloug Beg}, p. 67 textus,}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0253-body-L023}{}{}{93 vers., in VII cap. secundae partis), qui postea ascensionibus a coluro solstitiali hiemali}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0253-body-L024}{}{}{supputatis utitur in calculis astrologicis directionis significatoris (p. 161 textus, 214 vers.) et}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0253-body-L025}{}{}{proiectionis radiorum (p. 158 textus, 210 vers.), in quibus commodius est distantias a coluro}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0253-body-L026}{}{}{solstitiali quam a coluro aequinoctiali sumere. Tabulas ascensionum rectarum a coluro solsti-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0253-body-L027}{}{}{tiali hiemali incipiendo construxit etiam, pro singulis eclipticae minutis, \Name{‘Abd al–Qādir ibn}}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0253-body-L028}{}{}{\Name{Muḥammad al–Fayyūmī}, anno 1022 heg. (1613–14 Chr.) mortuus, quae in Bibliotheca}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0253-body-L029}{}{}{Kahirensi asservantur; cfr. \Name{Suter}, \textit{Die Mathematiker und Astronomen der Araber,}}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0253-body-L030}{}{}{p. 193, nr. 479.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0253-P04}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0253-body-L031}{}{}{\Indent Ascensiones obliquae autem semper ab Arietis initio supputantur.}
\par\fontsize{15}{17}\selectfont
\par\vspace{9.40mm}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0253-H32}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0253-body-L032}{}{}{\CenterLine{A\textsc{d} \textsc{caput} VI.}}
\par\fontsize{11.1}{13.8}\selectfont
\par\vspace{3.77mm}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0253-H33}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0253-body-L033}{}{}{\CenterLine{\textbf{\textit{a}}) — \textit{Pag. 17, l. 10–11.}}}
\par\vspace{4.26mm}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0253-P05}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0253-body-L034}{}{}{\Indent Male Plato 3500. — Veteres illi igitur gradum circumferentiae terrestris censebant 75}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0253-body-L035}{}{}{milliariorum esse; quae 65 (apud Platonem 85) in fine capitis leguntur error videntur, ut}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0253-body-L036}{}{}{in pag. 19, adn. 8, dixi. Talis gradus origo Graeco–syria est. Milliarium Romanum (1479, 5 me-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0253-body-L037}{}{}{trorum) septem complectitur stadia \textit{philetaeria} (213,048 m.) in Aegypto et Syria, Romano}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0253-body-L038}{}{}{imperio declinante, quam maxime pervulgata. Stadia quorum 180 000 \Name{Ptolemaeus} circum-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0253-body-L039}{}{}{ferentiae terrestri tribuit, scriptores Graeco–syrii philetaeria esse crediderunt; statuerunt igitur}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0253-body-L040}{}{}{aequatorem Terrae 27 000 milliariorum, gradum 75 milliaria complecti. Arabes quidam autem,}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0253-body-L041}{}{}{milliaria Romana cum Arabicis (1973 m.) confundentes, scriptorum Syrorum numeros absque}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0253-body-L042}{}{}{mutatione receperunt. Conferantur quae disserui in \textit{Il valore metrico del grado di meri-}}
\DSource{0254}{165}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0254-body-L001}{}{}{\textit{diano secondo i geografi arabi,} in \textit{Cosmos} di \Name{Guido Cora}, t. XI, Torino 1892, p. 50–53}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0254-body-L002}{}{}{(= p. 9–11 opusculi seorsum editi); addatur quod \Name{Iacobus Edessenus}, anno 708 vel}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0254-body-L003}{}{}{710 Chr. mortuus, in \textit{Hexemerone} suo Syriaco scribit, quosdam gradum 90 milliariorum,}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0254-body-L004}{}{}{alios vero 75 facere (A. \Name{Hjelt}, \textit{Études sur l’Hexaméron de Jacques d’Édesse,} Helsing-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0254-body-L005}{}{}{fors 1892, p. 20).}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0254-P01}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0254-body-L006}{}{}{\Indent Dimensionem gradus meridiani quam, al–Ma’mūn regnante (813–833 Chr.), astronomi}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0254-body-L007}{}{}{Arabici inter urbes Palmyram (Tadmur) et ar–Raqqah, atque in planitie Sinǵār (Mesopota-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0254-body-L008}{}{}{miae) perfecerunt, al–Battānī non memorat. Invenerant illi, inter 35° et 36° circiter latitudinis,}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0254-body-L009}{}{}{gradum 56 $\qfrac{2}{3}$ milliariorum Arabicorum esse, hoc est 111 814,67 m.; errore igitur 877 me-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0254-body-L010}{}{}{trorum tantum lapsi erant. Cfr. quae scripsi \textit{l. c.} p. 53–63, 195-121 (= p. 12–39 opusculi}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0254-body-L011}{}{}{seorsum editi).}
\par\vspace{5.08mm}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0254-H12}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0254-body-L012}{}{}{\CenterLine{\textbf{\textit{b}}) — \textit{Pag. 17, l. 22.}}}
\par\vspace{4.38mm}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0254-P02}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0254-body-L013}{}{}{\Indent Indi insulam \textit{Laṅkā} (Taprobanem, hodie Ceylan), in aequatore posita, medium longitu-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0254-body-L014}{}{}{dinis Terrae locum obtinere censebant; Arabes, traditionem Indicam haud recte intelligentes,}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0254-body-L015}{}{}{meridianum centralem Terrae per insulam, nullo nomine notam et in aequatore inter Indiam}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0254-body-L016}{}{}{Aethiopiamque sitam, transire statuerunt. Locum abscissionis meridiani centralis et aequatoris}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0254-body-L017}{}{}{appellaverunt \textit{qubbat al–arḍ} « fornicem Terrae », ut auctor dicit, vel tantum \textit{al–qubbah}}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0254-body-L018}{}{}{« fornicem, cupolam », unde vox corrupta \textit{Catha} in vetere astrologiae \Name{‘Alī ibn Abī ’r–ri-}}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0254-body-L019}{}{}{\Name{ǵāl} versione Latina (1).}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0254-P03}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0254-body-L020}{}{}{\Indent Nomine quoque \textit{qubbat Uzayn} (vel \textit{Azīn} \ArabicRun{ازين}) innotuit; primum meridianum enim in-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0254-body-L021}{}{}{sulae Laṅkā censebant Indi per celeberrimam apud eos urbem \textit{Ujjayinī} (\textgreek{Ὀζήνη} Ptolemaei,}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0254-body-L022}{}{}{hodie Uǵayn \ArabicRun{اجين} in provincia Mālwa Indiae centralis, 75° 51′ 45″ E Gr., 23° 11′ 10″ lat. N)}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0254-body-L023}{}{}{transire, commerciis florentissimam primis tribus saeculis aerae Christianae, et fortasse prae-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0254-body-L024}{}{}{cipuam studiorum astronomicorum in India sedem. Arabes plerumque \textit{Uzayn} vel \textit{Azīn} (nam,}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0254-body-L025}{}{}{vocalium notis ut mos est omissis, \ArabicRun{ازين} utroque modo legi potest) cum ipsa insula Laṅkā con-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0254-body-L026}{}{}{fuderunt, et in aequatore posuerunt; seriores, puncto in scriptura omisso, \textit{Arīn} (\ArabicRun{ارين}) pronun-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0254-body-L027}{}{}{tiaverunt, unde \textit{Arin}, \textit{Arim, Arym} apud nostrates aevi medii. Legantur quae optime disse-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0254-body-L028}{}{}{ruit \Name{Reinaud}, \textit{Introduction générale à la Géographie} d’\Name{Aboulféda}, Paris 1848, pag.}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0254-body-L029}{}{}{CCXXXVI–CCLIV; ex Arabibus est praesertim \Name{al-Bīrūnī} (\textit{India,} p. 158–159 textus, I,}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0254-body-L030}{}{}{p. 306–308 versionis Anglicae) consulendus.}
\par\vspace{4.87mm}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0254-H31}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0254-body-L031}{}{}{\CenterLine{\textbf{\textit{c}}) — \textit{Pag. 17, l. 22–27.}}}
\par\vspace{4.33mm}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0254-P04}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0254-body-L032}{}{}{\Indent Iam in versione notavi lineas 7–25 iisdem verbis apud \Name{al–Mas‘ūdī}, \textit{Prairies,} t. I,}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0254-body-L033}{}{}{p. 179–181, legi, qui ea « sapientibus » (\textit{al–ḥukamā’}) tribuit; al–Battānī autem anonymis}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0254-body-L034}{}{}{« veteribus » (cfr. l. 3). Videbimus in adnotatione \textbf{\textit{d}} primam huius VI capitis fontem esse}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0254-body-L035}{}{}{Graeco–syriam. Auctor noster relata refert, nec probans nec improbans, ut in cap. L (p. 120–}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0254-body-L036}{}{}{–123) de distantiis et magnitudinibus sphaerarum et corporum caelestium; mirum tamen eum}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0254-body-L037}{}{}{duos crassos errores harum linearum non reiecisse. [Superficies enim quadrilateri sphaerici}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0254-body-L038}{}{}{duobus parallelis duobusque meridianis definiti non invenitur amplitudinem angularem latitu-}
\par\vspace{3.05mm}
\par\vspace{6pt}\nointerlineskip\hbox to\textwidth{\hss\vrule width 115.43pt height.25pt depth0pt\hss}\prevdepth=-1000pt \vspace{5pt}
\noindent\begin{minipage}[t]{.48\textwidth}\vspace{0pt}\fontsize{9}{11.5}\selectfont\setlength{\GutterOffset}{0pt}\setlength{\FullSourceWidth}{\linewidth}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0254-N01}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0254-notes_left-L001}{}{}{\Indent (1) \Name{Albohazen Haly filii Abenragel,} \textit{De}}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0254-notes_left-L002}{}{}{\textit{iudiciis astrorum libri octo, doctorum aliquot vi-}}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0254-notes_left-L003}{}{}{\textit{rorum opera in Latinum sermonem conversi, po-}}
\end{minipage}
\hfill\vrule width.3pt\hfill\begin{minipage}[t]{.48\textwidth}\vspace{0pt}\fontsize{9}{11.5}\selectfont\setlength{\GutterOffset}{0pt}\setlength{\FullSourceWidth}{\linewidth}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0254-notes_right-L001}{}{}{\textit{stremo a barbarie vindicati et puritati linguae}}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0254-notes_right-L002}{}{}{\textit{donati, per Antonium Stupam.} Basileae 1571, lib.VIII,}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0254-notes_right-L003}{}{}{cap. 8, p. 374.}
\end{minipage}
\DSource{0255}{166}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0255-body-L001}{}{}{dinis in amplitudinem angularem longitudinis multiplicando, cum gradus alterius paralleli in}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0255-body-L002}{}{}{mensuris linearibus necessario differant a gradibus alterius. Sed etiamsi areas ut auctor dicit}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0255-body-L003}{}{}{supputare liceret, calculus falsus esset; dimidium circumferentiae non in sextam partem omnium}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0255-body-L004}{}{}{360°, sed in tertiam eorum 180° quos latitudo a polo ad polum complectitur multiplicari de-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0255-body-L005}{}{}{beret, qua re superficies non $\qfrac{1}{12}$ sed $\qfrac{1}{6}$ Terrae inveniretur (1). — Area zonae sphaericae}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0255-body-L006}{}{}{aequatore et parallelo 60 graduum interceptae est ad sphaeram totam ut sinus 60° est ad}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0255-body-L007}{}{}{diametrum; ergo dimidium illius zonae erit ad sphaeram totam ut sinus 60° (i. e. $\qfrac{1}{2}$$\smash{\sqrt{3}}$}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0255-body-L008}{}{}{vel 0,8660) ad duplam diametrum (i. e. 4, cum radius = 1 sumptus sit). Pro 0,8660 : 4 seu}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0255-body-L009}{}{}{0,2165 : 1 veteres illi 1 : 12 seu 0,0833 : 1 invenerunt, quod est ter fere minus veritate. —}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0255-body-L010}{}{}{\textsc{Schiaparelli}.]}
\par\vspace{5.25mm}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0255-H11}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0255-body-L011}{}{}{\CenterLine{\textbf{\textit{d}}) — \textit{Pag. 17–19.}}}
\par\vspace{4.48mm}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0255-P01}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0255-body-L012}{}{}{\Indent \textit{Marium terrarumque descriptio.} — In versione iam suis locis indicavi auctores Arabi-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0255-body-L013}{}{}{cos qui ex eodem ignoto fonte hauserunt a quo al–Battānī notitias suas derivavit; dixique}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0255-body-L014}{}{}{auctoris nostri locum iam a \Name{Reinaud} editum et Gallice versum esse. Restat ut quis sit}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0255-body-L015}{}{}{hic fons communis investigemus.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0255-P02}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0255-body-L016}{}{}{\Indent Antiquissimi omnium Arabicorum scriptorum qui in libris suis, hodie quoque exstantihus,}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0255-body-L017}{}{}{talia de maribus narrent sunt \Name{Ibn Rosteh}, qui inter annos 285 et 300 heg. (898–913 Chr.)}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0255-body-L018}{}{}{scripsit, et \Name{Qodāmah ibn Ǵa‘far}, anno 337 heg. (inc. 10 Iulii 948) mortuus; al–Kharaqī}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0255-body-L019}{}{}{autem, ut infra videbimus, notitias suas ab \Name{al-Ǵayhānī} (qui paulo post 301 heg., 913–14 Chr.}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0255-body-L020}{}{}{scripsit) « aliisque sapientibus » se mutuatum esse dicit. Omnes ergo pertinent ad aetatem}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0255-body-L021}{}{}{nostri. Sed maiorem lucem nobis locus \Name{al–Mas‘ūdī}, \textit{Tanbīh,} p. 51, affert; de mari Indico}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0255-body-L022}{}{}{haec enim narrat: « Iuxta eos qui operam dederunt metiendae et effingendae Terrae secun-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0255-body-L023}{}{}{« dum eius loca in longitudinibus et latitudinibus astronomicis, longitudo huius maris ab ortu}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0255-body-L024}{}{}{« ad occasum 8000 milliariorum complectitur; latitudo in septentriones 2700, et dicitur [etiam]}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0255-body-L025}{}{}{« 1900. Talia referunt \Name{Ptolemaeus} atque alii qui ante et post eum vixerunt; ultimus qui}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0255-body-L026}{}{}{« haec in islāmismo assecutus sit, est \Name{Ya‘qūb ibn Isḥāq al–Kindī} in epistula sua de ma-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0255-body-L027}{}{}{« ribus eorumque accessu et recessu, et eius discipulus \Name{Aḥmad ibn aṭ-Ṭayyib} in epistula}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0255-body-L028}{}{}{« de utilitatibus marium, montium et fluviorum ». Eosdem \Name{al–Mas‘ūdī}, \textit{Prairies,} t. I, p. 276,}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0255-body-L029}{}{}{memorat, ubi de latitudine maris Romani loquitur (2). Attingimus hoc modo tempus aetatem}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0255-body-L030}{}{}{al–Battānī praecedens; nam al–Kindī, qui pro se versionem Geographiae Ptolemaei facere man-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0255-body-L031}{}{}{daverat (3), anno circiter 260 heg. (inc. 26 Octob. 873) obiit; eius discipulus Aḥmad ibn aṭ-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0255-body-L032}{}{}{–Ṭayyib as–Sarakhsī anno 286 (inc. 16 Ian. 899).}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0255-P03}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0255-body-L033}{}{}{\Indent Consideretur nunc gradum 75 milliariorum a scriptoribus Graeco–syriis originem ducere,}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0255-body-L034}{}{}{ut in adn. \textbf{\textit{a}} ostendi; addatur quod in secunda huius operis parte demonstrabo (4), in tabulis}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0255-body-L035}{}{}{nempe Albatenianis longitudinum et latitudinum urbium vestigia Syriaca haud dubia agnosci;}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0255-body-L036}{}{}{coniectura non erit temeraria Arabes veteres a \Name{Syris} notitias illas geographicas illasque}
\par\vspace{2.19mm}
\par\vspace{6pt}\nointerlineskip\hbox to\textwidth{\hss\vrule width 115.43pt height.25pt depth0pt\hss}\prevdepth=-1000pt \vspace{5pt}
\noindent\begin{minipage}[t]{.48\textwidth}\vspace{0pt}\fontsize{9}{11.5}\selectfont\setlength{\GutterOffset}{0pt}\setlength{\FullSourceWidth}{\linewidth}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0255-N01}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0255-notes_left-L001}{}{}{\Indent (1) Error denuo pag. 19, l. 21, legitur.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0255-N02}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0255-notes_left-L002}{}{}{\Indent (2) Alibi (\textit{Prairies,} I, p. 256), de Romanorum}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0255-notes_left-L003}{}{}{mari iterum loquens, al-Battānī laudat: « Multi ta-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0255-notes_left-L004}{}{}{« bularum astronomicarum auctores, inter quos sunt}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0255-notes_left-L005}{}{}{« Muḥammad ibn Ǵābir \Name{al-Battānī} aliique, dicunt}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0255-notes_left-L006}{}{}{« in operibus astronomicis suis eius longitudinem}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0255-notes_left-L007}{}{}{« 5000 milliariorum esse, eiusque latitudinem variare}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0255-notes_left-L008}{}{}{« ab 800 ad 700 et 600 milliaria ».}
\end{minipage}
\hfill\vrule width.3pt\hfill\begin{minipage}[t]{.48\textwidth}\vspace{0pt}\fontsize{9}{11.5}\selectfont\setlength{\GutterOffset}{0pt}\setlength{\FullSourceWidth}{\linewidth}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0255-N03}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0255-notes_right-L001}{}{}{\Indent (3) Cfr. meum \textit{al-Ḫuwârizmî e il suo rifaci-}}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0255-notes_right-L002}{}{}{\textit{mento della Geografia di Tolomeo,} pag. 6, adn. 10}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0255-notes_right-L003}{}{}{(Memorie della R. Accademia dei Lincei, Classe di}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0255-notes_right-L004}{}{}{scienze morali, ser. V, t. II, 1894).}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0255-N04}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0255-notes_right-L005}{}{}{\Indent (4) Id iam exposui in opusculo meo \textit{Le tabelle}}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0255-notes_right-L006}{}{}{\textit{geografiche d’al-Battânî tradotte ed annotate,} To-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0255-notes_right-L007}{}{}{rino 1898, p. 3 (= Cosmos di Guido Cora, ser. II,}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0255-notes_right-L008}{}{}{t. XII, p. 163).}
\end{minipage}
\DSource{0256}{167}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0256-body-L001}{}{}{mensuras marium accepisse. Coniectura autem certissima fit, cum re vera omnes hae men-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0256-body-L002}{}{}{surae iam apud celebrem scriptorem Syrum \Name{Iacobum Edessenum} (1) reperiantur.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0256-P01}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0256-body-L003}{}{}{\Indent Si nunc quaeramus unde Graeco–Syrii haec omnia collexerint, non possumus nisi verbis}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0256-body-L004}{}{}{supra adductis \Name{al-Mas‘ūdī} adsentiri, ea nempe a \textit{Geographia} \Name{Ptolemaei} primam originem}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0256-body-L005}{}{}{trahere. Milliaria in gradus convertantur, gradu 75 milliariorum sumpto, nulloque discrimine}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0256-body-L006}{}{}{facto inter gradum meridiani et gradus parallelorum (quem errorem vidimus in adn. \textbf{\textit{c}} au-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0256-body-L007}{}{}{ctores illos iam fecisse); invenietur omnia perfecte cum mappa \Name{Ptolemaei} convenire. Quod}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0256-body-L008}{}{}{mirum est \Name{Reinaud} (2) et \Name{Lelewel} (3) non solum non agnovisse, sed interdum negasse;}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0256-body-L009}{}{}{prior enim, lectionem codicis quae mari Indico 1900 (pro 900) milliaria latitudinis ultra aequa-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0256-body-L010}{}{}{torem tribuit considerans, scribit, p. CCLXXXIV: « Mille et neuf cents milles, à raison de}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0256-body-L011}{}{}{« soixante et quinze milles par degré, donnaient un peu plus de vingt–cinq degrés. Voilà un}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0256-body-L012}{}{}{« des motifs qui me font croire qu’Albateny n’a pas admis le système de Ptolémée, relative-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0256-body-L013}{}{}{« ment à la prolongation du continent africain du côté de l’est ». Qua re in delineanda ima-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0256-body-L014}{}{}{gine mundi ad mentem al-Battānī (vel potius veterum Graecorum et \Name{Syrorum} quos al–Battānī}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0256-body-L015}{}{}{transcripsit) ponit littus Africanum, statim ab austro aequatoris, ab extrema Ṣomālorum penin-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0256-body-L016}{}{}{sula in occidentem linea fere recta verti et cum littore Oceani Atlantici coniungi. \Name{Lelewel},}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0256-body-L017}{}{}{t. I, p. 37, adn. 86, haec oppugnat, inter alias considerationes iure observans quod latitudinem}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0256-body-L018}{}{}{Oceani Indici determinare idem est ac terram ab eius austro ponere; sed postea addit: « Mais}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0256-body-L019}{}{}{« nourrir l’idée d’une mer interne Hind, n’est pas suivre le système de Ptolémée ou copier}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0256-body-L020}{}{}{« sa carte, et je désire disculper Albateni d’une semblable accusation ». Et p. 37, adnot. 83,}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0256-body-L021}{}{}{aliquot mensuris Albatenianis marium relatis, perperam omnino observat: « Comptant 75 mil-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0256-body-L022}{}{}{« les au degré, ces nombres paraissent trop forts même pour le système de Ptolémée. La}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0256-body-L023}{}{}{« longueur des deux mers intérieurs [Romanorum nempe et Indici], 5000 et 8000, ferait}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0256-body-L024}{}{}{« évaluer la longueur de l’habitable sur le grand cercle à 185 $\qfrac{1}{3}$ degrés [sic, sed 13000 : 75}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0256-body-L025}{}{}{« = 173 $\qfrac{1}{3}$], à quoi il faudrait ajouter quelques degrés de plus. Pour adapter ces nombres}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0256-body-L026}{}{}{« à la carte ptoléméenne, il faudrait compter au moins 80 milles au degré du grand cercle ».}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0256-body-L027}{}{}{Error igitur arithmeticae divisionis fortasse fuit praecipua causa qua Lelewel originem Pto-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0256-body-L028}{}{}{lemaicam huius capitis al–Battānī negaverit; alia causa minor fuit quod eo tempore scriptores}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0256-body-L029}{}{}{Arabici et Syrii easdem mensuras indicantes ignorabantur.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0256-P02}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0256-body-L030}{}{}{\Indent Ut melius omnia quae dixi de communi, eaque \Name{Ptolemaica}, origine descriptionum}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0256-body-L031}{}{}{marium ostendam, atque numeros auctoris nostri passim corrigere liceat, conspectum nunc}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0256-body-L032}{}{}{praebebo mensurarum ab al–Battānī (cum quo Ibn Rosteh convenit), al-Ǵayhānī (iuxta al–Kha-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0256-body-L033}{}{}{raqī) et Iacobo Edesseno relatarum, ceteris Arabicis scriptoribus quoque collatis (4).}
\par\vspace{3.78mm}
\par\fontsize{9.5}{8.6}\selectfont
\DLine{AB01-PDF0256-body-L034}{}{}{\mbox{}\rlap{\hspace*{68.65mm}\smash{\makebox[0pt][c]{al-Battānī}}}\rlap{\hspace*{100.26mm}\smash{\makebox[0pt][c]{al-Ǵayhānī}}}\rlap{\hspace*{132.72mm}\smash{\raisebox{-.55\baselineskip}{\makebox[0pt][c]{Iacobus Edessenus}}}}}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0256-body-L035}{}{}{\mbox{}\rlap{\hspace*{68.65mm}\smash{\makebox[0pt][c]{et Ibn Rosteh}}}\rlap{\hspace*{100.26mm}\smash{\makebox[0pt][c]{apud al-Kharaqī}}}}
\par\fontsize{11.1}{13.8}\selectfont
\par\vspace{-1.44mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0256-body-L036}{}{}{\Name{Terra habitabilis:}}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0256-body-L037}{}{}{\mbox{}\rlap{\hspace*{8.29mm}\smash{\rlap{longitudo}}}\rlap{\hspace*{63.41mm}\smash{\llap{13.500}}}\rlap{\hspace*{65.51mm}\smash{\rlap{[= 180°]}}}\rlap{\hspace*{105.09mm}\smash{\llap{13.500}}}\rlap{\hspace*{130.25mm}\smash{\llap{13.500}}}}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0256-body-L038}{}{}{\mbox{}\rlap{\hspace*{8.29mm}\smash{\rlap{latitudo}}}\rlap{\hspace*{63.41mm}\smash{\llap{. . . .}}}\rlap{\hspace*{65.51mm}\smash{\rlap{[= 60°]}}}\rlap{\hspace*{105.09mm}\smash{\llap{. . . .}}}\rlap{\hspace*{130.25mm}\smash{\llap{. . . .}}}\rlap{\hspace*{132.29mm}\smash{\rlap{[= 60°]}}}}
\par\vspace{6.59mm}
\par\vspace{6pt}\nointerlineskip\hbox to\textwidth{\hss\vrule width 115.43pt height.25pt depth0pt\hss}\prevdepth=-1000pt \vspace{5pt}
\noindent\begin{minipage}[t]{.48\textwidth}\vspace{0pt}\fontsize{9}{11.5}\selectfont\setlength{\GutterOffset}{0pt}\setlength{\FullSourceWidth}{\linewidth}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0256-N01}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0256-notes_left-L001}{}{}{\Indent (1) Natus est anno 633 Chr. circiter, obiit 708}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0256-notes_left-L002}{}{}{vel 710. Pars geographica Syriace et Latine legitur}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0256-notes_left-L003}{}{}{apud A. \Name{Hjelt}, \textit{Études sur l’Hexaméron de} \Name{Jac-}}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0256-notes_left-L004}{}{}{\Name{ques} d’\Name{Édesse}, \textit{notamment sur ses notions géo-}}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0256-notes_left-L005}{}{}{\textit{graphiques contenues dans le 3\textsuperscript{me} Traité,} Helsing-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0256-notes_left-L006}{}{}{fors 1892.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0256-N02}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0256-notes_left-L007}{}{}{\Indent (2) In libro quem p. 17, adn. 1, indicavi.}
\end{minipage}
\hfill\vrule width.3pt\hfill\begin{minipage}[t]{.48\textwidth}\vspace{0pt}\fontsize{9}{11.5}\selectfont\setlength{\GutterOffset}{0pt}\setlength{\FullSourceWidth}{\linewidth}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0256-N03}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0256-notes_right-L001}{}{}{\Indent (3) J. \Name{Lelewel}, \textit{Géographie du moyen âge,}}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0256-notes_right-L002}{}{}{Breslau 1852, t. I: Cartes de géographes, p. 36-38.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0256-N04}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0256-notes_right-L003}{}{}{\Indent (4) Fragmenta quae apud \Name{Qodāmah} p. 230–}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0256-notes_right-L004}{}{}{-231 leguntur, respondent p. 17, l. 29-33 (usque ad}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0256-notes_right-L005}{}{}{numerum 200), p. 18, l. 9-16, 20-24 (milliaribus cir-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0256-notes_right-L006}{}{}{cuitus insularum omissis), et p. 19, l. 2-4, versionis}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0256-notes_right-L007}{}{}{meae al-Battānī.}
\end{minipage}
\DSource{0257}{168}
\par\fontsize{9.5}{8.6}\selectfont
\DLine{AB01-PDF0257-body-L001}{}{}{\mbox{}\rlap{\hspace*{68.65mm}\smash{\makebox[0pt][c]{al-Battānī}}}\rlap{\hspace*{100.26mm}\smash{\makebox[0pt][c]{al-Ǵayhānī}}}\rlap{\hspace*{132.72mm}\smash{\raisebox{-.55\baselineskip}{\makebox[0pt][c]{Iacobus Edessenus}}}}}

\par\fontsize{11.1}{13.8}\selectfont
\par\vspace{-1.44mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0257-body-L003}{}{}{\Name{Mare Indorum} (1):}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0257-body-L004}{}{}{\mbox{}\rlap{\hspace*{8.29mm}\smash{\rlap{longitudo}}}\rlap{\hspace*{63.41mm}\smash{\llap{8000}}}\rlap{\hspace*{65.51mm}\smash{\rlap{[= 106° $\qfrac{2}{3}$]}}}\rlap{\hspace*{105.09mm}\smash{\llap{8000}}}\rlap{\hspace*{130.25mm}\smash{\llap{8000}}}}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0257-body-L005}{}{}{\mbox{}\rlap{\hspace*{8.29mm}\smash{\rlap{latitudo tota,}}}\rlap{\hspace*{63.41mm}\smash{\llap{2700}}}\rlap{\hspace*{65.51mm}\smash{\rlap{[= 36°]}}}\rlap{\hspace*{105.09mm}\smash{\llap{2700}}}\rlap{\hspace*{130.25mm}\smash{\llap{2700}}}}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0257-body-L006}{}{}{\mbox{}\rlap{\hspace*{8.29mm}\smash{\rlap{cuius ab aequatoris austro}}}\rlap{\hspace*{63.41mm}\smash{\llap{1900}}}\rlap{\hspace*{65.51mm}\smash{\rlap{[= 25° $\qfrac{1}{3}$]}}}\rlap{\hspace*{105.09mm}\smash{\llap{1700}}}\rlap{\hspace*{130.25mm}\smash{\llap{900}}}\rlap{\hspace*{132.29mm}\smash{\rlap{[= 12°]}}}}
\par\vspace{0.86mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0257-body-L007}{}{}{\Name{Sinus Barbaricus} (2):}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0257-body-L008}{}{}{\mbox{}\rlap{\hspace*{8.29mm}\smash{\rlap{longitudo}}}\rlap{\hspace*{63.41mm}\smash{\llap{500}}}\rlap{\hspace*{65.51mm}\smash{\rlap{[= 6° $\qfrac{2}{3}$]}}}\rlap{\hspace*{105.09mm}\smash{\llap{500}}}\rlap{\hspace*{130.25mm}\smash{\llap{. . . .}}}}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0257-body-L009}{}{}{\mbox{}\rlap{\hspace*{8.29mm}\smash{\rlap{latitudo}}}\rlap{\hspace*{63.41mm}\smash{\llap{100}}}\rlap{\hspace*{65.51mm}\smash{\rlap{[= 1° $\qfrac{1}{3}$]}}}\rlap{\hspace*{105.09mm}\smash{\llap{100}}}\rlap{\hspace*{130.25mm}\smash{\llap{. . . .}}}}
\par\vspace{0.81mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0257-body-L010}{}{}{\Name{Mare al–Qolzum} (3):}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0257-body-L011}{}{}{\mbox{}\rlap{\hspace*{8.29mm}\smash{\rlap{longitudo}}}\rlap{\hspace*{63.41mm}\smash{\llap{1400}}}\rlap{\hspace*{65.51mm}\smash{\rlap{[= 18° $\qfrac{2}{3}$]}}}\rlap{\hspace*{105.09mm}\smash{\llap{1400}}}\rlap{\hspace*{130.25mm}\smash{\llap{1400}}}}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0257-body-L012}{}{}{\mbox{}\rlap{\hspace*{8.29mm}\smash{\rlap{latitudo in extremitate}}}\rlap{\hspace*{63.41mm}\smash{\llap{200}}}\rlap{\hspace*{65.51mm}\smash{\rlap{[= 2° $\qfrac{2}{3}$]}}}\rlap{\hspace*{105.09mm}\smash{\llap{200}}}\rlap{\hspace*{130.25mm}\smash{\llap{200}}}}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0257-body-L013}{}{}{\mbox{}\rlap{\hspace*{8.29mm}\smash{\rlap{latitudo in originis loco}}}\rlap{\hspace*{63.41mm}\smash{\llap{700}}}\rlap{\hspace*{65.51mm}\smash{\rlap{[= 9° $\qfrac{1}{3}$]}}}\rlap{\hspace*{105.09mm}\smash{\llap{700}}}\rlap{\hspace*{130.25mm}\smash{\llap{400}}}\rlap{\hspace*{132.29mm}\smash{\rlap{[= 5° $\qfrac{1}{3}$]}}}}
\par\vspace{0.88mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0257-body-L014}{}{}{\Name{Mare al–Baṣrah} (4):}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0257-body-L015}{}{}{\mbox{}\rlap{\hspace*{8.29mm}\smash{\rlap{longitudo}}}\rlap{\hspace*{63.41mm}\smash{\llap{1400}}}\rlap{\hspace*{65.51mm}\smash{\rlap{[= 18° $\qfrac{2}{3}$]}}}\rlap{\hspace*{105.09mm}\smash{\llap{1400}}}\rlap{\hspace*{130.25mm}\smash{\llap{1400}}}}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0257-body-L016}{}{}{\mbox{}\rlap{\hspace*{8.29mm}\smash{\rlap{latitudo in originis loco}}}\rlap{\hspace*{63.41mm}\smash{\llap{500}}}\rlap{\hspace*{65.51mm}\smash{\rlap{[= 6° $\qfrac{2}{3}$]}}}\rlap{\hspace*{105.09mm}\smash{\llap{500}}}\rlap{\hspace*{106.30mm}\smash{\rlap{\smash{\raisebox{-16.6pt}{\tikz\draw[line width=.55pt,decorate,decoration={brace,amplitude=2.3pt}] (0,25.8pt) -- (0,0);}}}}}\rlap{\hspace*{130.25mm}\smash{\raisebox{-.55\baselineskip}{\llap{700}}}}\rlap{\hspace*{132.29mm}\smash{\raisebox{-.55\baselineskip}{\rlap{[= 9° $\qfrac{1}{3}$]}}}}}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0257-body-L017}{}{}{\mbox{}\rlap{\hspace*{8.29mm}\smash{\rlap{latitudo recto itinere}}}\rlap{\hspace*{63.41mm}\smash{\llap{150}}}\rlap{\hspace*{65.51mm}\smash{\rlap{[= 2°]}}}\rlap{\hspace*{105.09mm}\smash{\llap{150}}}}
\par\vspace{0.78mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0257-body-L018}{}{}{\Name{Terra inter mare al–Qolzum et sinum Persicum:}}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0257-body-L019}{}{}{\mbox{}\rlap{\hspace*{8.29mm}\smash{\rlap{longitudo}}}\rlap{\hspace*{63.41mm}\smash{\llap{1500}}}\rlap{\hspace*{65.51mm}\smash{\rlap{[= 20°]}}}\rlap{\hspace*{105.09mm}\smash{\llap{1500}}}\rlap{\hspace*{130.25mm}\smash{\llap{. . . .}}}}
\par\vspace{0.76mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0257-body-L020}{}{}{\Name{Sinus viridis:}}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0257-body-L021}{}{}{\mbox{}\rlap{\hspace*{8.29mm}\smash{\rlap{longitudo}}}\rlap{\hspace*{63.41mm}\smash{\llap{1500}}}\rlap{\hspace*{65.51mm}\smash{\rlap{[= 20°]}}}\rlap{\hspace*{105.09mm}\smash{\llap{1500}}}\rlap{\hspace*{130.25mm}\smash{\llap{. . . .}}}}
\par\vspace{0.96mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0257-body-L022}{}{}{\Name{Mare Romanorum et Aegypti} (5):}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0257-body-L023}{}{}{\mbox{}\rlap{\hspace*{8.29mm}\smash{\rlap{longitudo}}}\rlap{\hspace*{63.41mm}\smash{\llap{5000}}}\rlap{\hspace*{65.51mm}\smash{\rlap{[= 66° $\qfrac{2}{3}$]}}}\rlap{\hspace*{105.09mm}\smash{\llap{5000}}}\rlap{\hspace*{130.25mm}\smash{\llap{5000}}}}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0257-body-L024}{}{}{\mbox{}\rlap{\hspace*{63.41mm}\smash{\llap{600}}}\rlap{\hspace*{65.51mm}\smash{\rlap{[= 8°]}}}\rlap{\hspace*{105.09mm}\smash{\llap{600}}}}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0257-body-L025}{}{}{\mbox{}\rlap{\hspace*{8.29mm}\smash{\rlap{latitudo}}}\rlap{\hspace*{53.50mm}\smash{\rlap{\smash{\raisebox{-16pt}{\tikz\draw[line width=.55pt,decorate,decoration={brace,amplitude=2.3pt}] (0,0) -- (0,37.6pt);}}}}}\rlap{\hspace*{63.41mm}\smash{\llap{700}}}\rlap{\hspace*{65.51mm}\smash{\rlap{[= 9° $\qfrac{1}{3}$]}}}\rlap{\hspace*{105.09mm}\smash{\llap{700}}}\rlap{\hspace*{106.30mm}\smash{\rlap{\smash{\raisebox{-16pt}{\tikz\draw[line width=.55pt,decorate,decoration={brace,amplitude=2.3pt}] (0,37.6pt) -- (0,0);}}}}}\rlap{\hspace*{130.25mm}\smash{\llap{400}}}\rlap{\hspace*{132.29mm}\smash{\rlap{[= 5° $\qfrac{1}{3}$]}}}}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0257-body-L026}{}{}{\mbox{}\rlap{\hspace*{63.41mm}\smash{\llap{800}}}\rlap{\hspace*{65.51mm}\smash{\rlap{[= 10° $\qfrac{2}{3}$]}}}\rlap{\hspace*{105.09mm}\smash{\llap{800}}}}
\par\vspace{2.45mm}
\par\vspace{6pt}\nointerlineskip\hbox to\textwidth{\hss\vrule width 115.43pt height.25pt depth0pt\hss}\prevdepth=-1000pt \vspace{5pt}
\noindent\begin{minipage}[t]{.48\textwidth}\vspace{0pt}\fontsize{9}{11.5}\selectfont\setlength{\GutterOffset}{0pt}\setlength{\FullSourceWidth}{\linewidth}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0257-N01}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0257-notes_left-L001}{}{}{\Indent (1) \Name{Ibn Rosteh}, \Name{Qodāmah}, \Name{al-Mas‘ūdī},}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0257-notes_left-L002}{}{}{ut p. 17, adn. 4, dixi, numeros al-Battānī confirmant;}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0257-notes_left-L003}{}{}{1700 apud \Name{al-Kharaqī} est amanuensis error (\ArabicRun{سبع}}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0257-notes_left-L004}{}{}{7 pro \ArabicRun{تسع} 9) in codicibus Arabicis perfrequens. Fons}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0257-notes_left-L005}{}{}{Arabicus (al-Kindī?) ergo iam 1900 habebat; sed vera}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0257-notes_left-L006}{}{}{lectio antiquissima est 900 ut ex mappa \Name{Ptolemaei}}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0257-notes_left-L007}{}{}{et \Name{Iacobo Edesseno} patet (« in latitudinem circa}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0257-notes_left-L008}{}{}{« 900 milliaria a loco aequinoctiali meridiem versus}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0257-notes_left-L009}{}{}{« irrumpit usque ad terram a terminis eius austra-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0257-notes_left-L010}{}{}{« libus versus meridiem sitam, quae ab hominibus}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0257-notes_left-L011}{}{}{« incognita appellatur et prorsus inhabitabilis est »,}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0257-notes_left-L012}{}{}{\Name{Hjelt}, \textit{L’Examéron,} p. LXVI). Cfr. locum \Name{al-Mas-}}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0257-notes_left-L013}{}{}{\Name{‘ūdī}, \textit{Tanbīh,} p. 51, supra (p. 166) adductum.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0257-N02}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0257-notes_left-L014}{}{}{\Indent (2) Est \Name{Ptolemaei} \textgreek{Βαρβαρικὸν πέλαγος}, sed}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0257-notes_left-L015}{}{}{apud hos Arabes sinum ‘Aden designare videtur.}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0257-notes_left-L016}{}{}{\Name{Ptolemaeus} \textgreek{Βαρβαρία} appellat littora quae a pro-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0257-notes_left-L017}{}{}{montorio Guardafui ad insulam Zanzibar protendun-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0257-notes_left-L018}{}{}{tur. — Iidem numeri apud \Name{al-Mas‘ūdī}, \textit{Prairies,}}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0257-notes_left-L019}{}{}{I, 231, et \Name{Barhebraeum}, p. 135 textus.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0257-N03}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0257-notes_left-L020}{}{}{\Indent (3) Latitudo 700 milliariorum est quoque apud}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0257-notes_left-L021}{}{}{\Name{Ibn Rosteh} et \Name{al-Mas‘ūdī}, \textit{Prairies,} I, 237; est}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0257-notes_left-L022}{}{}{ergo lectio antiqui fontis Arabici. Sed rectius \Name{Iaco-}}
\end{minipage}
\hfill\vrule width.3pt\hfill\begin{minipage}[t]{.48\textwidth}\vspace{0pt}\fontsize{9}{11.5}\selectfont\setlength{\GutterOffset}{0pt}\setlength{\FullSourceWidth}{\linewidth}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0257-notes_right-L001}{}{}{\Name{bus Edessenus} et Barhebraeus (l. l.) 400. —}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0257-notes_right-L002}{}{}{De hoc mari cfr. adn. \textit{e}.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0257-N04}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0257-notes_right-L003}{}{}{\Indent (4) \Name{Ibn Rosteh} eosdem habet numeros; item-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0257-notes_right-L004}{}{}{que \Name{al-Mas‘ūdī}, \textit{Prairies,} I, 238, scribit: « huius}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0257-notes_right-L005}{}{}{« sinus longitudo est 1400 milliariorum; latitudo in}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0257-notes_right-L006}{}{}{« originis loco 500, sed interdum latitudo eius dua-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0257-notes_right-L007}{}{}{« rum extremitatum fit 150 milliariorum ». Sed « eius}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0257-notes_right-L008}{}{}{« duarum extremitatum » (\ArabicRun{طرفيه}) manifeste error}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0257-notes_right-L009}{}{}{scripturae est pro pristino « recto itinere eius »}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0257-notes_right-L010}{}{}{(\ArabicRun{طريقه}) quod apud al-Battānī legitur. Idem error in}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0257-notes_right-L011}{}{}{alio al-Mas‘ūdī loco; v. adn. \textit{e}.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0257-N05}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0257-notes_right-L012}{}{}{\Indent (5) Iidem numeri apud \Name{Ibn Rosteh}; \Name{al-Mas-}}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0257-notes_right-L013}{}{}{\Name{‘ūdī}, \textit{Tanbīh,} p. 56-57; \Name{Barhebraeum}, p. 135}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0257-notes_right-L014}{}{}{textus. In \textit{Prairies,} I, 256 legimus: « Multi tabula-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0257-notes_right-L015}{}{}{« rum astronomicarum auctores, e quibus Muḥam-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0257-notes_right-L016}{}{}{« mad ibn Ǵābir al-Battānī aliique, in libris suis de}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0257-notes_right-L017}{}{}{« stellis dicunt eius longitudinem 5000 milliariorum}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0257-notes_right-L018}{}{}{« esse, latitudinem variare a 800 ad 700 et 600 ».}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0257-notes_right-L019}{}{}{Eosdem numeros contra \Name{Iacobi Edesseni} refert,}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0257-notes_right-L020}{}{}{ex auctoritate al-Kindī et Aḥmad ibn aṭ-Ṭayyib}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0257-notes_right-L021}{}{}{as-Sarakhsī, ipse \Name{al-Mas‘ūdī}, \textit{Prairies,} I, 276}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0257-notes_right-L022}{}{}{(cfr. \textit{Tanbīh,} p. 56).}
\end{minipage}

\DLine{AB01-PDF0258-body-L018}{}{}{\Indent De duabus insulis maris Ǵorǵān (Caspii), Ptolemaeo ignotis, \Name{Iacobus Edessenus},}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0258-body-L019}{}{}{pag. LXV, haec habet: « Quo in mari, quia identidem multitudine aquarum, quae perpetuo}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0258-body-L020}{}{}{« infunduntur, nimis augetur, fieri non potuit ut insulae exstiterint. Vix enim ab antiquissimis}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0258-body-L021}{}{}{« temporibus usque ad hoc tempus duae solae insulae parvae ibi conspectae sunt, quae ad}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0258-body-L022}{}{}{« habitandum parum sunt aptae ». Cfr. quae de insulis vulcanicis in hoc mari narrant \Name{al-}}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0258-body-L023}{}{}{\Name{–Mas‘ūdī}, \textit{Prairies,} II, 26; \textit{Tanbīh,} p. 60; \Name{Aboulféda}, p. 47 sq. (textus), cet.}

\DLine{AB01-PDF0258-notes_left-L003}{}{}{(p. 137 textus) habent 1300, ut \Name{al–Kharaqī} et}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0258-notes_left-L004}{}{}{\Name{Iacobus}. Apud \Name{al-Mas‘ūdī}, \textit{Prairies,} I, 260,}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0258-notes_left-L005}{}{}{longitudo 1100 evidens scripturae error est pro 1300.}

\DLine{AB01-PDF0258-notes_right-L002}{}{}{textus) habent quoque 1370; sed 1378 \Name{Iacobi}}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0258-notes_right-L003}{}{}{\Name{Edesseni} est numerus \Name{Ptolemaei} (\textit{Geogra-}}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0258-notes_right-L004}{}{}{\textit{phia,} VII, 4, 11).}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0258-N05}{}
