\DSource{0259}{170}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0259-body-L001}{}{}{« \textit{Pontus} (\textit{bunṭus}); quintum \textit{mare Ǵorǵān} (i. e. \textit{Caspium}). — \textit{Indorum maris} longitu-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0259-body-L002}{}{}{« dinem ab occasu ad ortum, ab extrema \textit{Aethiopia} (\textit{arḍ al–ḥabashah}) ad extremam \textit{In-}}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0259-body-L003}{}{}{« \textit{dorum} et \textit{Sinensium} terram protendi, et 8000 milliariorum complecti; latitudinem 2700}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0259-body-L004}{}{}{« milliariorum esse, et 1700 milliaria lineam aequatoris transgredi. Exire ab eo sinum prope}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0259-body-L005}{}{}{« \textit{Aethiopiam,} et in \textit{al–Barbar} versus porrigi; \textit{sinum Barbaricum} appellari; eius longitu-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0259-body-L006}{}{}{« dinem 500 milliariorum esse, latitudinem eius extremitatis (1) 100. — Ex eo [Indorum}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0259-body-L007}{}{}{« mari] alium quoque sinum versus \textit{Aylah} exire, scilicet \textit{mare al–Qolzum,} cuius longitu-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0259-body-L008}{}{}{« dinem 1400 milliaria complecti, latitudinem in origine 700, et eius finem versus extremi-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0259-body-L009}{}{}{« tatem proximam, quae \textit{mare Viride} appelletur, 200 (2); in eius extremitate fere acuta}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0259-body-L010}{}{}{« [urbem] \textit{al–Qolzum} esse, unde nomen mare accepisse; ex oriente eius terram \textit{al–Yemen}}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0259-body-L011}{}{}{« et \textit{‘Aden} esse, ab austro \textit{Aetkiopiam.} — Exire quoque ab eo [Indico mari] sinum ad ter-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0259-body-L012}{}{}{« ram \textit{Persidis (fāris)} versus, \textit{sinum Persicum} appellatum, seu \textit{mare al–Baṣrah et Per-}}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0259-body-L013}{}{}{« \textit{sidis,} in cuius [littore] orientali sint \textit{Tīz} et [provincia] \textit{Mukrān,} in occidentali ..... (3)}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0259-body-L014}{}{}{« \textit{‘Omān}; eius longitudinem 1400 milliariorum esse, latitudinem in origine 500, loco recti iti-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0259-body-L015}{}{}{« neris 150. Inter hos duos sinus, \textit{Aylah} scilicet et \textit{Persidis,} terram \textit{al–Ḥiǵāz, al–Yemen}}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0259-body-L016}{}{}{« et reliquas \textit{Arabum} regiones esse, amplitudine 1500 milliariorum. — Alium quoque sinum}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0259-body-L017}{}{}{« in extremas \textit{Indorum} terras versus exire, prope suum terminum \textit{Sinum Rubrum} (4) ap-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0259-body-L018}{}{}{« pellatum, cuius longitudinem 1500 milliariorum. — In hoc \textit{Indorum mari} 1370 insulas,}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0259-body-L019}{}{}{« sive habitatas, sive desertas, esse, e quibus insulam magnam in extremo mari, terrae \textit{In-}}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0259-body-L020}{}{}{« \textit{dorum} obiectam ab oriente prope \textit{Sinarum} regionem, et \textit{Taprobanam} vel \textit{Sarandīb} ap-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0259-body-L021}{}{}{« pellatam; circuitum 3000 milliariorum habere; esse in ea altos montes et multos amnes;}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0259-body-L022}{}{}{« provenire ab ea (5) rubinos et sapphiros. Circa eam 19 insulas habitatas esse, quae urbibus}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0259-body-L023}{}{}{« et pagis abundent; ex insulis huius maris insulas \textit{az–Zābiǵ} (6), insulas \textit{ad–Dībaḥāt} (7) et}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0259-body-L024}{}{}{« \textit{Qumayr} (8), insulam \textit{Kalah} (9) a qua stamnum (\ArabicRun{الرصاص القلعي}) importetur, insulamque \textit{Sar-}}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0259-body-L025}{}{}{« \textit{bozah} (10) a qua camphora proveniat, esse.}
\par\vspace{6.52mm}
\par\vspace{6pt}\nointerlineskip\hbox to\textwidth{\hss\vrule width 115.43pt height.25pt depth0pt\hss}\prevdepth=-1000pt \vspace{5pt}
\noindent\begin{minipage}[t]{.48\textwidth}\vspace{0pt}\fontsize{9}{11.5}\selectfont\setlength{\GutterOffset}{0pt}\setlength{\FullSourceWidth}{\linewidth}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0259-N01}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0259-notes_left-L001}{}{}{\Indent (1) Codd. \ArabicRun{طرفه}, sed procul dubio legendum \ArabicRun{طريقه}}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0259-notes_left-L002}{}{}{« eius recti itineris » ut apud \Name{al-Battānī}.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0259-N02}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0259-notes_left-L003}{}{}{\Indent (2) De hoc loco cfr. adn. \textit{e}, p. 175-177.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0259-N03}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0259-notes_left-L004}{}{}{\Indent (3) Codicum verba corrupta corrigere nescio.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0259-N04}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0259-notes_left-L005}{}{}{\Indent (4) Ita codices, cum quo posset nomen Oceani}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0259-notes_left-L006}{}{}{Indici apud Graecos (\textgreek{Ἐρυθρὰ θάλασσα}, mare Rubrum)}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0259-notes_left-L007}{}{}{conferri; sed cur sinus parvus nomen totius maris}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0259-notes_left-L008}{}{}{receperit haud intelligitur. Collatis \Name{al-Battānī}}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0259-notes_left-L009}{}{}{(p. 18, l. 4 et adn. 2) atque \Name{Ibn Rosteh}, vix dubi-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0259-notes_left-L010}{}{}{tandum est \ArabicRun{الاحمر} « rubrum » esse codicum aut ipsius}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0259-notes_left-L011}{}{}{auctoris errorem pro \ArabicRun{الاخضر} « viridi ».}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0259-N05}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0259-notes_left-L012}{}{}{\Indent (5) Ita codd. \ArabicRun{ومنها يخرج} recte; \Name{al-Battānī} \ArabicRun{و}}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0259-notes_left-L013}{}{}{omittit, qua re pronomen ad montes et amnes re-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0259-notes_left-L014}{}{}{ferri debet.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0259-N06}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0259-notes_left-L015}{}{}{\Indent (6) Est insula quam nos \textit{Giava} appellamus (lin-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0259-notes_left-L016}{}{}{gua Malesica \textit{Ǵāwa} \ArabicRun{جاو}, apud Arabes XIII saeculi}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0259-notes_left-L017}{}{}{\textit{Ǵāwah}); cfr. quae disseruit \Name{Van der Lith} in}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0259-notes_left-L018}{}{}{opere \textit{Le livre des merveilles de l’Inde par le ca-}}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0259-notes_left-L019}{}{}{\textit{pitaine} \Name{Bozorg fils des Chahriyâr,} \textit{texte}}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0259-notes_left-L020}{}{}{\textit{arabe et traduction française par P. A. Van der}}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0259-notes_left-L021}{}{}{\textit{Lith et M. Devic,} Leide 1883, p. 231–33.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0259-N07}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0259-notes_left-L022}{}{}{\Indent (7) Codd. sine punctis; alii legunt \textit{ad-Dībaǵāt}}
\end{minipage}
\hfill\vrule width.3pt\hfill\begin{minipage}[t]{.48\textwidth}\vspace{0pt}\fontsize{9}{11.5}\selectfont\setlength{\GutterOffset}{0pt}\setlength{\FullSourceWidth}{\linewidth}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0259-notes_right-L001}{}{}{(\ArabicRun{ج} ǵ pro \ArabicRun{ح} ḥ). Nomen ex Indico \textit{dvīpa,} i. e. insula,}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0259-notes_right-L002}{}{}{ortum; designat insulas quas hodie \textit{Laccadivas} et}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0259-notes_right-L003}{}{}{\textit{Maldivas} (\textit{Dībat al-mahal} apud Arabes XIV saec.)}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0259-notes_right-L004}{}{}{vocamus; v. \Name{Van der Lith}, 216-17.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0259-N08}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0259-notes_right-L005}{}{}{\Indent (8) Lectionem cod. L (\ArabicRun{قمير}) servo; P \ArabicRun{قمر} habet.}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0259-notes_right-L006}{}{}{Nomen etiam apud \Name{al-Bīrūnī}, \textit{India,} p. 103 text.}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0259-notes_right-L007}{}{}{(= I, 210 vers.) scriptum invenitur, ubi procul du-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0259-notes_right-L008}{}{}{bio eandem regionem indicat quam ceteri Arabes}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0259-notes_right-L009}{}{}{\ArabicRun{قمار} \textit{Qumār} (i. e. \textit{Cambodge,} cuius veteres incolae}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0259-notes_right-L010}{}{}{\textit{Khmēr} erant) vocant. Locum al-Bīrūnī haud omnino}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0259-notes_right-L011}{}{}{recte a Sachau intellectum, melius interpretatus est}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0259-notes_right-L012}{}{}{\Name{de Goeje}, in versione \Name{Ibn Khordādhbeh}, p. 50,}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0259-notes_right-L013}{}{}{adn. 1.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0259-N09}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0259-notes_right-L014}{}{}{\Indent (9) Est portus hodie \textit{Kēdā} (\textit{Quedah}) vocatus, in}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0259-notes_right-L015}{}{}{peninsula Malacca (Malāka \ArabicRun{ملاك}), ut demonstravit}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0259-notes_right-L016}{}{}{\Name{Van der Lith}, p. 255-264; cfr. quoque \Name{Ibn Ro-}}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0259-notes_right-L017}{}{}{\Name{steh} p. 88, al-Mas‘ūdī, \textit{Tanbīh,} p. 61.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0259-N10}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0259-notes_right-L018}{}{}{\Indent (10) Erat pagus vel regio in insula Sumatra, ad}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0259-notes_right-L019}{}{}{ripas \textit{Mūsī} fluminis, prope locum ubi nunc urbs}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0259-notes_right-L020}{}{}{\textit{Palembang} (\ArabicRun{ڤلمبڠ}) est; in annalibus Sinensibus \textit{Sam-}}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0259-notes_right-L021}{}{}{\textit{-bo–tsai} appellatur. V. \Name{Van der Lith}, p. 247-253}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0259-notes_right-L022}{}{}{(et 102, 105, 111, 124, 158-160, 165, 176, 190); \Name{al–}}
\end{minipage}
\DSource{0260}{171}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0260-P01}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0260-body-L001}{}{}{\Indent « \textit{Mare Occidentis,} quod \textit{mare Circumdans} et a Graecis \textit{Oceanus} quoque vocetur,}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0260-body-L002}{}{}{« coniunctum esse \textit{mari Indorum,} nec eius aliquid cognosci nisi partem quae, in occasu et}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0260-body-L003}{}{}{« septentrionibus sita, terram \textit{Russorum} (\textit{ar–rūs}) et \textit{Slavorum} (\textit{aṣ–ṣaqālibah}) (1) prospiciat.}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0260-body-L004}{}{}{« Ab ultimo termino in austro incipiens, \textit{Nigritiam} (\textit{arḍ as–sūdān}) prospectare et per fines}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0260-body-L005}{}{}{« \textit{Ultimae as–Sūs} (2), \textit{Tingis} (\textit{Ṭanǵah}), \textit{Tāhart} (3), \textit{Hispaniae, al–Ǵalāliqah} (4) atque \textit{Sla-}}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0260-body-L006}{}{}{« \textit{vorum} (5) usque ad \textit{Britanniam} (\textit{al–Berṭāniyah}) transire; postea illinc protendi, ultra}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0260-body-L007}{}{}{« montes inaccessos terrasque non habitatas, in \textit{mare Orientis} versus. Hoc mare, \textit{Oceanum}}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0260-body-L008}{}{}{« scilicet, navibus non sulcari et prope littora tantum navigari; esse in eo sex insulas, \textit{Aethio-}}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0260-body-L009}{}{}{« \textit{pum} terram prospicientes, quae \textit{Aeternae} (\textit{al–khālidāt}) vel \textit{Beatorum} (\textit{as–su‘adā’}) appel-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0260-body-L010}{}{}{« lentur. Aliam insulam, \textit{Gadira} appellatam, esse ex adverso \textit{Hispaniae,} prope \textit{sinum} qui}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0260-body-L011}{}{}{« ab hoc mari exeat, latitudinem 7 milliariorum in loco exitus habeat, inter \textit{Hispaniam} et}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0260-body-L012}{}{}{« \textit{Tingim} sit, et [antiquitus] \textit{vadus Herculis} (\textit{ma‘barat Hiraqlas}) vocatus sit, hodie \textit{az–Zo-}}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0260-body-L013}{}{}{« \textit{qāq} [i. e. angiportus] appelletur; hunc sinum in \textit{mare Romanorum et Syriae} influere.}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0260-body-L014}{}{}{« In tractu boreali \textit{Oceani} esse 12 insulas \textit{Britanniae.} Ex eo quoque, in septentrionibus}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0260-body-L015}{}{}{« \textit{Slavorum,} oriri sinum magnum, qui et \textit{mare Varengorum} (\textit{warank}) a gente eius littora}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0260-body-L016}{}{}{« habitante vocetur (6); protendi ad terram \textit{Bulgarorum} (\textit{bulghār}) muslimorum, et in longi-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0260-body-L017}{}{}{« tudinem ab ortu ad occasum 300 milliaria, in latitudinem 100 patere.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0260-P02}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0260-body-L018}{}{}{\Indent « \textit{Mare Romanorum, Africae, Aegypti et Syriae,} a sinu qui ex \textit{mari Occidentis} exeat}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0260-body-L019}{}{}{« procedens in orientem usque ad \textit{Tyrum} et \textit{Sidonem,} longitudinem 5000 milliariorum ha-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0260-body-L020}{}{}{« bere, latitudinem alio loco 600, alio 700, alio demum 800. Exire ab eo sinum, qui, in sep-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0260-body-L021}{}{}{« tentriones versus prope \textit{Romam} pergens, 500 milliaria in longitudinem pateat et \textit{Adrias} (7)}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0260-body-L022}{}{}{« appelletur. Alium sinum ad terram \textit{Narbonensem} versus exire; longitudinem 200 millia-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0260-body-L023}{}{}{« riorum habere. Hoc mari esse 162 insulas habitatas, quarum 5 (8) magnas, scilicet \textit{Rhodum,}}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0260-body-L024}{}{}{« \textit{Cyprum, Samum, Siciliam} et \textit{Cretam.}}
\par\vspace{3.50mm}
\par\vspace{6pt}\nointerlineskip\hbox to\textwidth{\hss\vrule width 115.43pt height.25pt depth0pt\hss}\prevdepth=-1000pt \vspace{5pt}
\noindent\begin{minipage}[t]{.48\textwidth}\vspace{0pt}\fontsize{9}{11.5}\selectfont\setlength{\GutterOffset}{0pt}\setlength{\FullSourceWidth}{\linewidth}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0260-notes_left-L001}{}{}{\Name{–Mas‘ūdī}, \textit{Tanbīh,} p. 61; \textit{Prairies,} I, 243 (ubi}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0260-notes_left-L002}{}{}{nomen corruptum \textit{Serīrah} \ArabicRun{سريرة} pro \ArabicRun{سربزة}); \Name{Yā-}}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0260-notes_left-L003}{}{}{\Name{qūt} I, 61, III, 68 et 354; \Name{Aboulféda}, p. 22, 368,}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0260-notes_left-L004}{}{}{375 (= II, 26, III, 126 et 132 vers.), ubi semper \textit{Se-}}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0260-notes_left-L005}{}{}{\textit{rīrah}; \Name{ad–Dimashqī}, p. 22, 199, 204 (\textit{Serīrah}), cet.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0260-N01}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0260-notes_left-L006}{}{}{\Indent (1) Arabici scriptores hoc nomine Slavos et}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0260-notes_left-L007}{}{}{multas stirpes Germanicas designant; cfr. G. \Name{Jacob},}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0260-notes_left-L008}{}{}{\textit{Studien in arabischen Geographen,} Berlin 1891-92,}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0260-notes_left-L009}{}{}{fasc. I, p. 17.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0260-N02}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0260-notes_left-L010}{}{}{\Indent (2) \textit{Ultima as–Sūs} est provincia meridionalis}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0260-notes_left-L011}{}{}{imperii Maroccani cuius praecipua urbs hodie \textit{Tā-}}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0260-notes_left-L012}{}{}{\textit{rūdānt.}}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0260-N03}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0260-notes_left-L013}{}{}{\Indent (3) Nomen erat duarum magnarum urbium a se}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0260-notes_left-L014}{}{}{quinque milliaria Arabica (decem fere Km.) distan-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0260-notes_left-L015}{}{}{tes, quarum altera vetus (\textit{al-qadīmah}) cognomen}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0260-notes_left-L016}{}{}{habebat, altera nova (\textit{ḥadīthah}). Prior in nostris}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0260-notes_left-L017}{}{}{mappis \textit{Tiharet} (\textit{Tiyāret}) vocatur, altera \textit{Tāqdemt};}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0260-notes_left-L018}{}{}{ambae in Algeria occidentali. Legantur quae \Name{de}}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0260-notes_left-L019}{}{}{\Name{Goeje} disseruit, \textit{Specimen e litteris orientalibus}}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0260-notes_left-L020}{}{}{\textit{exhibens descriptionem al-Magribi sumtam e Libro}}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0260-notes_left-L021}{}{}{\textit{Regionum} \Name{al-Ja’qubii}, Lugduni Batav. 1860, p. 103}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0260-notes_left-L022}{}{}{seqq., et postea in \textit{Description de l’Afrique et de}}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0260-notes_left-L023}{}{}{\textit{l’Espagne par} \Name{Edrisi}, Leyde 1866, p. 100; cfr.}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0260-notes_left-L024}{}{}{quoque R. \Name{Basset}, \textit{Documents géographiques sur}}
\end{minipage}
\hfill\vrule width.3pt\hfill\begin{minipage}[t]{.48\textwidth}\vspace{0pt}\fontsize{9}{11.5}\selectfont\setlength{\GutterOffset}{0pt}\setlength{\FullSourceWidth}{\linewidth}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0260-notes_right-L001}{}{}{\textit{l’Afrique septentrionale} traduits de l’arabe, Paris}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0260-notes_right-L002}{}{}{1898, p. 23.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0260-N04}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0260-notes_right-L003}{}{}{\Indent (4) Sunt \textit{Callaeci} Romanorum, \textit{Gallaeci} aevi}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0260-notes_right-L004}{}{}{medii, cuius nomen in provincia hodierna \textit{Galicia}}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0260-notes_right-L005}{}{}{(Hispaniae borealis) exstat.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0260-N05}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0260-notes_right-L006}{}{}{\Indent (5) Cfr. adn. 1.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0260-N06}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0260-notes_right-L007}{}{}{\Indent (6) Est mare Balticum, cuius littora orientalia,}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0260-notes_right-L008}{}{}{IX Christi saeculo, incolebant \textit{Varengi} (Germanice}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0260-notes_right-L009}{}{}{\textit{Wäringer} seu \textit{Waräger}), Normannorum gens. No-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0260-notes_right-L010}{}{}{men apud Arabicos scriptores infrequens; \Name{Aboul-}}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0260-notes_right-L011}{}{}{\Name{féda}, p. 35 (= II, 42 vers.), se id tantum apud al-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0260-notes_right-L012}{}{}{-Bīrūnī et in \textit{Tadhkirah} Naṣīr ad-dīn scriptum in-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0260-notes_right-L013}{}{}{venisse testatur. — Quae autem apud \Name{al-Kharaqī}}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0260-notes_right-L014}{}{}{sequuntur miro modo, ut quoque infra videbimus,}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0260-notes_right-L015}{}{}{Balticum mare confundunt cum Palude Maeotica}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0260-notes_right-L016}{}{}{(Mare Azov), prope quam Bulgari muslimici incole-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0260-notes_right-L017}{}{}{bant, et ad quam procul dubio pertinent dimensio-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0260-notes_right-L018}{}{}{nes hoc loco relatae.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0260-N07}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0260-notes_right-L019}{}{}{\Indent (7) Apud Syros \textit{Adrias} (\textsyriac{ܐܕܪܝܘܣ} \textit{adryūs}) est}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0260-notes_right-L020}{}{}{Mare Mediterraneum; v. F. \Name{Nau}, \textit{Notice sur le li-}}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0260-notes_right-L021}{}{}{\textit{vre des Trésors de Jacques de Bartela,} in Journal}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0260-notes_right-L022}{}{}{Asiatique, ser. IX, t. VII, 1896, p. 309-312 (= 26-29}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0260-notes_right-L023}{}{}{opusculi seorsum editi).}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0260-N08}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0260-notes_right-L024}{}{}{\Indent (8) Cod P \ArabicRun{خمسون} 50; at sensus et constructio}
\end{minipage}
\DSource{0261}{172}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0261-P01}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0261-body-L001}{}{}{\Indent « \textit{Pontum mare} terrestre (i. e. internum) appellari [quoque] \textit{mare Trapezuntis} a portu}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0261-body-L002}{}{}{« quodam in eius littore; a \textit{Lādhiqah} ultra \textit{Constantinopolim} protendi, in terram \textit{Russorum}}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0261-body-L003}{}{}{« et \textit{Slavorum}; longitudinem 1300 milliariorum, latitudinem 300 habere. Effundi in hoc mare}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0261-body-L004}{}{}{« flumen \textit{Tanaim} appellatum, a parte boreali respectu \textit{maris Maeotidis,} quod est \textit{mare Va-}}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0261-body-L005}{}{}{« \textit{rengorum} (\textit{warank}), decurrens (1). A \textit{Ponto} prope moenia \textit{Constantinopolis} sinum egredi,}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0261-body-L006}{}{}{« amni similem (2) et in \textit{mare Aegypti} defluentem; prope \textit{Constantinopolim,} quae sit in eius}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0261-body-L007}{}{}{« littore occidentali, 3 milliariorum latitudinem obtinere, semperque angustiorem fieri usque}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0261-body-L008}{}{}{« quo in \textit{mare Syriae et Aegypti} influat. In littore australi huius mari [\textit{Aegypti et Roma-}}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0261-body-L009}{}{}{« \textit{norum}] esse terras \textit{al–Maghrib} (3) et \textit{Africae} usque ad \textit{Alexandriam Aegyptumque}; ex}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0261-body-L010}{}{}{« adverso, in plaga boreali, exstare terras \textit{Hispaniae, Romanorum} et \textit{Francorum} (\textit{faran-}}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0261-body-L011}{}{}{« \textit{ǵah}) usque \textit{Antiochiam,} quam \textit{Syria} et \textit{Palaestina} subsequantur.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0261-P02}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0261-body-L012}{}{}{\Indent « \textit{Mare Ǵorǵān} sive [\textit{Caucasicae}] \textit{Portae} in longitudinem ab occasu ad ortum 800 mil-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0261-body-L013}{}{}{« liaria patere, in latitudinem 600; duas insulas continere, quae temporibus actis habitarentur.}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0261-body-L014}{}{}{« Appellari quoque hoc mare \textit{mare Ābaskūn} a quodam portu eius (4); porrigi postea usque}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0261-body-L015}{}{}{« ad [provincias] \textit{Ṭabaristān, ad–Daylam, Shirwān,} [urbemque] \textit{Bāb al–Abwāb} (5), et pla-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0261-body-L016}{}{}{« gam \textit{Alanorum} (\textit{al–Lān}) (6); deinde ad \textit{Chazaros} (\textit{al–khazar}) et ostium fluminis \textit{Itil} (7), de-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0261-body-L017}{}{}{« mum ad regiones \textit{Persarum,} usque quo rursus \textit{Ābaskūn} assequatur. Nomine cuiusque}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0261-body-L018}{}{}{« regionis quam lambiat vocari; antiquitus \textit{mare Ǵorǵān} appellatum esse, hodie \textit{mare Ābas-}}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0261-body-L019}{}{}{« \textit{kūn} et \textit{mare Chazarorum} dici; in aliud mare non influere.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0261-P03}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0261-body-L020}{}{}{\Indent « Haec esse maria magna; reliqua esse lacus paludesque, ut lacum \textit{Khuwārizm} (i. e.}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0261-body-L021}{}{}{« \textit{Chorasmiae,} hodie \textit{Aral}), lacum \textit{Tiberiadis} in \textit{Syria,} et \textit{Īssik-kūl} prope \textit{Barsukhān} (8).}
\par\vspace{1.92mm}
\par\vspace{6pt}\nointerlineskip\hbox to\textwidth{\hss\vrule width 115.43pt height.25pt depth0pt\hss}\prevdepth=-1000pt \vspace{5pt}
\noindent\begin{minipage}[t]{.48\textwidth}\vspace{0pt}\fontsize{9}{11.5}\selectfont\setlength{\GutterOffset}{0pt}\setlength{\FullSourceWidth}{\linewidth}
\par\fontsize{9}{11.67}\selectfont
\DLine{AB01-PDF0261-notes_left-L001}{}{}{grammaticalis (nam postea \ArabicRun{جزائر عظام} legitur) repo-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0261-notes_left-L002}{}{}{scunt \ArabicRun{خمس} 5, ut cod. L, \Name{al-Battānī} et \Name{Ibn Ro-}}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0261-notes_left-L003}{}{}{\Name{steh} habent.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0261-N01}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0261-notes_left-L004}{}{}{\Indent (1) Mare Balticum cum Palude Maeotica con-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0261-notes_left-L005}{}{}{fundit, ut dixi p. 171, adn. 6.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0261-N02}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0261-notes_left-L006}{}{}{\Indent (2) Codd. corrupte \ArabicRun{ٮحرى ڡيه مايه نهر}, quod cum}
\par\vspace{1.13mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0261-notes_left-L007}{}{}{\Name{al-Battānī} et \Name{Ibn Rosteh} restituo \ArabicRun{يجري كانه نهر}.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0261-N03}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0261-notes_left-L008}{}{}{\Indent (3) \textit{Mauritania} veterum; hodie Maroccanum}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0261-notes_left-L009}{}{}{imperium, Algeria et provincia Tunetana.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0261-N04}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0261-notes_left-L010}{}{}{\Indent (4) Abuskūn, Abiskūn vel Aboskūn erat ma-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0261-notes_left-L011}{}{}{gnum emporium maritimum regionis et urbis Ǵor-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0261-notes_left-L012}{}{}{ǵān (cuius ruinae prope Pisarak \ArabicRun{پسرك} exstant). De}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0261-notes_left-L013}{}{}{situ loci duae sunt sententiae; B. \Name{Dorn}, \textit{Caspia, Ue-}}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0261-notes_left-L014}{}{}{\textit{ber die Einfälle der alten Russen in Tabaristan,} St.}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0261-notes_left-L015}{}{}{Petersburg 1875, p. 5, 6, 7, eum collocat ad hostium}
\par\vspace{0.69mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0261-notes_left-L016}{}{}{fluvii Gurgān \ArabicRun{ڭرڭان}, haud longe a vico \textit{Gümüsh-}}
\par\vspace{1.13mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0261-notes_left-L017}{}{}{\textit{tepeh} \ArabicRun{ڭومش تپه}, ubi Mouravieff (primo saec. XIX}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0261-notes_left-L018}{}{}{quadrante) multas ruinas invenit; \Name{Melgunof}, \textit{Das}}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0261-notes_left-L019}{}{}{\textit{südliche Ufer des kaspischen Meeres,} Leipzig 1868,}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0261-notes_left-L020}{}{}{p. 62, 63, 112, 119-120, eum circa hodiernum mau-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0261-notes_left-L021}{}{}{soleum (imām-zādeh) \textit{Kharāb-i-shehr} appellatum,}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0261-notes_left-L022}{}{}{10 km. a littore, 6 ab ortu urbis Ǵez \ArabicRun{جـز} (in re-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0261-notes_left-L023}{}{}{gione Anezān provinciae Asterābād). Sed semper est}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0261-notes_left-L024}{}{}{prope locum quo maris littus orientale in occiden-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0261-notes_left-L025}{}{}{tem versus se flectit.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0261-N05}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0261-notes_left-L026}{}{}{\Indent (5) Hodie \textit{Derbend.}}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0261-N06}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0261-notes_left-L027}{}{}{\Indent (6) Sunt qui hodie \textit{Ossethi} vocantur.}
\end{minipage}
\hfill\vrule width.3pt\hfill\begin{minipage}[t]{.48\textwidth}\vspace{0pt}\fontsize{9}{11.5}\selectfont\setlength{\GutterOffset}{0pt}\setlength{\FullSourceWidth}{\linewidth}
\par\fontsize{9}{11.67}\selectfont
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0261-N07}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0261-notes_right-L001}{}{}{\Indent (7) Quod veteres \textit{Rha,} nos \textit{Volga} appellamus.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0261-N08}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0261-notes_right-L002}{}{}{\Indent (8) Nomina in codd. depravata. Urbs vetustio-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0261-notes_right-L003}{}{}{ribus Arabicis geographis, ut \Name{Qodāmah}, p. 203}
\par\vspace{0.40mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0261-notes_right-L004}{}{}{(ubi corrupte \ArabicRun{نوشجان} Nūshaǵān pro \ArabicRun{برسخان}), et \Name{al–}}
\par\vspace{0.30mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0261-notes_right-L005}{}{}{\Name{-Muqaddasī}, p. 48, 263, 275, 340, nota; lacus}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0261-notes_right-L006}{}{}{contra ignotus, si \Name{Qodāmah} excipiatur qui eum}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0261-notes_right-L007}{}{}{sine nomine l. l. memorat. Lacum tamen, in Turke-}
\par\vspace{0.44mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0261-notes_right-L008}{}{}{stān orientali positum, iam \textit{Īssik-kūl} (\ArabicRun{ايسكول} vel}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0261-notes_right-L009}{}{}{corrupte \ArabicRun{السكول}) appellat liber Persicus ignoti au-}
\par\vspace{0.35mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0261-notes_right-L010}{}{}{ctoris anno 372 heg. (inc. 26 Iunii 982 Chr.) confe-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0261-notes_right-L011}{}{}{ctus et \textit{ḥudūd al-‘ālam} « Fines mundi » inscriptus;}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0261-notes_right-L012}{}{}{v. V. \Name{Barthold}, \textit{Otčet pojezdkije v srednjuju}}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0261-notes_right-L013}{}{}{\textit{Aziju s naučnoju cjelju, 1893-94} (Commentarius}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0261-notes_right-L014}{}{}{de itinere annis 1893-94 in Asia centrali scientia-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0261-notes_right-L015}{}{}{rum causa suscepto), St. Peterburg 1897, p. 31-32}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0261-notes_right-L016}{}{}{(= Mémoires de l’Académie Impér. des Sciences de}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0261-notes_right-L017}{}{}{St.-Pétersbourg, VIII\textsuperscript{e} série, Classe historico-philolo-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0261-notes_right-L018}{}{}{gique, vol. I, nr. 4). Nomen Turcicum « lacum cali-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0261-notes_right-L019}{}{}{dum » significat; scribendi rationes variae sunt: \textit{īs-}}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0261-notes_right-L020}{}{}{\textit{sīq, issīq, īssīgh, issīgh.} Urbem Barsukhān et lacum}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0261-notes_right-L021}{}{}{(\textit{īssigh-kūl}) anno fere 1050 Chr. copiose describit}
\par\vspace{0.41mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0261-notes_right-L022}{}{}{Abū Sa‘īd ‘Abd al-Ḥayy \Name{Gardīzī} (\ArabicRun{ڭرديزي}) in suo}
\par\vspace{0.34mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0261-notes_right-L023}{}{}{libro \textit{zayn al–akhbār}; verba eius Persice apud}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0261-notes_right-L024}{}{}{\Name{Barthold}, p. 89-90, referuntur, et, Rossice versa,}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0261-notes_right-L025}{}{}{p. 113-114. Nomen \textit{īssīgh-kūl} scribit \Name{Muḥammad}}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0261-notes_right-L026}{}{}{\Name{Ḥaydar} in Persica sua « Historia Rashīdica » (\textit{ta’-}}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0261-notes_right-L027}{}{}{\textit{rīkh i rashīdī}), XVI saec. Chr.; cfr. \Name{Barthold}, p. 50.}
\end{minipage}
\DSource{0262}{173}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0262-P01}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0262-body-L001}{}{}{\Indent « Narrat \Name{Aristoteles} (1) \textit{Oceanum,} quasi coronam, terram [totam] circumdare; ex eo}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0262-body-L002}{}{}{« oriri sinum in plaga occidentali, loco \textit{Vado Herculis} (2) appellato, qui, terram habitabilem}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0262-body-L003}{}{}{« ingrediens, ad orientem procedat et sit \textit{mare Aegypti, Romanorum, Africae et Syriae.} In}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0262-body-L004}{}{}{« plaga autem orientali ab eo [\textit{Oceano}] alium sinum provenire, terram habitabilem ingredien-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0262-body-L005}{}{}{« tem, qui in occidentem porrigatur et sit \textit{mare Sinarum, Indorum, Persidis, al–Qolzum,}}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0262-body-L006}{}{}{« \textit{Barbarorum} (\textit{al–Barbar}). Haec sunt quae de maribus scimus ».}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0262-P02}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0262-body-L007}{}{}{\Indent Textum Arabicum totius \Name{al–Kharaqī} loci praebeo iuxta codices Parisiensem (P) et}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0262-body-L008}{}{}{Laurentianum (L):}
\par\fontsize{16.8}{26.42}\selectfont
\par\vspace{4.14mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0262-body-L009}{}{}{\smash{\makebox[\linewidth][s]{\textarabic{﴿الباب الثاني﴾ في ذكر البحور وكيفيّة وَضْعها من المساكن. قد حكى الجَيْهانيُّ\textsuperscript{1} وغيره من}\hspace*{10mm}}}}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0262-body-L010}{}{}{\smash{\makebox[\linewidth][s]{\textarabic{العلماء بمواقع البحور وامتداداتها ان البحور المعروفة خمسة احدها بحر الهند وهو الذي يقال له ايضًا بحـــر}}}}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0262-body-L011}{}{}{\smash{\makebox[\linewidth][s]{\textarabic{الصين والثاني بحر المغرب والثالث بحر الشام والروم ومصر والرابع بحر بنطس\textsuperscript{2} والخامس بحر جرجان}}}}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0262-body-L012}{}{}{\smash{\makebox[\linewidth][s]{\textarabic{فاما بحر الهند فانه يمتــدّ طوله من المغرب الى المشرق من اقصى ارض الحبشة الى اقصى ارض الهند}}}}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0262-body-L013}{}{}{\smash{\makebox[\linewidth][s]{\textarabic{والصين يكون مقدار ذلك ثمانية آلاف ميل وعرضه الفان وسبعمائة ميـــل ويجاوز خطّ الاستواء الف}}}}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0262-body-L014}{}{}{\smash{\makebox[\linewidth][s]{\textarabic{وسبعمائة ميل يخرج منه خليج عند ارض الحبشة ويمتدّ الى ناحيــة البربر يُسمَّي ذلك الخليـج البربريّ}}}}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0262-body-L015}{}{}{\smash{\makebox[\linewidth][s]{\textarabic{يكون طوله مقدار خمسمائة ميل وعرض طرفه مائة ميل ويخرج منه ايضًا خليج آخَرُ نحو أيْلةَ وهو بحر}}}}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0262-body-L016}{}{}{\smash{\makebox[\linewidth][s]{\textarabic{القلزم طوله الف واربعمائة ميل وعرضه في الاصل سبعمائة ميل ومنتهاه الى طرف\textsuperscript{3} الأذْنَى الذي يُسمَّى}}}}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0262-body-L017}{}{}{\smash{\makebox[\linewidth][s]{\textarabic{البحر الاخضر مقدار مائتَيْ ميل وعلى طرفه المستدقّ القلزم فلذلك سُمِّيَ بـــه على شرقيّه ارض اليمـــن}}}}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0262-body-L018}{}{}{\smash{\makebox[\linewidth][s]{\textarabic{وعدن وعلى جنوبيّه ارض الحبشة ويخرج منه خليـج آخَرُ نحـــو ارض فارس يُسمَّى الخليـج الفارسيّ}}}}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0262-body-L019}{}{}{\smash{\makebox[\linewidth][s]{\textarabic{وهو بحـــر البصرة وفارس الذي على شرقيّه تِــيز ومُكران وعلى غربيّه \textsuperscript{*}وٮحاله ى حٮه\textsuperscript{4} عمان طوله}}}}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0262-body-L020}{}{}{\smash{\makebox[\linewidth][s]{\textarabic{الف واربعمائة ميل وعرضه في الاصل خمسمائة ميل وعرض طريقه مائة وخمسون ميـــلًا وبين هذَيْن}}}}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0262-body-L021}{}{}{\smash{\makebox[\linewidth][s]{\textarabic{الخليجَيْن اعني خليج ايلة وخليـج فارس ارض الحجاز واليمن وسائر بــلاد العرب فيما بين مسافة الف}}}}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0262-body-L022}{}{}{\smash{\makebox[\linewidth][s]{\textarabic{وخمسمائة ميل ويخرج منه خليج آخَرُ الى اقصى ارض الهند عند تمامــه يُسمَّى الخليـج الاحمر وطوله}}}}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0262-body-L023}{}{}{\smash{\makebox[\linewidth][s]{\textarabic{الف وخمسمائة ميل وفي بحر الهند هذا من الجزائر العامرة وغــير العامرة الف وثلثمائة وسبعون جزيرة}}}}
\par\fontsize{11.1}{13.8}\selectfont
\par\vspace{3.51mm}
\par\noindent\rule[.5ex]{36.8mm}{.8pt}\par
\par\vspace{3.38mm}
\par\fontsize{9}{11.5}\selectfont
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0262-P03}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0262-body-L024}{}{}{\Indent 1) P \ArabicRun{الجبهانى}, L \ArabicRun{الجهيانى} — 2) P \ArabicRun{نبطس} — 3) Sic codd. pro \ArabicRun{طرفه} vel \ArabicRun{الطرف} — 4) Sic P; L \ArabicRun{وٮحالهِ ى خنبه}}
\par\fontsize{11.1}{13.8}\selectfont
\par\vspace{8.51mm}
\par\vspace{6pt}\nointerlineskip\hbox to\textwidth{\hss\vrule width 115.43pt height.25pt depth0pt\hss}\prevdepth=-1000pt \vspace{5pt}
\noindent\begin{minipage}[t]{.48\textwidth}\vspace{0pt}\fontsize{9}{11.5}\selectfont\setlength{\GutterOffset}{0pt}\setlength{\FullSourceWidth}{\linewidth}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0262-N01}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0262-notes_left-L001}{}{}{\Indent (1) Totum hunc Aristotelis locum praebet quo-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0262-notes_left-L002}{}{}{que \Name{Quṭb ad-dīn Maḥmūd ash-Shīrāzī},}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0262-notes_left-L003}{}{}{\textit{at–tuḥfah ash-shāhiyyah,} f. 158,r.}
\end{minipage}
\hfill\vrule width.3pt\hfill\begin{minipage}[t]{.48\textwidth}\vspace{0pt}\fontsize{9}{11.5}\selectfont\setlength{\GutterOffset}{0pt}\setlength{\FullSourceWidth}{\linewidth}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0262-N02}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0262-notes_right-L001}{}{}{\Indent (2) Cfr. supra, p. 171, lin. 12, atque infra ad}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0262-notes_right-L002}{}{}{textum Arabicum, p. 174 et 175.}
\end{minipage}
\DSource{0263}{174}
\par\fontsize{16.8}{26.48}\selectfont
\par\vspace*{\dimexpr 2.74mm-\baselineskip\relax}\prevdepth=0pt
\DLine{AB01-PDF0263-body-L001}{}{}{\smash{\makebox[\linewidth][s]{\textarabic{منها جزيرة ضَخمة في اقصى البحر مقابل ارض الهند في ناحية المشرق عند بلد الصين تُسمَّى طبروباني\textsuperscript{1}}}}}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0263-body-L002}{}{}{\smash{\makebox[\linewidth][s]{\textarabic{وهي سَرَندِيب يحيط بها ثلثة آلاف ميل فيها جِبال عظيمة وانهار كثيرة ومنهـا يخرج الياقوت الاحمر}}}}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0263-body-L003}{}{}{\smash{\makebox[\linewidth][s]{\textarabic{ولون السماء وحَوْلَ هذه الجزيرة تسع عشرة جزيرة عامرة فيهـا مدائن وقُرَى كثيرة ومن جزائر هـــذا}}}}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0263-body-L004}{}{}{\smash{\makebox[\linewidth][s]{\textarabic{البحر جزائر الزابِج وجزائر الدِّيبَجات وقُمَيْر\textsuperscript{2} ومنها جزيرة كَلَه التي يُجْلَب منها الرَّصاص القَلْعِيّ وجزيرة}}}}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0263-body-L005}{}{}{\smash{\makebox[\linewidth][s]{\textarabic{سَرْبُزَة\textsuperscript{3} التي يُجْلَب منها الكافور واما بحر المغرب فهو الذي يُسمَّى المحيط وتُسَمِّيه اليونانيّون اوقيانوس}}}}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0263-body-L006}{}{}{\smash{\makebox[\linewidth][s]{\textarabic{ويَتَّصِل به بحر الهند ولا يُعْرَف منه إلّا في ناحية المغرب والشمال عند مُحاذاة ارض الروس والصقالبة}}}}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0263-body-L007}{}{}{\smash{\makebox[\linewidth][s]{\textarabic{فيأخذ من اقصى المُنتهَى في الجنوب محاذيًا لارض السودان مارًّا على حدود السوس الأَقْصَى وطنجة}}}}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0263-body-L008}{}{}{\smash{\makebox[\linewidth][s]{\textarabic{وتاهَرْت\textsuperscript{4} ثمّ الاندلس والجلالقة والصقالبة الى البرطانية ثمّ يمتـــدّ من هناك وراء الجبـال\textsuperscript{5} المسلوكة}}}}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0263-body-L009}{}{}{\smash{\makebox[\linewidth][s]{\textarabic{والاراضي غير المسكونة نحو بحر المشرق وهذا البحر اعني اوقيانوس لا يجري فيه السُّفُن وانّمـا يُسْلَك}}}}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0263-body-L010}{}{}{\smash{\makebox[\linewidth][s]{\textarabic{بالقُرْب من ساحله وفيه ستّ جزائر مقابـل ارض الحبشة تُسمَّى جزائر الخالدات وتُسمَّى ايضًا جزائر}}}}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0263-body-L011}{}{}{\smash{\makebox[\linewidth][s]{\textarabic{السُّعَداء وجزيرة اخرى تُسمَّى غديرة\textsuperscript{6} مقابل الاندلس عند خليج يخرج من هذا البحر وعرضه حيث}}}}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0263-body-L012}{}{}{\smash{\makebox[\linewidth][s]{\textarabic{يخرج سبعة اميال وهـــو بين الاندلس وطنجة كان يُعْرَف بمَعْبَرَة هِرَقْلَس\textsuperscript{7} والآن يُعْرَف بالزُّقاق}}}}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0263-body-L013}{}{}{\smash{\makebox[\linewidth][s]{\textarabic{وهذا الخليج ينصبّ في بحر الروم والشام وفيه اعني بحر اوقيانوس من ناحية الشمال جزائر برطانية وهي}}}}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0263-body-L014}{}{}{\smash{\makebox[\linewidth][s]{\textarabic{اثنتا عشرة جزيرة ويخرج منه ايضًا خليج عظيم في شمال الصقالبة يُسمَّى ايضًا بحر وَرَنْك\textsuperscript{8} وهم امّـــة}}}}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0263-body-L015}{}{}{\smash{\makebox[\linewidth][s]{\textarabic{على ساحله ويمتدّ هـــذا الخليج الى ارض بلغـــار المسلمين طوله من المشرق الى المغرب ثلثمائة ميـــل}}}}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0263-body-L016}{}{}{\smash{\makebox[\linewidth][s]{\textarabic{وعرضه مائة ميل وامـــا بحر الروم وافريقية ومصر والشام طوله من عنـــد الخليج الذي يخرج من بحر}}}}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0263-body-L017}{}{}{\smash{\makebox[\linewidth][s]{\textarabic{المغرب الى ناحية المشرق ويمتدّ الى صور وصَيْداء يكون ذلك مقدار خمسة الاف ميل وعرضه في مكان}}}}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0263-body-L018}{}{}{\smash{\makebox[\linewidth][s]{\textarabic{ستّمائة ميل وفي مكان سبعمائة ميل وفي مكان ثمانمائة ميل ويخرج منه خليج الى ناحية الشمال قريب}}}}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0263-body-L019}{}{}{\smash{\makebox[\linewidth][s]{\textarabic{من الرومية يكون طوله خمسمائة ميل يُسمَّى اذريس\textsuperscript{9} ويخرج منه خليج آخَرُ الى ارض تربُونة\textsuperscript{10} يكون}}}}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0263-body-L020}{}{}{\smash{\makebox[\linewidth][s]{\textarabic{طوله مائتَيْ ميل وفي هذا البحر مائة واثنتان وستّون جزيرةً عامرةً منها خمس\textsuperscript{11} جزائر عظام وهي جزيرة}}}}
\par\fontsize{11.1}{13.8}\selectfont
\par\vspace{2.30mm}
\par\noindent\rule[.5ex]{35.7mm}{.8pt}\par
\par\vspace{-0.07mm}
\par\fontsize{9}{14.86}\selectfont
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0263-P01}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0263-body-L021}{}{}{\Indent 1) P \ArabicRun{طروٮاٮى}, L \ArabicRun{طرفاٮى} — 2) P \ArabicRun{ڡمر}, L \ArabicRun{ڡمير} — 3) P \ArabicRun{سريره}, L \ArabicRun{سرٮره} — 4) P \ArabicRun{وماهرت}, L \ArabicRun{وباهرت} —}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0263-body-L022}{}{}{5) Sic codd., sed procul dubio addendum \ArabicRun{غير} — 6) P \ArabicRun{عديره}, L \ArabicRun{عدٮره} — 7) P \ArabicRun{بمغره مرقلس}, L \ArabicRun{بمعره هرقلس}}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0263-body-L023}{}{}{Cfr. quoque p. 175, adn. 11; \Name{Yāqūt}, I, 61, l. 19, \ArabicRun{معبرة هِيرَقْلَس}; \Name{Qāḍī–zādeh}, in commentario ad pri-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0263-body-L024}{}{}{mum caput tertiae sectionis operis \textit{at–tadhkirah,} f. 120,v., \ArabicRun{معره هرقل}; \Name{Quṭb ad–dīn Maḥmūd ash–}}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0263-body-L025}{}{}{\Name{–Shīrāzī}, \textit{at–tuḥfah ash–shāhiyyah,} f. 158,r., \ArabicRun{معبره هرقلس} — 8) P \ArabicRun{ورٮله}, L \ArabicRun{ورٮك} — 9) Codd. \ArabicRun{ادرس} —}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0263-body-L026}{}{}{10) P \ArabicRun{برٮريه}, L \ArabicRun{ٮرٮرٮه} — 11) P \ArabicRun{خمسون}, sed postea \ArabicRun{جزاير}}
\par\fontsize{11.1}{13.8}\selectfont
\DSource{0264}{175}
\par\fontsize{16.8}{26.47}\selectfont
\par\vspace*{\dimexpr 2.74mm-\baselineskip\relax}\prevdepth=0pt
\DLine{AB01-PDF0264-body-L001}{}{}{\smash{\makebox[\linewidth][s]{\textarabic{رودص وقبرس وسامس وسقلية واقريطش وامّا بحر بنطس الارضيّ فإنّه يُسمَّى طرابزند\textsuperscript{1} لأنّها فُرْضة}}}}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0264-body-L002}{}{}{\smash{\makebox[\linewidth][s]{\textarabic{عليه ويمتدّ من لاذقة\textsuperscript{2} الى خَلْفِ قسـطنطينيّة في ارض الروس والصقالبة طوله مقدار الف وثلثمائة ميل}}}}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0264-body-L003}{}{}{\smash{\makebox[\linewidth][s]{\textarabic{وعرضه ثَلثمائة ميل ويدخل فيه النهر الذي يُسمَّي طانيس وهو يجري من ناحية الشمال من البحر الذي}}}}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0264-body-L004}{}{}{\smash{\makebox[\linewidth][s]{\textarabic{يُسمَّى مايطس وهو بحر وَرَنْك وينفجر من بحر بنطس عند سُوَر قسطنطينيّة خليج يجري فيه مائـة\textsuperscript{3}}}}}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0264-body-L005}{}{}{\smash{\makebox[\linewidth][s]{\textarabic{نهر وينصبّ في بحر مصر وعرضه عند قسطنطينيّة قدر ثلثـة اميال وقسطنطينيّة هي على شاطئه الغربيّ}}}}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0264-body-L006}{}{}{\smash{\makebox[\linewidth][s]{\textarabic{ولا يزال يتضايق حتّى يقع في بحر الشام ومصر الذي على جنوبيّهِ بلادُ المغرب وافريقيّة الى الاسكندريّة}}}}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0264-body-L007}{}{}{\smash{\makebox[\linewidth][s]{\textarabic{ومصر وبحِذائه في الشمال ارض الاندلس والروم وفَرَنْجة\textsuperscript{4} الى انطاكِيَة ويليها بلاد الشام وفِلَسْطِين}}}}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0264-body-L008}{}{}{\smash{\makebox[\linewidth][s]{\textarabic{وامّا بحر جُرْجان وهو بحر الباب\textsuperscript{5} طوله من المغرب الى المشرق ثمانمائة ميل وعرضه ستّمائة ميل وفيـه}}}}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0264-body-L009}{}{}{\smash{\makebox[\linewidth][s]{\textarabic{جزيرتان كانتا عامرتَيْن فيما مضى من الزمان ويُعْرَف هـذا البحر ببحر آبسْكُون\textsuperscript{6} لأنّها على فُرْضته ثمّ}}}}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0264-body-L010}{}{}{\smash{\makebox[\linewidth][s]{\textarabic{يمتدّ الى طبرستان والدَّيْلَم وشِرْوان وباب الابواب وناحية اللّان ثمّ الخزر على مَصَبّ نهر إتِل ثمّ ديار}}}}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0264-body-L011}{}{}{\smash{\makebox[\linewidth][s]{\textarabic{الفُرْس الى ان يعود الى آبسكون\textsuperscript{7} ويُسمَّى باسم كلّ بُقْعة حاذاها كان في القديم يُعْرَف ببحر جرجان}}}}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0264-body-L012}{}{}{\smash{\makebox[\linewidth][s]{\textarabic{والآن ببحر آبَسْكُون\textsuperscript{8} وببحر الخزر وليس يتَّصِل ببحر آخَرَ وهذه هي البحور العظام فامّا غيرها بُحَيْرات}}}}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0264-body-L013}{}{}{\smash{\makebox[\linewidth][s]{\textarabic{وبطائح كبُحَيْرة خوارزم وبحيرة طَبَرِيَّـة بأرض الشام وإيسِكّول\textsuperscript{10} بالقرب من بَرْسُخَان\textsuperscript{9} وقـد حكى}}}}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0264-body-L014}{}{}{\smash{\makebox[\linewidth][s]{\textarabic{ارسطاطاليس انّ بحر اوقيانوس محيط الارض بمَنْزِلة إكْليل لها وانّه ينفتح منـه خليج في ناحيـة}}}}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0264-body-L015}{}{}{\smash{\makebox[\linewidth][s]{\textarabic{المغرب من الموضع المسمّى بمَعْبَرَة هِرَقْلَس\textsuperscript{11} فيدخل الى المعمورة مارًّا نحو المشرق وهو بحر مصر والروم}}}}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0264-body-L016}{}{}{\smash{\makebox[\linewidth][s]{\textarabic{وافريقيّة والشام وينفتح منه ايضًا في ناحية المشرق خليج فيدخل الى المعمورة مارًّا نحو المغرب وهــو}}}}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0264-body-L017}{}{}{\smash{\makebox[\linewidth][r]{\textarabic{بحر الصين والهند وفارس والقلزم والبربر فهذا ما عرفناه من امر البحور}}}}
\par\fontsize{11.1}{13.8}\selectfont
\par\vspace{5.76mm}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0264-H18}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0264-body-L018}{}{}{\CenterLine{\textbf{\textit{e}}) — \textit{Pag. 17, l. 33.}}}
\par\vspace{4.37mm}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0264-P01}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0264-body-L019}{}{}{\Indent De hoc \textit{mari Viridi} alii aliter sentierunt. \Name{Reinaud}, l. c., p. CCLXXXV, adn. 2, scribit:}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0264-body-L020}{}{}{« En grec, mer \textgreek{πρασώδης}, du mot \textgreek{πράσον}, signifiant \textit{poireau} »; manifeste igitur id esse \textit{Ocea-}}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0264-body-L021}{}{}{\textit{num Indicum} putabat, cuius pars, littora Africae ab insula Menuthiadis (Zanzibar?) usque}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0264-body-L022}{}{}{ad extremum orientem lambiens, appellatur \textgreek{ἡ Πρασώδης ϑάλασσα} a \Name{Ptolemaeo} (cfr. pag. 18,}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0264-body-L023}{}{}{adn. 2). Sed « rectum iter, mare Viride appellatum » non potest extra mare ipsum al–Qolzum}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0264-body-L024}{}{}{esse; praeterea partem maris al–Qolzum Oceano Indico contiguam auctor postea designat}
\par\vspace{0.27mm}
\par\noindent\rule[.5ex]{37.5mm}{.9pt}\par
\par\vspace{0.42mm}
\par\fontsize{9}{14.9}\selectfont
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0264-P02}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0264-body-L025}{}{}{\Indent 1) P \ArabicRun{طراوند}, L \ArabicRun{طراوٮد} — 2) P \ArabicRun{لادقيه}, L \ArabicRun{لاذقيه} — 3) Sic codd.; sed cfr. p. 172, adn. 2. — 4) P \ArabicRun{فرخه},}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0264-body-L026}{}{}{L \ArabicRun{ڡرحه} — 5) Codd. \ArabicRun{الراب} — 6) P \ArabicRun{السلون}, L \ArabicRun{انسكون} — 7) P \ArabicRun{السكون} — 8) P \ArabicRun{السلون} — 9) Codd. \ArabicRun{واسلول} —}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0264-body-L027}{}{}{10) P \ArabicRun{برسحان}, L \ArabicRun{ٮرشخان} — 11) P \ArabicRun{ٮمعره مرڡلس}, L \ArabicRun{بمعره هرقلس}; cfr. supra p. 174, adn. 7.}
\par\fontsize{11.1}{13.8}\selectfont
\DSource{0265}{176}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0265-body-L001}{}{}{verbis « loco originis », cui, Ptolemaeum sequens, enormem latitudinem 700 milliariorum (1)}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0265-body-L002}{}{}{tribuit.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0265-P01}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0265-body-L003}{}{}{\Indent Locus parallelus apud \Name{Ibn Rosteh}, p. 84, est: « et latitudinem prope eius extremita-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0265-body-L004}{}{}{« tem in occidente, nempe in eius extremo proximo, quod \textit{mare Ultimum} appelletur, 200 »}
\par\vspace{-0.12mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0265-body-L005}{}{}{(\ArabicRun{وعند منتهاه في المغرب اعني طرفه الادنى الذي يسمى بالبحر الاخير مائتا ميل}). Parva litterarum mutatione « eius}
\par\vspace{0.41mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0265-body-L006}{}{}{extremo » (\ArabicRun{طرفه}) respondet « eius recto itinere » (\ArabicRun{طريقه}) quod \Name{al–Battānī} habet; similiter in}
\par\vspace{0.46mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0265-body-L007}{}{}{scriptura Arabica \ArabicRun{الاخير} « Ultimum » haud multum differt a \ArabicRun{الاخضر} « Viride ».}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0265-P02}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0265-body-L008}{}{}{\Indent \Name{Qodāmah}, p. 230, scribit: « et latitudinem prope eius extremitatem in occidente, et}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0265-body-L009}{}{}{« loco contiguo (vel: coniuncto) mari Viridi 200 » (\ArabicRun{وعند منتهاه في المغرب والموضع المتصل بالبحر الاخضر مائتا}}
\par\vspace{0.45mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0265-body-L010}{}{}{\ArabicRun{ميل}). In versione Gallica libri \Name{Qodāmah}, p. 174, adn. 2, \Name{de Goeje} haec explicat: « Reinaud,}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0265-body-L011}{}{}{« Aboulféda, p. 211 (comp. aussi Ibn Khaldoun \textit{Prolég.} I, 96) dit que ce nom appartient à}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0265-body-L012}{}{}{« l’Océan Indien. Mais on l’emploie souvent aussi pour désigner l’Atlantique, et même la Mé-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0265-body-L013}{}{}{« diterranée porte parfois ce nom. On pourrait croire que Kodâma a supposé avec Bâtiny}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0265-body-L014}{}{}{« [sic, pro al–Battānī] que l’Afrique, au sud de l’Abyssinie, tournait à l’ouest et se dirigeait}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0265-body-L015}{}{}{« vers l’océan Atlantique (comp. Reinaud, Introd., p. CCLXXXVI, note 2) (2), mais la com-}
\par\vspace{-0.09mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0265-body-L016}{}{}{« paraison du texte avec celui d’Ibn Rosteh prouve que Kodâma n’a pas compris sa source}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0265-body-L017}{}{}{« et que c’est en réalité à l’Atlantique que le nom de mer Verte appartient ici ».}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0265-P03}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0265-body-L018}{}{}{\Indent Sed quo modo Oceanus Atlanticus potest dici coniunctus vel contiguus mari al–Qolzum,}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0265-body-L019}{}{}{quod nos hodie Rubrum mare vocamus? Nemo est qui non videat sententiam quoque \Name{de}}
\par\vspace{0.09mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0265-body-L020}{}{}{\Name{Goeje} omnino esse reiiciendam.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0265-P04}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0265-body-L021}{}{}{\Indent Consideremus nunc locos similes apud alios scriptores. \Name{Al–Mas‘ūdī}, \textit{Prairies,} I, 237,}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0265-body-L022}{}{}{haec habet: « eius longitudo est 1400 milliariorum; latitudo eius duarum extremitatum, et}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0265-body-L023}{}{}{« est locus omnium proximus in eius latitudine, 200; latitudo demum in medio loco 700, quae}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0265-body-L024}{}{}{« est maxima eius latitudo » (\ArabicRun{طوله الف واربعمائة ميل وعرض طرفيه مائتا ميل وهو اقرب المواضع في عرضه وعرضه}}
\par\vspace{0.48mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0265-body-L025}{}{}{\ArabicRun{في الوسط سبعمائة ميل وهو اكـــــبر العرض فيه}). Verba « eius duarum extremitatum » (\ArabicRun{طرفيه}) sunt parvus}
\par\vspace{0.31mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0265-body-L026}{}{}{scripturae error (3) pro \ArabicRun{طريقه} « eius recti itineris »; verba igitur al–Mas‘ūdī cum al–Battānī}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0265-body-L027}{}{}{concordant. — \Name{Al–Kharaqī}, fol. 74,v. cod P = 57,v. cod. L: « eius finem, versus extremi-}
\par\vspace{0.12mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0265-body-L028}{}{}{« tatem proximam quae \textit{mare Viride} appelletur, 200 milliariorum esse » (\ArabicRun{ومنتهاه الى طرفه (طرف \textlatin{codd.})}}
\par\vspace{0.32mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0265-body-L029}{}{}{\ArabicRun{الادنى الذي يسمّى البحر الاخضر مقدار مائتي ميل}).}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0265-P05}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0265-body-L030}{}{}{\Indent \Name{Quṭb ad–dīn Maḥmūd ash–Shīrāzī}, \textit{at–tuḥfah ash–shāhiyyah,} f. 155,v.: « Ducentae}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0265-body-L031}{}{}{« parasangae [= 600 milliaria] sunt latitudo originis secundi sinus, qui Sinus Ruber appel-}
\par\vspace{0.09mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0265-body-L032}{}{}{« latur, et cuius extremitas Mare Viride dicitur; eius longitudo in septentriones versus est}
\par\vspace{-0.11mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0265-body-L033}{}{}{« 460 parasangarum [= 1380 milliaria]; ubi angustior fit, et est eius finis, 60 parasangas}
\par\vspace{0.16mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0265-body-L034}{}{}{« [= 180 milliaria] complectitur » \ArabicRun{وهي عرض اصل الخليج الثاني ويُسمَّى الخليج الاحمر وطَرَفه [يُعْرَف \textlatin{add.}] بالبحر}}
\par\vspace{0.27mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0265-body-L035}{}{}{\ArabicRun{الاخضر وطوله في الشمال اربعمائة وستّون فرسخًا وحيث يستدقّ وهو منتهاه ستّون فرسخًا}.}
\par\vspace{0.18mm}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0265-P06}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0265-body-L036}{}{}{\Indent \Name{Qāḍī–zādeh}, in commentario ad I caput (\textit{faṣl}) tertiae sectionis (\textit{bāb}) libri \textit{at–tadhkirah,}}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0265-body-L037}{}{}{f. 120,v., ait Sinum Rubrum 200 parasangas [= 600 mill.] prope originem suam (\ArabicRun{عنــد اصله})}
\par\vspace{-0.08mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0265-body-L038}{}{}{complecti; « postea angustior fit eo usque quo finis eius 60 parasangarum [= 180 mill.] eva-}
\par\vspace{-0.03mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0265-body-L039}{}{}{« dit » \ArabicRun{ثمّ انّه يستدقّ الى أن يصير منتهاه ستّين فرسخًا}.}
\par\vspace{0.04mm}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0265-P07}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0265-body-L040}{}{}{\Indent « Proxima extremitas », « proximum rectum iter », cet., omnium fere Arabicorum scripto-}
\par\vspace{-0.07mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0265-body-L041}{}{}{rum non possunt nisi « proximum nobis » explicari; et quoniam tam auctores Arabici quam Syrii}
\par\vspace{0.16mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0265-body-L042}{}{}{(a quibus Arabes notiones illas de maribus acceperunt) in Syria et in Mesopotamia vivebant,}
\par\vspace{0.34mm}
\par\vspace{6pt}\nointerlineskip\hbox to\textwidth{\hss\vrule width 115.43pt height.25pt depth0pt\hss}\prevdepth=-1000pt \vspace{5pt}
\noindent\begin{minipage}[t]{.48\textwidth}\vspace{0pt}\fontsize{9}{11.5}\selectfont\setlength{\GutterOffset}{0pt}\setlength{\FullSourceWidth}{\linewidth}
\par\fontsize{9}{11.88}\selectfont
\par\vspace{0.25mm}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0265-N01}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0265-notes_left-L001}{}{}{\Indent (1) Seu potius 400; cfr. p. 168, adn. 3.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0265-N02}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0265-notes_left-L002}{}{}{\Indent (2) Sed hanc coniecturam \Name{Reinaud} iam vi-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0265-notes_left-L003}{}{}{dimus (adn. \textbf{\textit{d}}, p. 167) omnino falsam esse.}
\end{minipage}
\hfill\vrule width.3pt\hfill\begin{minipage}[t]{.48\textwidth}\vspace{0pt}\fontsize{9}{11.5}\selectfont\setlength{\GutterOffset}{0pt}\setlength{\FullSourceWidth}{\linewidth}

\par\vspace{0.25mm}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0265-N03}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0265-notes_right-L001}{}{}{\Indent (3) Idem error in descriptione maris al-Baṣ-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0265-notes_right-L002}{}{}{rah, cfr. adn. \textbf{\textit{d}}, pag. 168, adn. 4.}
\end{minipage}
